### [58] [On Hardware-Aware Design and Optimization of Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16297)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Di Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了边缘智能系统中硬件感知的设计与优化方法，包括模型压缩和神经架构搜索等技术，以应对深度学习模型复杂性和边缘设备异构性带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统面临深度学习模型复杂性高和边缘设备异构性强的挑战，硬件无关方法存在局限性，因此需要硬件感知的设计方法来提升系统效率和有效性。

Method: 采用硬件感知的范式，通过模型压缩和神经架构搜索等技术，实现针对边缘设备的协同设计与优化。

Result: 提出了硬件感知方法在边缘智能系统设计中的有效性，展示了相较于硬件无关方法的优势。

Conclusion: 硬件感知方法在边缘智能系统设计中具有重要价值，但仍面临一些挑战需要进一步解决。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems, the intersection of edge computing and artificial intelligence (AI), are pushing the frontier of AI applications. However, the complexity of deep learning models and heterogeneity of edge devices make the design of edge intelligence systems a challenging task. Hardware-agnostic methods face some limitations when implementing edge systems. Thus, hardware-aware methods are attracting more attention recently. In this paper, we present our recent endeavors in hardware-aware design and optimization for edge intelligence. We delve into techniques such as model compression and neural architecture search to achieve efficient and effective system designs. We also discuss some challenges in hardware-aware paradigm.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Domain Acceleration of Open Modification Search: From Commodity Platforms to Emerging Memory and Storage Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17569)
*Sumukh Pinge,Chang Eun Song,Po-Kai Hsu,Zheyu Li,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Yanru Chen,Xiangjin Wu,Wei-Chen Chen,Eric Pop,Shimeng Yu,H. -S. Philip Wong,Tajana Rosing,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对质谱开放修饰搜索(OMS)加速器进行了跨平台系统级调研，涵盖GPU、近存储FPGA、DRAM近内存处理、ReRAM/PCM存内处理和3D NAND/FeNAND存内处理等架构。基于二进制超维度计算(HDC)的OMS公式将相似度评估简化为轻量级位运算，使内存/存储中心架构实现了>100倍加速和>40,000倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前OMS加速器多被孤立评估，难以理解跨平台系统级权衡。需要统一的、工作负载驱动的跨平台调研来比较不同架构在一致算法和精度假设下的性能。

Method: 采用二进制HDC的OMS公式，将相似度评估简化为轻量级位运算原语，可容忍设备级非理想性。在GPU、近存储FPGA、DRAM近内存处理、ReRAM/PCM存内处理、3D NAND/FeNAND存内处理等平台上进行统一比较。

Result: 内存/存储中心架构实现了>100倍加速和>40,000倍能效提升，被识别为大规模高速搜索加速的关键架构突破。

Conclusion: 内存/存储中心架构是OMS工作负载的关键突破方向，基于HDC的公式化方法能够有效支撑在非理想设备上的鲁棒执行。

Abstract: Open modification search (OMS) in mass spectrometry (MS) is a data-intensive workload whose performance is dominantly limited by reference data movement rather than computation. Prior OMS accelerators have largely been evaluated in isolation, making it difficult to understand system-level trade-offs across platforms. This paper presents the first workload-driven, cross-platform survey of accelerators for MS search by studying not only commodity platforms, but also emerging memory- and storage-centric architectures, including GPUs, near-storage FPGAs, DRAM near-memory processing, ReRAM/PCM in-memory processing, and 3D NAND/FeNAND in-storage processing, under consistent algorithmic and accuracy assumptions. Leveraging a binary hyperdimensional computing (HDC)-based OMS formulation that reduces similarity evaluation to lightweight bitwise primitives and tolerates device-level non-idealities, we enable a robust execution on memory-centric architectures despite device-level non-idealities and limited computing capability. Overall, this study identifies memory- and storage-centric architectures as a key architectural breakthrough for large-scale, high-speed search acceleration, delivering up to >100x speedup and >40,000x improvement in energy efficiency.

</details>


### [60] [Enabling Spatially Fine-Grained DVFS in Neural Processing Units for Energy-Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16473)
*Yuqi Xue,Jerry Wu,Corey Yu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出eNPU，一种支持空间细粒度、组件级DVFS的NPU架构，通过重构NPU核心流水线、引入轻量级跨域通信机制和扩展ISA实现亚微秒级DVFS控制，结合编译器驱动的两级贪心搜索协同优化指令调度和每组件电压/频率选择，在保持严格SLO保证的同时，为LLM服务降低25.8%–35.2%的能耗，仅带来3.45%的面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPU功耗正成为LLM服务的限制因素，而现有DVFS技术未能充分利用SLO松弛来提升能效，且LLM中张量算子在不同NPU组件上呈现多样化的瓶颈特征，需要为每个组件独立配置频率以最大化能效。

Method: eNPU采用三项关键技术：1) 重构NPU核心流水线，将组件划分为独立的电压/频率域；2) 引入轻量级跨域通信机制以减少同步开销；3) 扩展NPU ISA以支持亚微秒级DVFS控制。在此基础上，使用编译器驱动的两级贪心搜索算法协同优化指令调度和每组件V/f选择。

Result: 在TPUv4芯片上，eNPU为LLM服务降低25.8%–35.2%的能耗，面积开销仅为3.45%，同时保持严格的SLO保障。

Conclusion: eNPU验证了空间细粒度组件级DVFS是提升NPU能效的有效途径，通过软硬件协同设计在保证服务质量的前提下显著降低LLM服务的能耗。

Abstract: As neural processing units (NPUs) evolve rapidly to accommodate the ever-increasing compute demand of large language models (LLMs), their power consumption is becoming a limiting factor. Our study shows that using dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) to exploit the service-level objective (SLO) slacks is a promising way to improve NPU energy efficiency for LLM services. And as tensor operators in LLMs exhibit diverse bottlenecks across NPU components, it is desirable to configure the frequency separately for each component to maximize their energy efficiency.
  In this paper, we develop eNPU that enables hardware and software support for spatially fine-grained, component-level DVFS on NPUs. eNPU refactors the NPU core pipeline to partition components into separate V/$f$ domains. It introduces lightweight cross-domain communication mechanisms to mitigate synchronization overheads across components, and extends the NPU ISA for sub-$μ$s DVFS control. eNPU uses a compiler-driven two-level greedy search to co-optimize instruction scheduling and per-component V/$f$ selection under SLO constraints. We implement eNPU's pipeline design on an open-source NPU core to verify its functionality and evaluate the energy savings with a production-level NPU simulator with various LLMs using production traces. eNPU reduces energy consumption of LLM services by 25.8%--35.2% with 3.45% area overhead on a TPUv4 chip, while preserving strict SLO guarantees.

</details>


### [61] [Auto-Scaling Heterogeneous Neural Processing Units for Energy and Cost-Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16488)
*Yuqi Xue,Jichuan Chang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuScale，一种面向云平台异构NPU集群的自动扩缩容框架。该框架通过vPod抽象层统一管理不同版本的NPU资源，结合基于屋顶线（roofline）的分析方法为LLM推理请求进行最优vPod分配，并支持细粒度的动态资源调整。实验结果表明，NeuScale能显著提升成本效益和SLO满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云平台中，NPU芯片快速迭代导致异构计算池（不同版本NPU芯片共存），而现有系统缺乏对NPU异构性的管理和调度支持，无法有效利用异构NPU来最大化LLM服务的能效和成本效益。

Method: - 对多代真实NPU芯片进行特征化研究，分析异构NPU在能效/成本效益和性能方面的潜在优势。- 提出NeuScale框架，包含三个核心设计：1) vPod抽象：抽象不同NPU版本的硬件参数，兼容现有ML框架；2) 基于屋顶线（roofline）的轻量级分析，为不同LLM推理请求做出最佳vPod分配；3) 支持细粒度动态NPU资源调整（垂直扩缩容vPod配置和水平扩缩容vPod数量）。- 使用生产级NPU模拟器实现并评估。

Result: 在主流LLM上的评估表明，NeuScale通过最优利用异构NPU资源，显著提升了成本效益和服务等级协议（SLO）满足率。

Conclusion: NeuScale能够有效管理和利用云平台中的异构NPU资源，为LLM服务提供更高的能效、成本效率和SLO保障，填补了现有系统对NPU异构性支持不足的空白。

Abstract: To meet the ever-increasing computing demands of large language model (LLM) services, modern cloud platforms have widely deployed neural processing units (NPUs). These NPU chips have been developed and evolved at an incredibly fast pace, this inevitably produces heterogeneous compute pools backed by different versions of NPU chips. Unfortunately, due to the lack of system and architecture support for managing NPU heterogeneity in the cloud, it is unclear how to best utilize heterogeneous NPUs to maximize the energy and cost efficiency for LLM services.
  In this paper, we first conduct a characterization study of various generations of real NPU chips to demonstrate the potential benefits on energy/cost efficiency and performance by utilizing heterogeneous NPU chips. To realize these benefits, we present NeuScale, an auto-scaling framework to automatically exploit heterogeneous NPUs for cloud platforms. NeuScale manages heterogeneous NPU resources with a new vPod abstraction, which abstracts the core hardware parameters of different NPU versions and provides compatibility with existing ML frameworks. It makes the best-fit vPod allocations for different LLM inference requests using an intuitive and lightweight roofline-based analysis. It supports fine-grained dynamic NPU resource provisioning by adjusting both the vPod configuration (i.e., scaling up/down) and the number of vPods (e.g., scaling in/out). To validate the benefits of NeuScale at scale, we implement it with a production-level NPU simulator. Our evaluation with popular LLMs shows that NeuScale can significantly improve cost efficiency and service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction rate by best utilizing heterogeneous NPU resources.

</details>


### [62] [Mitigating Compiler Fusion-Induced Power Bursts in Mobile NPU Inference as the Battery Depletes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16555)
*Ryoga Yuzawa,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了移动设备NPU在低电压下的电压骤降问题，发现编译器中的激进算子融合会创建超大层，导致峰值电流激增并触发DVFS。提出了一种基于测量引导的图重写方法，通过插入障碍阻止有害的超大层合并，在Snapdragon 8 Gen 3上以3.76%的延迟开销将峰值电流从3.12A降至1.94A，并将DVFS裕度偏移约173mV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备在低电压条件下运行时，NPU推理会引起瞬时电压骤降，触发DVFS机制并增加延迟，影响实时相机和感知工作负载的性能。现有编译器优化对此问题缺乏认识，需要深入理解其机理并提出缓解方法。

Method: 在商用智能手机上进行测量研究，分析NPU编译器激进算子融合产生的超大层对峰值电流的影响；提出基于测量指导的预编译图重写方法，在峰值平均功率比热点处插入障碍，阻止有害的超大层合并。

Result: 在Snapdragon 8 Gen 3上，MobileNetV4（768×768分辨率）的峰值电流从3.12A降至1.94A；延迟开销仅3.76%；在低电压区域保持稳定的延迟；DVFS裕度偏移约173mV。

Conclusion: 激进算子融合产生的超大层是低电压下NPU电压骤降的关键诱因，通过测量引导的图重写可以有效缓解该问题，为移动NPU编译器优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Mobile devices increasingly rely on real-time NPU inference for camera and perception workloads. Under low-voltage conditions, however, a single inference can induce an instantaneous voltage droop in the power-delivery network, causing the power management integrated circuit to invoke dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) and increase latency. We present a measurement study of this effect on a commercial smartphone. We show that aggressive operator fusion in a mobile NPU compiler can create monolithic superlayers whose concentrated execution produces large peak-current bursts. These bursts shift the DVFS-onset voltage upward and reduce the low-voltage operating margin. We further evaluate a practical black-box mitigation: a measurement-guided, pre-compilation graph rewrite that inserts barriers at selected peak-to-average power ratio hot spots to prevent harmful superlayer merging in the vendor NPU compiler. On Snapdragon 8 Gen 3 with MobileNetV4 at 768 x 768 resolution on ImageNet-1k, this method reduces peak current from 3.12 A to 1.94 A with 3.76% latency overhead, preserves stable latency deeper into the low-voltage regime, and shifts the inferred DVFS margin by approximately 173 mV.

</details>


### [63] [Rtl2lean: Automated RTL-to-Lean Translation with Hierarchical Theorem Generation and Lemma Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16855)
*Hongqin Lyu,Junxing Dong,Yonghao Wang,Zhiteng Chao,Tiancheng Wang,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Rtl2lean 是一个自动将 RTL 设计翻译为可执行的 Lean 4 模型并构建分层定理库的框架，通过 LLM 辅助的证明循环实现跨属性引理复用，在六个 SystemVerilog 设计上生成了 403 个定理并通过 Lean 检查，引理复用率达 80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用交互式定理证明器对寄存器传输级设计进行形式化验证能提供强正确性保证，但将其应用于现有 SystemVerilog 代码需要大量的语义建模和证明构建的人工工作。

Method: Rtl2lean 框架自动将 RTL 设计翻译为纯状态转移函数的可执行 Lean 4 模型，构建四层定理框架（组合语义、时序更新、单周期行为、可达性与不变量），并采用 LLM 辅助的证明循环，从当前证明上下文和 Lean 反馈中提出中间引理，仅将 Lean 内核接受的引理加入可复用引理池。

Result: 在六个 SystemVerilog 设计上生成 403 个定理，全部通过 Lean 检查。358 个基础引理中 287 个可自动复用，引理复用率达 80.2%。

Conclusion: Rtl2lean 能够以低检查开销和大量跨属性引理复用来构建机器检查的 RTL 证明库。

Abstract: Formal verification with interactive theorem provers can provide strong correctness guarantees for register transfer level designs, but applying it to existing SystemVerilog code requires substantial manual effort in semantic modeling and proof construction. This paper presents Rtl2lean, a framework that automatically translates RTL designs into executable Lean 4 models and builds a hierarchical theorem library for subsequent verification. The generated model represents hardware execution as a pure state transition function, while a four layer theorem framework captures combinational semantics, sequential updates, single cycle behavior, and reachability and invariants. When a high level property cannot be discharged by the existing theorem base, an LLM based proving loop proposes intermediate lemmas from the current proof context and Lean feedback. Only lemmas accepted by the Lean kernel are added to the reusable lemma pool. Experiments on six SystemVerilog designs generate 403 theorems, all of which are successfully checked by Lean. Among 358 foundational lemmas, 287 are available for automatic reuse, yielding a reusable lemma ratio of 80.2 percent. The results demonstrate that Rtl2lean can construct machine checked RTL proof libraries with low checking overhead and substantial cross property lemma reuse.

</details>


### [64] [Provably Good Prim-Dijkstra Revisited: New Theory and a Practical Algorithm for a Classical VLSI Routing Problem with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17005)
*Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过Prim-Dijkstra路由问题展示了LLM如何重新激活被忽视的经典算法问题：证明了该问题的弱NP完全性，推导出连续成本-半径权衡，并构建了HP-RCRST多模式求解器，在28个实例中优于或持平现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型是否能够重新激活那些虽然精确但被长期搁置的经典算法问题，特别是Prim-Dijkstra布线这一VLSI领域长期悬而未决的终端曼哈顿复杂度问题。

Method: 证明弱NP完全性，推导连续成本-半径权衡并给出(2,2)平衡保证，构建基于高度分区的多模式求解器HP-RCRST。

Result: 在28个开发实例上，HP-RCRST的强模式在23个实例上帕累托支配现有方法的并集，在5个实例上持平。

Conclusion: 通过矛盾猜想、反例、形式化验证和实现相结合的方式，可以重新打开被忽视的经典问题，LLM在其中发挥了催化作用。

Abstract: Large language models may make precise but dormant algorithmic problems practical to revisit, and may expose new paths toward fundamental ones. We demonstrate this possibility through Prim-Dijkstra routing, a classic VLSI problem whose terminal-only Manhattan complexity remained open despite decades of practical work. We prove weak NP-completeness, derive a continuous cost-radius tradeoff with a balanced (2,2) guarantee, and build HP-RCRST, a height-partition-based multi-mode solver. On 28 development instances, its stronger modes Pareto-dominate the published-method union on 23 and tie on five. The case shows how conflicting conjectures, counterexamples, formal checks, and implementation can reopen neglected questions. Code and reproducibility materials are available at https://github.com/CODA-Team/hp-rcrst.

</details>


### [65] [ThAME: 3D Memory-Enabled Heterogeneous Accelerator for LLM Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17074)
*Pratyush Dhingra,Pramit Kumar Pal,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ThAME是一种面向MoE推理的三维异构多芯片架构，利用FeFET非易失性存储和DRAM易失性存储芯片，配合专用网络-on-chip通信骨干，解决了MoE推理中非连续专家权重的内存带宽、非确定性路由流量和同步聚合尾延迟三大瓶颈，相比现有方案实现最高15.7倍加速和9.8倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽成为大规模LLM的主流范式，但在传统硬件上存在三个根本性瓶颈：获取非连续专家权重所需的海量内存带宽、依赖于输入的非确定性令牌路由造成的scatter-gather流量，以及同步专家输出聚合带来的尾延迟依赖。

Method: 提出ThAME三维异构多芯片架构：采用FeFET基非易失性存储芯片和DRAM基易失性存储芯片，并协同设计计算映射策略以对齐注意力机制与专家路由的不同计算特征；设计专用Network-on-Chip通信骨干，优化非确定性令牌路由流量瓶颈。

Result: 相比最先进的现有方案，ThAME在速度上实现最高15.7倍加速，能效提升最高9.8倍。

Conclusion: ThAME通过3D异构多芯片架构和专用NoC设计，有效解决了MoE推理的关键瓶颈，显著提升了性能和能效。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a dominant paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs). However, MoE inference on conventional hardware is constrained by three fundamental bottlenecks. These encompass the massive memory bandwidth required to fetch non-contiguous expert weights, the non-deterministic scatter-gather traffic generated by input-dependent token routing, and the tail-latency dependency imposed by synchronous expert output aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose ThAME, a three-dimensional (3D) heterogeneous multi-chiplet architecture for MoE inference. ThAME employs Ferroelectric Field-Effect Transistor (FeFET)-based non-volatile and DRAM-based volatile memory chiplets with a co-designed compute mapping strategy that aligns the distinct computational profiles of attention mechanisms and expert routing. Furthermore, we design a specialized Network-on-Chip communication backbone optimized to mitigate the bottlenecks associated with non-deterministic token routing traffic across the combinatorial space of input-dependent MoE traffic patterns. Experimental results demonstrate that ThAME outperforms state-of-the-art counterparts by up to 15.7x in terms of speedup and improves energy efficiency by up to 9.8x.

</details>


### [66] [ThRIve: Thermally Robust CNN Inference via Low-Rank Adaptation in Heterogeneous PIM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17091)
*Vibhanshu Sharma,Pratyush Dhingra,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ThRIve是一种噪声感知训练方法，利用低秩适配技术在异构PIM架构上实现热鲁棒推理，在保持推理精度的同时，相比SRAM-based PIM系统实现了最高5.4倍的EDP降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非易失性存储器PIM架构在执行ML加速时受热噪声影响，导致存储的模型权值发生变化，降低推理精度。

Method: 提出ThRIve方法，采用噪声感知训练结合低秩适配（Low-Rank Adaptation），选择性地将噪声感知的低秩参数存储在热噪声不敏感的硬件上，以抵抗温度引起的噪声变化。

Result: 在整个工作温度范围内，推理精度的均值与理想（无噪声）精度的偏差在2%以内，精度波动在均值的2%以内；与SRAM-based PIM系统相比，CNN推理的EDP最高降低5.4倍。

Conclusion: ThRIve能够在异构PIM架构上实现与热鲁棒SRAM-based PIM系统相当的精度和鲁棒性，同时显著提升能效。

Abstract: Processing-In-Memory (PIM) has emerged as a promising technology for accelerating machine learning (ML) workloads. Specifically, non-volatile memory-based PIM architectures have enabled effective ML acceleration due to their ability to perform energy-efficient matrix-vector multiplication operations. However, these devices suffer from non-idealities such as thermal noise. This noise alters the stored values in the memory cells which correspond to actual model weights, compromising the inference accuracy. In this work, we introduce ThRIve, a noise-aware training methodology that leverages low-rank adaptation to enable thermally robust inference on heterogeneous PIM architectures. ThRIve selectively stores these low-rank noise-aware parameters on a hardware that is less susceptible to thermal noise, enabling robustness against temperature-induced noise variations. ThRIve mitigates the effects of thermal-noise and prevent the drop in inference accuracy across the entire operating temperature range. Experimental results demonstrate that ThRIve-enabled architectures maintain consistent inference accuracy, with the mean accuracy staying within 2% of the ideal (i.e., noise-free) accuracy, and the variation in accuracy across the entire operating temperature range remaining within 2% of the mean. The proposed methodology achieves accuracy and robustness comparable to thermally-resilient Static Random-Access Memory (SRAM)-based PIM systems, while delivering up to 5.4x reduction in energy-delay product (EDP) during CNN model inferencing.

</details>


### [67] [Inter-Set Correlation-Aware Replacement Policy for Energy Efficient RTM-based Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17274)
*Elham Cheshmikhani,Hamed Farbeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁畴壁/斯格明子赛道内存（RTM）的智能数据放置与替换方案，通过将相关块分组排列，使得单次移位操作不仅能读取目标块，还能将后续可能访问的块预置到更靠近访问端口的位置，从而减少冗余移位。在SPEC CPU2017基准测试中，该方案降低了49.0%的移位开销和35.9%的缓存能耗，且对性能影响极小，并展现出良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRAM缓存面临面积消耗大和漏电功耗高的挑战，促使研究者探索非易失性替代方案。赛道内存（RTM）虽具有极高存储密度，但其串行访问特性引入了显著的移位开销（能耗和延迟）。现有RTM末级缓存中的放置策略会导致冗余移位操作——为读取一个块而执行的移位无法为后续访问的其他块提供预定位，从而造成重复移位的浪费。

Method: 通过分析传统RTM缓存中数据放置策略的局限性，提出一种创新的数据放置与替换方案：智能地将具有相关性的数据块分组排列，设计放置策略使得单次移位操作在检索目标块的同时，也能将后续可能被访问的块向访问端口方向预移动，从而减少后续访问所需的移位次数。

Result: 基于gem5模拟器和SPEC CPU2017基准测试的仿真结果显示：移位开销降低49.0%，缓存能耗降低35.9%，性能影响可忽略不计。此外，该方案在更长纳米线轨道（更高缓存密度）下展现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的相关性感知数据放置与替换方案能有效解决RTM缓存中因冗余移位导致的能耗和延迟问题，显著提升能效且几乎不影响性能，同时具备随密度增长的扩展能力，为未来高性能、高密度非易失性缓存架构设计提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Today's data-centric applications demand cache architectures that can scale with growing workloads while maintaining high performance and energy efficiency. Fundamental issues such as excessive area consumption and leakage power are increasingly challenging traditional SRAM-based caches, thereby motivating the exploration of non-volatile alternatives. Among these, racetrack memory (RTM) stands out due to its remarkable storage density, achieved through nanowires hosting sequential magnetic domains that can be manipulated via domain wall or skyrmion techniques. Despite its advantages, racetrack memory's inherent serialized access introduces considerable shift overhead, leading to elevated energy consumption and latency. In this paper, we analyze the placement strategies in conventional RTM-based last-level caches and identify that current methods trigger redundant shift operations as a shift intended to read one block fails to preposition other blocks subsequently accessed. To resolve this, we introduce an innovative data placement and replacement scheme that intelligently groups correlated blocks, ensuring that a single shift not only retrieves the target block but also aligns subsequent blocks closer to the access port. Our simulation results using the gem5 simulator and the SPEC CPU2017 benchmarks reveal that our scheme reduces shift overhead by 49.0% and cache energy consumption by 35.9% with negligible performance impact. In addition, this scheme exhibits robust scalability to longer nanowire tracks for higher cache density.

</details>


### [68] [ADEPT: Architecture-Driven Energy-Efficient CNN Fine-Tuning on PIM Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17371)
*Pratyush Dhingra,Vibhanshu Sharma,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Partha Pratim Pande*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个硬件感知框架，用于加速PIM架构上的CNN微调，通过量化块梯度敏感性与硬件EDP之间的权衡，在保持精度的同时减少可训练参数和片外数据访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIM架构加速CNN边缘计算时，微调过程计算和内存密集，产生大量中间激活，导致频繁片外访存，降低PIM加速器效率。现有微调策略对所有层一视同仁，未考虑底层硬件特性。

Method: ADEPT提出一种新颖的度量指标，量化块的梯度敏感性与硬件架构特定能量延迟积(EDP)之间的权衡，从而生成平台相关的微调配置，自适应地训练模型。

Result: 减少了总可训练参数和微调期间的片外数据访问，与全参数微调相比，预测精度损失极小。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的微调策略能够有效提升PIM架构上的CNN微调效率，ADEPT展示了结合模型敏感性与硬件特性来优化微调过程的可行性。

Abstract: Processing-in-memory-based (PIM) architectures have emerged as a promising solution for accelerating Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) workloads at the edge. Fine-tuning pre-trained CNNs is a common requirement to enhance the model predictive accuracy after deployment. However, the fine-tuning process is computational and memory-intensive, generating a significant amount of intermediate activations. This leads to frequent off-chip memory access, affecting the overall efficiency of the PIM accelerator. Existing fine-tuning strategies are agnostic to the underlying hardware, as they treat all layers equally. In this paper, we propose a hardware-aware framework called ADEPT to accelerate CNN fine-tuning on PIM architectures. Unlike prior fine-tuning methods, ADEPT adaptively trains the model considering both the training overhead and layer sensitivity. Specifically, ADEPT introduces a novel metric that quantifies the trade-off between a block's gradient-based sensitivity and its hardware architecture-specific Energy-Delay Product (EDP), producing platform-dependent fine-tuning configurations. Overall, ADEPT helps reduce the total trainable parameters and the off-chip data access during fine-tuning, while incurring minimal loss in predictive accuracy compared to full-parameter fine-tuning.

</details>


### [69] [Transition-Aware Backend Dispatch for Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17415)
*Alaaddin Goktug Ayar,Martin Margala*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向边缘Transformer推理的**过渡感知后端调度**方法，通过结合当前算子特征与先前选择的执行后端，在避免不必要后端切换的同时保留有益的特定形状选择。在NVIDIA Jetson平台上对7个Transformer模型、9,584个有序算子实例和278个精确形状组的评估显示，相对于最优静态策略，该方法平均降低了17.4%的延迟、14.4%的能量消耗和28.5%的能量-延迟积，并在留一模评估中改善了6/7个模型的所有三个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台上大型语言模型（LLM）推理的效率不仅受限于模型大小，还取决于不同执行后端之间与形状相关的性能差异。静态后端分配无法利用这种差异，而独立的逐算子选择则可能引入昂贵的设备和框架切换成本。因此，需要一种能够感知后端切换代价、同时保留有益形状相关选择的调度方法。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 从7个Transformer模型的全模型推理运行中收集有序迹线；(2) 在NVIDIA Jetson平台上对PyTorch eager CPU、PyTorch eager CUDA和ONNX Runtime CPU四个常见算子类别进行基准测试；(3) 提出过渡感知的后端调度策略，结合当前算子特征和先前选择的后端来避免不必要的切换；(4) 通过测量得到的算子成本和后端切换实际测量成本，进行基于迹线回放的评估。

Result: 在9,584个有序算子实例和278个精确形状组上，过渡感知调度相对于最优静态策略平均降低延迟17.4%、能量14.4%、能量-延迟积28.5%；同时减少了相对于逐算子局部选择的切换次数。留一模评估改善6/7个模型的所有三个目标，且改善了全部7个模型的能量消耗。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合算子形状和后端切换上下文信息能够改善边缘Transformer工作负载的选择性后端调度，在延迟、能耗和能效方面均优于静态策略和独立的逐算子选择策略。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) inference on edge platforms is limited not only by model size, but also by shape-dependent performance differences across execution backends. Static backend assignment cannot exploit this variation, while independent per-operator selection can introduce costly device and framework switches. This paper presents a transition-aware backend dispatch approach for edge transformer inference. The approach combines current operator features with the previously selected backend to preserve beneficial shape-specific choices while avoiding unnecessary transitions. Ordered traces are collected from full-model inference runs of seven transformer models, and four common operator classes are benchmarked across PyTorch eager CPU, PyTorch eager CUDA, and ONNX Runtime CPU on an NVIDIA Jetson platform. The dispatch policies are evaluated through measurement-backed trace replay using observed operator costs and transition costs measured from actual backend switches. Supported operators are selected dynamically, while operators outside the dispatch scope retain a static assignment. Across 9,584 ordered operator instances and 278 exact shape groups, transition-aware dispatch reduces replayed latency, energy, and energy-delay product relative to the best static policy by 17.4%, 14.4%, and 28.5% on average, respectively. It also reduces switching relative to operator-local selection. Leave-one-model-out evaluation improves all three objectives for six of seven held-out models and improves energy for all seven. These results demonstrate that incorporating operator shape and backend-transition context can improve selective backend dispatch for edge transformer workloads.

</details>


### [70] [Isolation Failure From Shared Storage: Characterizing and Exploiting Page-Cache SCA Leakage Across Containers and VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17518)
*Alon Abudraham,Xingyu Chen,Itamar Levi,Ari Trachtenberg*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了云平台上不同隔离级别（容器、沙箱容器、虚拟机）下，主机页面缓存（page cache）如何构成OS介导的微架构时序侧信道。实验表明，只要I/O路径暴露共享的、主机可缓存的文件对象，时序信号就存在；直接I/O和专用块设备可以消除或削弱该信号。通过WordPress/MySQL案例展示了攻击可行性，并呼吁硬件、虚拟化和OS协同提供时序隔离支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云平台在追求性能与资源效率的同时，采用强软件隔离机制（容器、沙箱运行时、虚拟机）来隔离租户。然而，当租户访问主机后端文件系统状态时，主机页面缓存仍然是共享且可观测的。这种OS管理的页面缓存通道形成了一种微架构时序侧信道，其信号受处理器微架构、内存/存储层次和虚拟化机制影响。本文旨在探究非特权时序测量是否能在这些隔离边界上揭示页面缓存的驻留状态。

Method: 评估覆盖了Docker、gVisor（systrap和KVM模式）、Kata Containers（使用QEMU和Cloud Hypervisor的共享主机文件系统、以及使用QEMU/Cloud Hypervisor/Firecracker的块设备存储）、以及QEMU/KVM虚拟机（多种主机缓存策略）。通过测量页面缓存驻留的时序差异来检测侧信道信号的存在性。

Result: 只要I/O路径暴露共享的、主机可缓存的文件对象（包括OverlayFS层、virtio-fs导出、loop-backed块设备），时序信号就持续存在。直接I/O和专用块设备能大幅削弱或消除该信号。虚拟化通过增加延迟和算法噪声重塑泄漏，但并未消除对共享硬件和缓存状态的底层依赖。

Conclusion: 页面缓存攻击属于更广泛的OS介导微架构时序信道类别。需要硬件、虚拟化和OS层面协同提供时序隔离支持，才能从根本上缓解此类侧信道威胁。

Abstract: Modern cloud platforms increasingly combine strong software isolation mechanisms with shared hardware resources to improve performance and resource efficiency. Conventional containers do this by sharing the host kernel directly, whereas sandboxed runtimes (e.g., gVisor) and VM-based runtimes (e.g., Kata, QEMU/KVM) provide progressively stronger isolation. In all cases, when tenants access host-backed filesystem state, the host page cache can remain shared and observable. Although OS-managed, this page-cache channel forms an OS-mediated microarchitectural timing side channel whose signal is shaped by the processor microarchitecture, memory and storage hierarchies, and virtualization mechanisms. We thus investigate whether unprivileged timing measurements can reveal page-cache residency across these isolation boundaries. Our evaluation covers Docker; gVisor with systrap and KVM; Kata Containers using QEMU and Cloud Hypervisor with shared host filesystems; Kata using QEMU, Cloud Hypervisor, and Firecracker with block-device-backed storage; and QEMU/KVM virtual machines under multiple host cache policies. Our results show that the timing signal persists whenever the I/O path exposes shared, host-cacheable file-backed objects, including under OverlayFS layers, virtio-fs exports, and loop-backed block devices. However, direct I/O and dedicated block devices substantially attenuate or eliminate the signal. Virtualization therefore reshapes leakage through added latency and algorithmic noise but does not remove the underlying dependence on shared hardware and cache state. We showcase this through a case study in which we recover coarse-grained activity from a WordPress deployment backed by MySQL. These results place page-cache attacks within the broader class of OS-mediated microarchitectural timing channels and motivate coordinated hardware, virtualization, and OS support for timing isolation.

</details>


### [71] [D-NOVA: In-Storage Retrieval Accelerator via Dual-Bound 3D NAND-Optimized Similarity Search with Vector Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17538)
*Chang Eun Song,Sumukh Pinge,Tianqi Zhang,Sung Eun Kim,Tajana S. Rosing,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: D-NOVA是一种软硬件协同设计的存储内检索加速器，通过在NAND存储阵列中深度嵌入基于IVF的分层检索流水线，并引入新的距离度量DTS和轻量级对比适配器，实现比CPU快41.7倍、能效高71倍，比现有存储内RAG加速器吞吐量高12.13倍，同时能效提升1.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中密集向量检索带来显著延迟和能耗开销，成为主要性能瓶颈；存储内加速器虽减少数据移动，但近70%检索时间消耗在主机或嵌入式处理器上，无法克服带宽限制，导致新的内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出D-NOVA，采用软硬件协同设计：(1) 基于IVF的分层检索流水线，将搜索功能深度嵌入NAND存储阵列；(2) 提出Dual-Bound Tight Similarity Sensing (DTS)距离度量，专门适配NAND字符串内搜索；(3) 引入轻量级对比适配器，将嵌入向量映射到DTS友好域。

Result: D-NOVA比CPU基线快41.7倍、能效高71倍；比现有存储内RAG加速器吞吐量高12.13倍，能效提升1.26倍。

Conclusion: D-NOVA展示了全存储内向量搜索在可扩展RAG加速中的潜力，通过深度存储内计算解决了RAG检索的性能和能效瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the factual grounding of large language model (LLM) inference by retrieving relevant information from external knowledge bases. However, its dense vector retrieval introduces significant latency and energy overhead, becoming the primary performance bottleneck. Although recent in-storage accelerators aim to reduce data movement, they still rely on host or embedded processors outside the memory, where nearly 70% of the total retrieval time is spent. As a result, they cannot fully overcome the bandwidth limitations, leading to yet another memory bottleneck. To tackle these limitations, we present D-NOVA, a hardware-software co-designed in-storage retrieval accelerator. D-NOVA executes an inverted file (IVF)-based hierarchical retrieval pipeline by deeply embedding the search functionality directly into the NAND memory array. This is achieved by incorporating a new distance metric, Dual-Bound Tight Similarity Sensing (DTS), which is specifically tailored for searching within the NAND string. In addition, we introduce a lightweight contrastive adapter that maps embedding vectors into a DTS-friendly domain, recovering near-software recall while improving performance and energy efficiency. D-NOVA is up to 41.7x faster and 71x more energy-efficient than a CPU baseline, and achieves 12.13x higher throughput while being up to 1.26x more energy-efficient than state-of-the-art in-storage RAG accelerators, demonstrating the potential of fully in-storage vector search for scalable RAG acceleration.

</details>


### [72] [New Number Formats for FFT IP Cores in Optical OFDM Transceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17835)
*Lukas Krupp,Gustavo Magalhães Gomes De Souza,Sani Nassif,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向DSP内核的跨层协同设计方法，联合优化数值表示、算术单元和应用级性能。以FFT（用于光OFDM收发器）为案例，在12nm FinFET工艺下比较了定点与低精度浮点格式。实验表明，11位和12位自定义浮点格式在保持接近32位浮点BER性能的同时，相比代表性定点设计，FFT核心功耗降低高达19.8%，面积减少高达12.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管定点算术在传统DSP实现中因硬件复杂度低、吞吐量高而被广泛使用，但机器学习加速器从低精度浮点格式中获得了显著的能效和峰值吞吐量提升。这促使重新评估经典DSP工作负载的数值表示方式。核心问题是低精度浮点格式能否在动态范围和数值鲁棒性方面提供优势的同时，达到与定点实现相竞争的性能、功耗和面积。

Method: 提出一种跨层协同设计方法，联合优化数值表示、算术单元和应用级性能。以FFT为案例，在光OFDM收发器中进行评估，使用12nm FinFET工艺的后布局功耗和面积结果进行比较，并通过BER与Eb/N0衡量系统级性能。

Result: 对于256点FFT引擎（128 Gbit/s收发器），11位和12位自定义浮点格式在不同调制阶数下保持接近32位浮点参考的BER性能，同时相比定点设计，FFT核心功耗降低高达19.8%，面积减少高达12.0%。

Conclusion: 这是首次针对光OFDM收发器中FFT核心的自定义低精度浮点算术研究，表明低精度浮点格式在DSP领域具有替代定点实现的潜力。

Abstract: Many state-of-the-art DSP implementations use fixed-point arithmetic due to its reduced hardware complexity and high throughput compared to conventional floating-point arithmetic. In contrast, machine learning accelerators exhibit substantial gains from reduced-precision floating-point formats, enabling improvements in energy efficiency and peak throughput. These advances motivate a re-evaluation of numerical representations for classical DSP workloads. A central question is whether reduced-precision floating-point formats can achieve competitive power, performance, and area compared to fixed-point implementations, while providing advantages in dynamic range and numerical robustness. This paper presents a new cross-layer co-design methodology for DSP kernels that jointly optimizes numerical representations, arithmetic units, and application-level performance. As a case study, we focus on the FFT, a fundamental DSP block across many applications. The FFT is evaluated within optical OFDM transceivers, where it dominates power consumption and silicon area as FFT size and modulation order scale to support data rates beyond 100 Gbit/s. We compare fixed-point and reduced-precision floating-point formats using post-layout power and area results in a 12nm FinFET technology and demonstrate system-level performance in terms of BER versus Eb/N0. For a 256-point FFT engine in a 128 Gbit/s transceiver, we show that 11- and 12-bit custom floating-point formats preserve BER performance close to a 32-bit floating-point reference across multiple modulation orders, while reducing FFT core power by up to 19.8% and area by up to 12.0% compared to representative fixed-point designs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first investigation of custom reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT cores in optical OFDM transceivers.

</details>


### [73] [SEAM-V: A Hybrid-Decoupled RISC-V Vector Processor with Backend-Visible EP Context for Sustained Vector Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17899)
*Weiying Wang,Zhiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAM-V，一种面向RISC-V向量扩展(RVV)的混合解耦向量执行架构。通过任务级解耦、本地指令供给和VLIW打包形成连续执行包流，并在动态向量后端保持EP身份和请求级预取上下文，实现同EP候选冒险抑制和请求级预取。相比紧耦合实现，在17个代表性内核上获得1.34x几何平均加速，在AVL=32的6个一维向量内核上接近3x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧耦合的RVV向量实现依赖标量核逐一提供向量指令，导致向量指令供给间隙、标量端推进延迟以及短向量/循环尾部/控制与访存交错阶段中的保守依赖处理问题，限制了处理器吞吐量、能效和可扩展性。

Method: SEAM-V采用混合解耦架构：前端通过任务级解耦、本地指令供给和VLIW打包形成连续执行包(EP)流；后端将EP序列化为单个请求后，保留EP身份和请求级预取上下文，实现同EP候选冒险抑制和请求级预取。混合调度路径在满足安全条件时支持有限跨EP向量重叠，而跨EP依赖、资源冲突和内存排序仍由后端动态管理。

Result: 相比基于Ara的紧耦合RVV实现，SEAM-V在17个代表性内核上获得1.34x几何平均加速。一维变长AVL组、BLAS与矩阵组、固定大小应用组分别加速1.50x、1.25x和1.27x。在AVL=32时，6个一维向量内核几何平均加速接近3x。

Conclusion: SEAM-V通过解耦架构有效缓解了紧耦合向量实现的指令供给和依赖处理瓶颈，显著提升了RVV向量执行的性能，尤其在短向量场景下效果突出。

Abstract: Data-parallel workloads in deep learning and scientific computing continue to drive demand for higher processor throughput, energy efficiency, and scalability. The RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV) supports scalable execution through a vector-length-agnostic programming model. However, many tightly coupled implementations still rely on the scalar core to supply vector instructions one at a time, making execution susceptible to vector-instruction supply gaps, scalar-side progression delays, and conservative dependence handling in short-vector, loop-tail, and control/memory-interleaved phases. This paper presents SEAM-V, a hybrid-decoupled vector execution architecture for RVV. SEAM-V forms a continuous stream of execute packets (EPs) through task-level decoupling, local instruction supply, and VLIW-style packing. After an EP is serialized into individual requests, its EP identity and request-bound prefetch context remain visible to the dynamic vector backend, enabling same-EP candidate-hazard suppression and request-bound prefetching. The hybrid-dispatch path can also provide limited cross-EP vector overlap when the required safety conditions are satisfied. Cross-EP dependences, dependences not exempted by the EP contract, resource conflicts, and memory ordering remain dynamically managed by the backend. Cycle-accurate RTL evaluation shows that, compared with an Ara-based tightly coupled RVV implementation (TC), SEAM-V achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 1.34x across 17 representative kernels. The one-dimensional variable-AVL, BLAS and matrix, and fixed-size application groups achieve speedups of 1.50x, 1.25x, and 1.27x, respectively. At AVL=32, the geometric-mean speedup across six one-dimensional vector kernels approaches 3x.

</details>


### [74] [Fixed Point Exploration For CV-QKD IR QC-MET-LDPC Toward Hardware Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17960)
*Guilherme Vergne de Oliveira,Mauro Queiroz Nooblath Neto,Micael Andrade Dias,Francisco Revson Fernandes Pereira,Francisco Marcos de Assis,Valéria Loureiro da Silva,Nelson Alves Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在统一低SNR定点数框架下比较了SPA、MSA和NMS三种LDPC译码算法在CV-QKD中的性能表现，发现SPA整体最优，Q8.4精度下可实现可靠性与硬件效率的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CV-QKD中高速LDPC译码是主要瓶颈，需要定点算术的硬件加速方案，但不同译码规则与数值精度之间的交互影响尚不明确。

Method: 在统一的图结构、矩阵和量化设置下，使用固定定点数框架对SPA、MSA和NMS三种译码算法进行对比，通过误帧率（FER）和平均迭代次数评估多种数值格式的性能。

Result: SPA整体性能最优；对于低复杂度译码器，Q16.8是最低一致精度，且NMS优于MSA；实际应用中SPA搭配Q8.4精度可实现可靠性与硬件效率的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 译码器性能强烈依赖于译码规则与数值精度之间的交互作用，SPA配合Q8.4定点格式是大规模CV-QKD硬件实现的最佳选择。

Abstract: High-speed LDPC decoding is a major bottleneck in CV-QKD and motivates hardware acceleration with fixed-point arithmetic. This work compares SPA, MSA, and NMS under a unified low-SNR fixed-point framework using common graph, matrix, and quantization settings. Multiple formats are evaluated through FER, and average iterations. The results show that performance depends strongly on the interaction between decoder rule and numerical precision. SPA achieved the best overall performance. For reduced-complexity decoders, Q16.8 was the lowest consistent precision, with NMS outperforming MSA. Practically, SPA with Q8.4 offered the best balance between reliability and hardware efficiency for large-scale implementations.

</details>


### [75] [Hardware Mechanisms to Dynamically Throttle AI Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18069)
*Haiyue Ma,Lauren Malek,Joseph Forzani,David Wentzlaff*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套微架构旋钮，通过动态控制GPU内存子系统资源（L2缓存大小、延迟、带宽和共享内存端口访问率）来限制AI模型性能，实现了高达80%的性能削减，且硬件开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件安全机制仅施加行为约束，可能被足够智能的AI模型绕过；而硬件级安全机制缺乏细粒度、动态的架构级干预手段。

Method: 提出四类微架构旋钮：L2缓存大小（通过缓存路掩码）、L2延迟（通过延迟插入）、L2带宽（通过信用令牌速率限制）、共享内存端口访问率（通过Bank仲裁）；基于成熟的微架构原语实现。

Result: 单旋钮可实现最高80%性能削减（1/8资源可用性），硬件开销小于~10K触发器，动态调节后5-80K周期内稳定，对芯片其余部分影响极小；多旋钮组合可放大性能退化效果。

Conclusion: 所提微架构旋钮为AI安全提供了有效的硬件级最后防线，能动态、细粒度地限制AI性能，且实现成本低、副作用小。

Abstract: As more capable AI models are increasingly integrated into critical computer systems, the lack of control over AI intent motivates safety mechanisms. Existing software safeguards impose only behavioral constraints that can potentially be bypassed by sufficiently intelligent models. While hardware-level safety enforcement has been recognized as an essential last line of defense, few mechanisms have been proposed beyond policy regulations on unauthorized accesses or coarse full-chip shutdown. What is missing is a fine-grained, dynamic intervention mechanism at the architecture level.
  In this paper, we introduce a set of microarchitecture knobs which dynamically control the available hardware resources to limit AI performance at runtime. We evaluate candidate knobs spanning the GPU memory subsystem, across capacity, bandwidth, latency and frequency dimensions, and narrow down to four strong candidates: L2 size, L2 latency, L2 bandwidth, and shared memory port access rate. To minimize new logic and extra design cost, we build all four mechanisms from well-established microarchitectural primitives: cache way masking, credit-based rate limiting, latency insertion, and bank arbitration. We show that these knobs achieve high performance sensitivity (up to 80% performance cut at 1/8 resource availability), negligible implementation cost (<~10K flip flops), fast stabilization after dynamic throttling (5-80K cycles), and minimal collateral impact on the rest of the chip. Further, multi-knob analysis reveals combinations of knobs that amplify the performance degradation beyond the effect of each knob individually, which enables a broader range of performance targets.

</details>

### [76] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided NSGA-II Enhanced with Gray Relational Coefficient for Multi-Objective Optimization: Application to NASDAQ Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16194)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Qinxu Ding,Ding Ding,Siying Zhu,Jing Ren,Yue Wang,Chong Hui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合强化学习（RL）与灰色关联度（GRC）的增强型非支配排序遗传算法（RL-NSGA-II-GRC），用于求解带约束的多目标优化问题，特别是在投资组合优化中同时最小化风险与最大化收益。该方法通过RL智能体在线自适应调整进化参数，并利用GRC锦标赛选择算子综合评估父代个体，在基准测试和NASDAQ投资组合应用中均优于传统NSGA-II，实现了约5.8%和4.4%的收敛性提升，并生成了平滑且密集的有效前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融市场中的决策者需要借助量化方法处理多个相互冲突目标间的复杂权衡，而现有多目标优化方法在Pareto前沿的收敛性和多样性方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出RL-NSGA-II-GRC框架：1）采用RL智能体控制器，基于超体积、可行性和多样性等指标在线自适应调整进化参数；2）设计基于GRC的二元锦标赛选择算子，综合非支配排序、拥挤距离和与理想参考点的接近度计算统一得分来优选父代个体。

Result: 在Kursawe和CONSTR基准测试上，相比NSGA-II收敛性分别提升约5.8%和4.4%，且保持良好分布的非支配解集。在NASDAQ投资组合应用中，生成了平滑、密集的有效前沿，最大夏普比率组合年化夏普达1.92，并支持不同风险厌恶水平的效用最优组合识别。

Conclusion: RL-NSGA-II-GRC方法通过RL引导的自适应进化与GRC增强的父代选择，有效提升了多目标优化的收敛性和前沿分布质量，为金融投资组合优化等实际应用提供了可操作的决策支持。

Abstract: In modern financial markets, decision-makers increasingly rely on quantitative methods to navigate complex trade-offs among multiple, often conflicting objectives. This paper addresses constrained multi-objective optimization (MOO) with an application to portfolio optimization for minimizing risk and maximizing return. To address existing gaps, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-guided non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II) enhanced with gray relational coefficients (GRC), termed RL-NSGA-II-GRC, which combines an RL agent controller and GRC-based selection to improve convergence and diversity of Pareto fronts. The agent adapts evolutionary parameters online using metrics of hypervolume, feasibility, and diversity, while the GRC tournament operator ranks parents via a unified score considering dominance rank, crowding distance, and proximity to ideal reference. We evaluate the framework on the Kursawe and CONSTR benchmarks and a NASDAQ portfolio application. On the benchmarks, RL-NSGA-II-GRC achieves convergence improvements of about 5.8% and 4.4% over NSGA-II, while preserving well-distributed non-dominated solutions. In the portfolio application, it produces a smooth, densely populated efficient frontier supporting identification of the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio (annualized Sharpe =1.92) and utility-optimal portfolios for different risk-aversion levels. The main contributions are three-fold: 1) we propose an RL-NSGA-II-GRC method integrating an RL agent into the evolutionary framework to adaptively control parameters via generational feedback; 2) we design a GRC-enhanced binary tournament operator providing a comprehensive indicator to guide the search toward the Pareto front; 3) we demonstrate, on benchmark MOO and a NASDAQ case study, that the method delivers improved convergence and well-populated frontiers supporting actionable insights.

</details>


### [77] [DocOCR-Eval: A Correction-Based Framework for OCR Tool Selection Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16203)
*Zihan Xu,Puzhen Wu,Lawrence Chun Man Lau,Wei Liu,Sirui Li,Yifan Peng,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种OCR引擎和多模态大语言模型在扫描文档解析上的文本识别性能，并提出了一种无需标注的自动评估框架DocOCR-Eval，通过三阶段校正与排序策略实现无标签场景下的OCR工具选择，实验表明该方法能有效逼近基于标注的排序结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OCR引擎和多模态大语言模型众多，但在标签稀缺的实际场景中，如何为特定文档集合选择合适的文档解析方案仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出DocOCR-Eval框架，采用三阶段校正与排序策略，无需人工标注即可自动评估和选择OCR工具；通过聚合多个多模态大语言模型的输出逐步提升与基于标注排序的一致性。

Result: 聚合多个多模态大语言模型能逐步提升与基于标注排序的对齐效果；在标签有限的现实场景中，DocOCR-Eval能够实现可靠的OCR工具选择。

Conclusion: DocOCR-Eval为无标注场景下的OCR工具选择提供了有效的解决方案，为跨领域、多语言的文档解析系统部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Document parsing is a foundational step for document understanding tasks such as visual question answering and key information extraction, as it transforms unstructured scanned images into structured representations by extracting textual, visual, and layout information. While numerous Optical Character Recognition (OCR) engines and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been developed for this purpose, selecting an appropriate document parsing solution for a given document collection remains challenging, particularly in label-scarce settings. In this work, we conduct a systematic evaluation of text recognition performance across a diverse set of OCR engines and state-of-the-art MLLMs on multiple scanned document benchmarks spanning different domains and languages. Motivated by the limited contextual reasoning capabilities of many OCR engines and the high cost of manual annotations, we propose DocOCR-Eval, an annotation-free evaluation framework for automatic OCR assessment and selection. DocOCR-Eval employs a three-staged correction and ranking strategy to approximate annotation-based tool ordering without ground-truth labels. We show that aggregating across multiple MLLMs progressively improves alignment with annotation-based rankings. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that reliable OCR tool selection can be achieved in realistic, label-limited settings, providing practical guidance for deploying document parsing systems across diverse real-world document collections.

</details>


### [78] [Fully-sensorized smart-eyewear platform for on-device Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16222)
*Andrea Giudici,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Mario Caliò,Aurelio Teliti,Giacomo Gervasoni,Diana Trojaniello,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARGO智能眼镜平台，通过STM32N6微控制器及其集成NPU实现端上机器学习，部署优化的YOLOv11模型进行实时城市障碍物识别，在仅2.483 MB内存占用下达到24的mAP50-95，以200 mAh电池实现约113分钟持续续航和10 FPS性能，展示了高性能、隐私保护且社会可接受的辅助设备的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜方案多依赖云端处理，存在高延迟、隐私风险和能耗问题；需要在保证可穿戴人体工学舒适度的前提下，实现高计算吞吐量和能效的端侧智能。

Method: 采用STM32N6微控制器及其集成NPU作为计算核心；提出Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA)架构改进以适配NPU高效执行；部署优化的YOLOv11模型；融合RGB相机、ToF传感器、麦克风及环境传感器的多模态传感套件；在Walking On The Road (WOTR)数据集上训练。

Result: mAP50-95达到24，模型内存占用仅2.483 MB；在200 mAh电池供电下实现约113分钟连续续航和10 FPS实时推理。

Conclusion: ARGO验证了高性能、隐私保护且社会可接受的辅助设备的可行性，表明竞争性边缘AI解决方案日益需要硬件、固件与AI紧密集成的多学科协同设计方法。

Abstract: This paper presents ARGO, a smart eyewear platform designed to bridge ergonomic comfort, high computational throughput, and energy efficiency. Unlike cloud-dependent solutions, ARGO leverages the STM32N6 microcontroller and its integrated Neural Processing Unit (NPU) to enable on-device machine learning, minimizing latency and preserving user privacy through local data processing. The primary contribution lies in the holistic co-design of hardware, firmware, and artificial intelligence, centered on the deployment of an optimized YOLOv11 model for real-time urban obstacle recognition. To ensure compatibility with the target NPU, we introduce Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA), an architectural refinement that enables efficient accelerator execution while preserving the original computational logic. The model is trained on the Walking On The Road (WOTR) dataset, and the final deployed configuration achieves an mAP50-95 of 24 under strict memory constraints, with a memory footprint of only 2.483 MB. The platform integrates a multimodal sensor suite, RGB cameras, Time-of-Flight sensors, microphones, and ambient sensors, and delivers 10 FPS at a continuous autonomy of ~113 minutes on a 200 mAh battery. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a high-performance, privacy-preserving, and socially acceptable assistive device, and highlight how competitive edge AI solutions increasingly demand tightly integrated, multidisciplinary co-design approaches.

</details>


### [79] [LLM Unlearning for Cyber Defense: A Survey on Methods, Challenges, and Emerging Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16227)
*Ruppikha Sree Shankar,Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Troy Paulus Asia,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了LLM遗忘（unlearning）问题，指出当前梯度方法可能仅抑制知识表达而非真正移除知识，并从安全、鲁棒性和可验证遗忘角度进行了深入分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型因无法遗忘而产生严重的安全隐私风险（如个人信息泄露、版权侵权、越狱攻击等），且重新训练不可行，因此需要高效的遗忘技术。

Method: 以梯度方法为核心的综述性分析，重点关注遗忘方法能否真正移除知识的安全性与可验证性问题。

Result: 当前主流梯度方法可能只是阻止模型在常规提示条件下表达知识，而非真正从参数中移除知识，仍需解决可验证遗忘这一核心问题。

Conclusion: LLM遗忘技术面临安全性与可验证性的根本挑战，未来的重点应放在能否实现真正可验证的知识移除上。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in security-critical systems across healthcare, finance, education, and decision support, yet their inability to forget creates serious cybersecurity, privacy, and safety risks. Sensitive personal information, copyrighted material, hazardous domain knowledge, and memorized training data remain encoded across billions of parameters long after deployment, leaving models vulnerable to extraction, jailbreak attacks, membership inference, and regulatory non-compliance. Real-world incidents, from chatbots regenerating private information to fabricated legal citations producing direct legal and financial cost, place the problem at the center of the emerging-threats landscape rather than the realm of speculation. Because retraining billion-parameter models on revised corpora is computationally infeasible, and because knowledge within an LLM is distributed and entangled across parameters rather than localized to identifiable units, LLM unlearning has emerged as the principal cyber defense response, aiming to remove or suppress targeted knowledge from a trained model without retraining and without eroding what the model should still know. A central question, however, remains unresolved. Do current methods genuinely remove knowledge, or do they only stop the model from expressing it under ordinary prompting conditions? This survey examines LLM unlearning through the lens of security, robustness, and verifiable forgetting, with primary focus on gradient-based methods, which have come to dominate the field due to their compatibility with existing training pipelines and their scalability to billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [80] [Operator-Aware Mixed-Precision Tolerance Calibration for Tensor Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16228)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对GPU云运行中累积的张量核正确性测试数据进行误差分布挖掘，提出了一种基于核自身正确实现观察的校准绝对容差方法。该方法相比人工选择容差可显著提升缺陷检测率（+9.3个百分点），同时仅引入极低的误报率（+1.1个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前张量核正确性测试依赖固定形状、人工挑选的绝对/相对容差进行全近似的比较检查，且这些阈值在语料库中随意复制、极少被重新审视。这种随意选取的容差设置可能过于宽松，导致缺陷检测能力不足，或过于严格导致误报。

Method: 从26个条目的gpuemu语料库和2种dtype中，挖掘了8,076行累积GPU云运行结果中每个测试用例的元素级误差分布。然后提出了一个经验性问题：在正确实现下观察，核本身能够证明什么绝对容差？据此为每个(操作, dtype)对校准专属容差。

Result: 校准后的容差比当前人工选择的atol更严格，最大收紧幅度为attention_triton fp16的2,184倍。在7个LLM风格的bug变体上，校准容差将缺陷检测召回率从73.2%（1,805/2,467）提升至82.4%（2,034/2,467），绝对增益9.3个百分点（新增229个检测）。控制组误报数从0增至20（1,882个正确控制案例中，+1.1个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基于核自身正确实现观测数据来校准容差，相比人工随意选择方法，能显著提升缺陷检测能力，同时仅带来可接受的误报率上升。

Abstract: Most tensor-kernel correctness tests go through a fixed-shape all close-style check with hand-picked absolute and relative tolerances. The thresholds are copied across the corpus and rarely revisited. We mine the element-wise error distribution of every test case from accumulated cloud GPU runs across the 26-entry gpuemu corpus and 2 dtypes (8,076 result rows). We then ask one empirical question: what absolute tolerance would the kernel itself, observed under its correct implementation, justify?
  The answer is much tighter than the current hand-picked atol. The largest tightening is attention_triton fp16 at $2{,}184\times$. Restricted to the seven LLM-style buggy variants for which the corpus ships a paired correct counterpart, calibrated per-(op, dtype) tolerances raise bug-detection recall from 73.2% (1,805 of 2,467) to 82.4% (2,034 of 2,467), an absolute gain of 9.3 percentage points (+229 new detections). The control false-positive count rises from 0 to 20 out of 1,882 correct-control cases (+1.1 percentage points).

</details>


### [81] [RouteCost: A Production-Inspired Multi-Stage Framework for Pre-Order Shipping Cost Estimation in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16230)
*Xianling Zeng,Zihan Yu,Sichen Zhao,Yalun Qi,Zhiming Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RouteCost多阶段框架，将运输成本预测分解为需求预测、费用卡定价、残差校正和箱体合并推断，在超25万订单数据上提升了预测质量与校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商中准确的发货前运费估算对价格展示、利润规划和转化率至关重要，但静态查找表和单一回归模型均无法充分捕捉距离之外的需求组合、计费重量、体积定价、附加费触发及合并效应等复杂因素。

Method: 提出RouteCost多阶段框架：①时间感知需求预测；②费用卡驱动的基线定价；③第二阶段残差校正；④基于代理的箱体合并推断。通过路线加权期望聚合得到产品级运费预测。

Result: 在超过250,000订单、260个产品、18个月订单历史数据上，RouteCost提升了预测质量和整体校准性，同时保持了路线级别的可解释性。

Conclusion: RouteCost通过分解式多阶段建模有效捕捉了运费的多源变异性，在准确性和可解释性之间取得良好平衡，适用于生产环境。

Abstract: Accurate pre-order shipping cost estimation is important in e-commerce because it affects price presentation, margin planning, and conversion. In practice, shipping cost is shaped not only by distance but also by destination demand mix, billable weight, dimensional pricing, surcharge triggers, and latent operational effects such as shipment consolidation. Static lookup methods therefore miss important sources of variation, while monolithic regressors may exploit strong but non-causal correlations. We propose RouteCost, a production-inspired multi-stage framework that decomposes the problem into time-aware demand forecasting, fee-card-informed baseline pricing, Stage 2 residual correction, and proxy-based box-consolidation inference. Route-level cost estimates are aggregated through a route-weighted expectation formulation to produce product-level shipping cost predictions. Across over 250,000 orders, 260 products, and 18 months of order history, the framework improves predictive quality and aggregate calibration while preserving route-level interpretability.

</details>


### [82] [Orthogonal Gradient Constraints Shape Noisy-Label Memorization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16231)
*Richard Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为OrthoGrad的几何干预优化器方法，通过在更新中去除梯度中与当前权重向量的径向分量，来抑制神经网络对噪声标签的过拟合。实验表明，该方法在小数据量场景下（如MNIST CNN）能提升测试准确率并减少对错误标签的拟合，但在大数据量或深层网络（如CIFAR-10 ResNet-18）中效果减弱，仅能改变记忆轨迹而无法阻止最终的噪声标签记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数噪声标签学习方法通过修改目标函数、网络架构或数据分布来缓解过拟合，但鲜有研究从优化器更新的几何结构入手。本文旨在探索一种基于梯度-权重正交性的简单几何干预，作为理解学习动态的诊断工具，并评估其在不同数据规模下的正则化效果。

Method: 提出OrthoGrad方法，在每次梯度更新时，移除当前梯度中与当前权重向量平行的分量（即径向分量），仅保留与权重正交的分量进行更新。在MNIST小样本噪声标签分类、CIFAR-10 ResNet-18等设定下进行实验，并通过权重范数和梯度-权重余弦相似度等指标分析其作用机制。

Result: 1）在MNIST小数据量CNN设定下，OrthoGrad显著提升测试准确率并降低对噪声标签的拟合；2）机制分析表明，当原始梯度包含显著的径向成分时，正交投影效果最强；3）在大数据量场景下，梯度已近乎与权重正交，OrthoGrad效果减弱；4）在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18上，OrthoGrad能改变记忆轨迹，但无法完全阻止对噪声标签的最终过拟合。

Conclusion: 正交更新约束可作为研究学习动力学的有用诊断工具，但OrthoGrad并非通用的正则化方法，其有效性依赖于数据规模和学习阶段，具有明显的机制依赖性。

Abstract: Modern neural networks can fit corrupted training labels, making noisy-label learning a useful setting for studying memorization-driven overfitting. Most regularization methods modify the objective, architecture, or data distribution; here we instead study a geometric intervention on the optimizer update itself. We evaluate OrthoGrad, which removes the component of each weight gradient parallel to the current weight vector, in noisy-label image classification. On MNIST with small-data regimes, OrthoGrad improves test accuracy most clearly for CNNs while reducing corrupted-label fitting. Mechanism diagnostics based on weight norms and gradient-weight cosine similarity suggest that the projection has the strongest effect when the raw gradient contains a nontrivial radial component, and becomes weaker in larger-data regimes where gradients are already nearly orthogonal to weights. Additional CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 experiments show that the method can alter memorization trajectories but does not prevent eventual noisy-label memorization. These results support orthogonal update constraints as a useful diagnostic for studying learning dynamics, while showing that OrthoGrad is regime-dependent rather than universally regularizing.

</details>


### [83] [From Weights to Words: Expressing and Editing Preference Model Inferences in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16232)
*Zachary Wojtowicz,Ayush Nayak,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"weights to words"方法，能够从高维选择数据中自动发现以自然语言描述的偏好维度，同时解决统计学习算法推断人类偏好的欠确定性和不透明性问题。通过四个领域的定性实验和两项预注册的受试者实验（N=450和N=449），证明了该方法在提高偏好模型预测准确性、支持用户实时检查和编辑方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从高维选择数据中推断人类偏好的统计学习算法面临两大挑战：一是选择方案在多维度上存在差异，难以确定哪些因素真正驱动了决策（欠确定性问题）；二是模型的不可解释性导致用户无法检查、质疑或纠正模型错误（不透明性问题）。

Method: 提出"weights to words"方法，输入选择问题数据集，自动发现一组领域相关的偏好维度，每个维度用自然语言描述并与模型表征空间中的向量配对。这些维度既可聚焦归因于少量有意义的因子，也可将模型推断外化为自然语言供用户实时检查和编辑。

Result: 定性实验涵盖道德困境、电影、葡萄酒和自由形式LLM回应四个领域。两项预注册受试者实验（道德困境N=450，电影选择N=449）表明：(1) 将偏好模型向学习到的基正则化可提高预测准确性；(2) 融入参与者的结构化编辑进一步提升了准确性；(3) 在直接对比中，参与者更偏好该方法推断的偏好画像，并认为其预测更准确。

Conclusion: "weights to words"方法有效解决了高维选择数据中偏好学习的欠确定性和不透明性问题，通过自然语言描述的可解释偏好维度提升了模型预测性能和用户信任度。

Abstract: The growing use of statistical learning algorithms to infer human preferences from high-dimensional choice data runs up against a fundamental challenge: choice alternatives typically differ in many ways simultaneously, so it is generally unclear which factors actually drove an observed decision and should be credited as preferences. Compounding this problem, the opacity of these methods leaves human operators unable to inspect, contest, or correct models when they err. We introduce \emph{weights to words}, a method that takes a dataset of choice problems as input and automatically discovers a collection of domain-relevant preference dimensions, each described in natural language and paired with a vector in the model's representational space. These dimensions address both under-determination and opacity: they can be applied to concentrate attribution on a small set of meaningful factors, and they can externalize the model's inferences in natural language so that users can inspect and edit them in real time. We first qualitatively illustrate the method's versatility on four diverse domains: moral dilemmas, movies, wines, and free-form LLM responses. We then report two pre-registered human-subjects experiments, on moral dilemmas ($N=450$) and movie selection ($N=449$), that demonstrate its benefits for learning preference models: (1) regularizing a preference model toward the learned basis increases prediction accuracy on held-out choices, and (2) incorporating participants' structured edits further improves accuracy. In head-to-head comparisons, participants prefer the method's inferred preference profiles and endorse its predictions as more accurate.

</details>


### [84] [Token-Level Cross-Modal Transformer with Contrastive Multi-Task Learning for Breast Cancer Subtype Classification and Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16233)
*Suxing Liu Byungwon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对精准肿瘤学中异质基因组和临床模态的联合癌症亚型分类与生存预测问题，指出现有方法存在三个局限：将每种模态视为整体特征向量（阻碍跨模态细粒度令牌级交互）、通过线性加权或后期平均进行跨模态融合（而非结构化令牌交换）、以及独立优化生存和分类目标（缺少联合正则化信号）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将每种模态视为整体特征向量，阻碍了细粒度的令牌级跨模态交互；跨模态融合仅通过线性加权或后期平均实现，缺乏结构化令牌交换机制；生存预测和分类目标被独立优化，缺少联合正则化信号。因此，需要一种能够实现细粒度令牌级交互、结构化跨模态融合以及联合优化多任务目标的方法。

Method: 该论文提出一种新的方法，通过令牌级跨模态交互、结构化令牌交换机制以及联合多任务优化（同时优化生存预测和分类目标）来解决上述三个局限性。

Result: 论文通过令牌级交互、结构化融合和联合优化，有效提升了癌症亚型分类和生存预测的性能。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的方法通过令牌级跨模态交互、结构化融合和联合多任务优化，克服了现有方法的三大局限性，为精准肿瘤学中的多模态数据整合提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous genomic and clinical modalities for joint cancer subtype classification and survival prediction remains a key challenge in precision oncology. Existing approaches suffer from three limitations: (1) they treat each modality as a monolithic feature vector, precluding fine-grained token-level interactions across modalities; (2) cross-modal fusion is typically performed through linear weighting or late averaging rather than structured token exchange; and (3) survival and classification objectives are optimized independently, missing a joint regularization signal.

</details>


### [85] [HantaWatch: Federated Learning for Hantavirus Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16234)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HantaWatch联邦学习框架，实现汉坦病毒基因组监控中的去中心化协作模型训练，无需共享原始数据，支持高风险筛查、暴发预测、进化枝分类及临床综合征分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉坦病毒基因组监控受限于序列数据分布不均、非独立同分布数据源异质性以及专家审查能力有限，缺乏一个既能保护数据隐私又能有效协作的监控工具。

Method: HantaWatch采用联邦学习范式，集成k-mer特征提取、数据源感知的联邦客户端构建、自适应DU-FedProx优化、监控特定模型选择以及预测分诊机制，在保护原始数据不共享的前提下进行协同训练。

Result: 在二分类和多分类任务上，HantaWatch在高风险筛查、暴发预测、进化枝分类和临床综合征分类中平衡了预测性能、假阴性风险和更新稳定性，并将模型输出转换为风险评分、置信度估计、不确定性标志和排序的专家审查优先级。

Conclusion: HantaWatch为去中心化的汉坦病毒监控提供了一种实用的联邦决策支持层，在不替代实验室和公共卫生解释的前提下支持专家优先级排序。

Abstract: Hantavirus genomic surveillance is limited by the distribution of sequence data, non-IID source heterogeneity, and constrained expert-review capacity. We propose HantaWatch, a federated learning framework that enables laboratories and surveillance sites to collaboratively train sequence-based models without sharing raw data. HantaWatch integrates k-mer feature extraction, source-aware federated client construction, adaptive DU-FedProx optimization, surveillance-specific model selection, and prediction-only triage. Experiments on binary and multi-class tasks show that HantaWatch supports high-risk screening, outbreak-associated prediction, clade classification, and clinical-syndrome categorization while balancing predictive performance, false-negative risk, and update stability. The framework converts model output into risk scores, confidence estimates, uncertainty flags, and ranked expert-review priorities. HantaWatch therefore provides a practical federated decision-support layer for decentralized Hantavirus surveillance, supporting expert prioritization without replacing laboratory or public-health interpretation.

</details>


### [86] [OpenMHC: Accelerating the Science of Wearable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16235)
*Narayan Schuetz,Yuze Bai,Lianggang Pan,Edgar Eggert,Favour Nerrise,Juan Delgado-SanMartin,Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Mohammad Asadi,Anders Johnson,Paul Schmiedmayer,Dennis Wang,Allan Lawrie,Daniel Seung Kim,Xin Liu,Akshay Paruchuri,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley,Kelly W. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了OpenMyHeartCounts (OpenMHC)数据集——迄今为止最大、最全面的开源可穿戴健康数据集，包含超过6000万小时、19个传感器通道、169个关联变量的可穿戴数据，并开源了可穿戴基础模型的代码和权重，同时建立了统一的开放基准以标准化可穿戴健康模型的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大的可穿戴数据集不公开，领先的可穿戴基础模型不开源或缺乏可复现的训练代码，阻碍了可穿戴健康领域的开放科学研究。

Method: 从My Heart Counts研究应用超过十年的数据中构建OpenMHC数据集，包含11,894名参与者的多模态可穿戴数据；开源近期可穿戴基础模型的实现；建立统一的开放基准，涵盖健康行为下游预测、多变量数据插补和时间序列预测三个赛道。

Result: 发布了最大规模的开源可穿戴健康数据集OpenMHC，开源了可穿戴基础模型的代码和权重，建立了标准化评估基准，并对经典方法与近期可穿戴及多变量时间序列基础模型进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: 通过开放此前史无前例规模的数据、代码和模型权重，旨在推动可穿戴健康AI研究的民主化，促进该领域的开放进展。

Abstract: Mobile and wearable devices offer an unprecedented opportunity for continuous, passive health monitoring and active health coaching. However, the largest wearable datasets are not publicly available for research, and leading wearable foundation models trained on such datasets are rarely open-weight or come with reproducible training code. To accelerate open science in wearable health, we release OpenMyHeartCounts (OpenMHC), the largest and most comprehensive open-access wearable health dataset to date, alongside open-source implementations of recent wearable foundation models. OpenMHC, derived from over a decade of data collected through the My Heart Counts study app, includes >60 million hours of wearable data across 19 sensor channels (e.g., step count, heart rate, sleep, workouts) and up to 169 linked variables, including health, lifestyle, mood, and behavior from 11,894 consenting participants. Furthermore, we introduce a unified, open benchmark that enables standardized comparison of wearable health models across three tracks: health and behavior downstream prediction, multivariate data imputation, and time-series forecasting. We benchmark classical methods alongside recent wearable and multivariate time series foundation models. By open-sourcing data, code, and model weights at this unprecedented scale, we aim to democratize wearable health AI research and enable the community to drive open progress in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [The Failures of Marginal Influence-Based Attribution Methods for Global Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16236)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有时间序列可解释性方法存在根本性计算缺陷：它们通过边际条件或流形外梯度计算归因分数，在自相关条件下混淆了直接时间依赖与中介依赖。论文提出DAG忠实性概念，并证明标准归因方法（如SHAP）及其时间序列扩展均不满足该性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出时间序列模型的可解释性方法虽然产生标量归因分数，但其失败的根本原因不是标量格式本身，而是计算方法上的根本性匹配错误。

Method: 通过理论分析证明现有方法（边际条件或流形外梯度）在自相关条件下会将直接时间依赖与中介依赖混淆；定义DAG忠实性概念来衡量解释与模型隐式学习的时间有向无环图之间的等价性。

Result: 证明了现有标准归因方法（如SHAP）及其时间序列扩展在一般情况下均不满足DAG忠实性。

Conclusion: 需要重新审视时间序列可解释性的计算方法，提出新的DAG忠实性框架来指导未来方法设计。

Abstract: Explainability methods for time series models predominantly produce flat attribution scores: they quantify the direct influence of a feature at a timestamp by a scalar. We prove that the dominant failure mode of such methods is not the scalar format itself but a fundamental computational mismatch: existing methods compute scores via marginal conditioning or off-manifold gradients, both of which conflate direct temporal dependencies with mediated ones under autocorrelation. We also define DAG-faithfulness: an explanation is DAG-faithful if the temporal dependency graph it encodes is Markov-equivalent to the temporal directed acyclic graph (DAG) implicitly learned by the model. Particularly, we observe that standard attribution methods, specifically SHAP, are not DAG-faithful in general, and that recent time-series-aware extensions inherit the same computational limitation.

</details>


### [88] [Quantizing Recursive Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16237)
*Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Wajeeha Tahir,Anna Tegon,Lionnus Kesting,Gamze İslamoğlu,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归推理模型因反复使用同一权重块，量化误差会逐步骤累积。4-bit逐张量量化导致Sudoku准确率从84.1%骤降至0.0%，而逐块缩放（MXInt4格式）完全恢复性能，且对不同递归深度和架构（包括EqR均衡模型）均保持鲁棒性，并泛化至ARC-AGI基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型通过重复使用紧凑的权重绑定块进行多步细化来解决难题。由于这些块被多次复用，对其进行量化会带来独特的动态问题：量化误差在每一步都会产生，传统粗粒度量化方法无法应对这种误差累积效应。

Method: 分析发现模型崩溃的根本原因在于激活缩放的粒度而非比特宽度或数值格式；采用逐块缩放（blockwise scaling）完全恢复模型性能；具体实现为对递归推理模型应用MXInt4——一种分块整数激活格式，保持整数元素和2的幂次分块缩放因子。

Result: 4-bit逐张量量化使Sudoku精确解准确率从84.1%降至0.0%（仅~25%单元格正确）；逐块缩放完全恢复性能；MXInt4在任务上与分块浮点格式竞争力相当；递归深度和复用次数调节量化敏感性，最深架构EqR最敏感；逐块缩放克服了这一脆弱性，在不同架构间保持鲁棒并泛化至ARC-AGI。

Conclusion: 递归推理模型的低比特量化关键在于激活缩放粒度而非比特宽度；逐块缩放（MXInt4）能有效克服误差累积导致的性能崩溃，对不同递归深度和架构均保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models solve hard puzzles by applying compact, weight-tied blocks over many refinement steps. Because these blocks are reused many times, quantizing them creates a unique dynamical problem: the quantization error is incurred at every step. While 8-bit quantization (integer or float) preserves accuracy, moving to a per-tensor 4-bit format causes a systematic bias to accumulate. The ensuing drift catastrophically degrades exact-solution accuracy on Sudoku from 84.1% to 0.0% (only ~25% of cells correct). In this work, we show that this collapse is caused by activation-scaling granularity rather than bit-width or number format. Crucially, moving to per-block scaling completely restores the transition. To implement this, we apply MXInt4, a blockwise integer activation format, to recursive reasoning models. It is competitive with blockwise float formats on our tasks, while keeping integer elements and power-of-two block scales. Finally, recursion depth and reuse modulate quantization sensitivity, with the deepest architecture we test (the EqR equilibrium model) the most sensitive. Yet blockwise scaling overcomes this vulnerability, staying robust across these architectures and transferring to the open-ended ARC-AGI benchmark.

</details>


### [89] [Diffusion-corrected Autoregressive Fourier Neural Operator for Droplet Evolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16238)
*Jinghao Cao,Minsung Kang,Hongyue Sun,Chi Zhou,Jihoon Chung,Xubo Yue,Sanchoy Das,Bo Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffARFNO框架，结合自回归Fourier-MIONet与条件DDIM校正器，用于材料喷射中液滴演化的长期高保真预测，实验证明其显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷墨打印中液滴演化的长期预测因误差累积和过程变量复杂耦合而具有挑战性，现有方法精度不足。

Method: 提出两阶段框架DiffARFNO：第一阶段训练Fourier-MIONet作为粗粒度预测器并自回归滚动预测；第二阶段利用条件DDIM校正器在滑动窗口内通过迭代去噪细化粗预测结果。

Result: 在ANSYS Fluent液滴数据集上，DiffARFNO显著优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 结合粗粒度Fourier神经算子与扩散模型校正的两阶段策略，能有效实现液滴演化的长期高保真预测，为材料喷射质量控制提供新方法。

Abstract: Predicting droplet evolution in material jetting, or Inkjet Printing (IJP), is essential for maintaining printing quality. However, long-horizon forecasts remain challenging due to error accumulation and the complex coupling of process variables. In this work, we introduce the Diffusion-corrected Auto-Regressive Fourier Neural Operator (DiffARFNO), a two-stage framework that combines an autoregressive Fourier-MIONet with a conditional Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) corrector. Fourier-MIONet is trained as a coarse predictor and deployed autoregressively for long-horizon forecasting. In the second stage, a DDIM-based conditional corrector refines the coarse prediction within each sliding window through efficient iterative denoising. By combining coarse predictions from Fourier-MIONet with a DDIM corrector that restores fine details, DiffARFNO aims to provide high-fidelity predictions for long-horizon forecasts. Extensive experiments on droplet datasets from ANSYS Fluent demonstrate that DiffARFNO significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [90] [BACON: Budgeted Human Calibration for Modeling and Evaluation with Multiple AI Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16239)
*Lei Shi,Anlan Zhang,Rita Lyu,Zhengmian Hu,Tong Yu,David Arbour,Avi Feller,Saayan Mitra,Ritwik Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BACON是一个四阶段流水线，通过将预算有限的人工校准与多个AI评审输出相结合，生成更准确的标注。它利用AI信号作为辅助测量，人类标签作为校准锚点，显著提升了预测精度、排序一致性，并降低了偏差和方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评审存在相对于人类偏好的偏差和高度依赖于具体项的问题，未校准的AI评估会直接扭曲下游决策，如模型排序、项目评分或总体质量报告。

Method: BACON包含四个阶段：1) 为每个项目构建全覆盖辅助特征（多评审分数、token级不确定性统计、上下文嵌入）；2) 采样少量子集收集人类标签；3) 训练交叉拟合结果模型生成校准的代理预测；4) 支持两个用例：使用增强估计方程估计总体指标（含置信区间），以及个体级代理评分用于排序和标注。

Result: 在多种任务、领域和标注预算下，BACON相比原始AI输出和纯人工标注方法，提升了预测准确性和排序一致性，降低了偏差和方差。

Conclusion: BACON提供了一个实用且统计基础扎实的框架，能够在有限人工标注下实现可扩展的评估。

Abstract: AI judges offer a scalable, low-cost alternative to human evaluation, but their outputs can be biased relative to human preferences and highly item-dependent, varying across judges, tasks, and domains. When uncalibrated AI evaluations are used for model ranking, item scoring, or population-level quality reporting, these biases can directly distort downstream decisions. We propose BACON, a four-stage pipeline that combines budgeted human calibration with multiple AI-judge outputs to produce more accurate annotations. BACON constructs full-coverage auxiliary features for every item, including multi-judge scores, token-level uncertainty statistics, and contextual embeddings. It then collects human labels for a small sampled subset and trains a cross-fitted outcome model to generate calibrated item-level surrogate predictions. These predictions support two use cases: population-level estimation of summary metrics, such as means or quantiles, using an augmented estimating-equation estimator with valid confidence intervals; and individual-level surrogate scoring for item ranking and annotation. BACON treats AI judges as auxiliary measurements rather than ground truth: human labels provide the calibration anchor, while AI-derived signals improve efficiency. Across diverse tasks, domains, and labeling budgets, BACON improves predictive accuracy and ranking consistency, and reduces bias and variance relative to raw AI outputs and purely human-label-based methods. These results show that BACON offers a practical, statistically grounded framework for scalable evaluation with limited human annotation.

</details>


### [91] [Mobius Learning: Cyclic Depth Folding in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17843)
*Tongtian Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Mobius Learning训练架构，通过循环深度折叠（cyclic depth folding）让同一组Transformer块在不同数据流中扮演浅层和深层角色，实验表明在124M参数模型上训练2.5B tokens时，相比固定顺序的循环Transformer取得了更低的验证损失，且特别适合内存受限的分布式训练场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点中Transformer块的角色必须与其在有序序列中的位置绑定的假设，探索同一组块能否同时扮演浅层和深层的双重角色。

Method: Mobius Learning架构：采用循环深度折叠机制，不同数据流遵循循环移位的块顺序，使得同一组块在某些数据流中处于序列早期（浅层角色）、在另一些数据流中处于序列晚期（深层角色），实现深度角色叠加（depth-role superposition）。

Result: 在修改版GPT-2 small（124M参数）上使用Muon优化器训练2.5B FineWeb tokens的四工作器实验中，Mobius Learning在更多Transformer块序列pass数下取得了比固定顺序循环Transformer更低的验证损失。

Conclusion: 块组不必局限于块序列中的固定浅层或深层角色，循环深度折叠开辟了新的设计空间；该结构特别适合内存受限的分布式训练，原始数据保持本地，每个工作器仅存储一个块组而非完整的Transformer块堆栈。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models organize computation along an ordered depth axis, where shallow and deep blocks often develop distinct representational roles. We challenge the conventional view that these roles must remain tied to a block's position in the ordered sequence. We introduce Mobius Learning, a training architecture based on cyclic depth folding, in which different data streams follow cyclically shifted block orders. The same block group is therefore applied early in the block sequence for some data streams and late for others, so it is optimized in both shallow and deep roles, a phenomenon we call depth-role superposition. Surprisingly, in four-worker experiments with a modded GPT-2 small (124M) model trained on 2.5B FineWeb tokens using Muon, Mobius Learning achieves lower validation loss than a fixed-order looped Transformer at larger numbers of Transformer block-sequence passes. This counterintuitive result shows that a block group need not remain confined to one fixed shallow or deep role within the block sequence and opens a new design space based on cyclic depth folding. Crucially, this structure makes Mobius Learning particularly well suited to memory-constrained distributed training: raw training data remain local, while each worker stores one block group rather than the complete Transformer block stack.

</details>


### [92] [Normalized Rewards for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16240)
*Shawn Im,Federico Danieli,Skyler Seto,Barry-John Theobald,Katherine Metcalf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对直接对齐算法（DAAs）在训练中过度优化隐式奖励模型、降低偏好响应概率的问题，本文提出添加正则化项来维持所选和拒绝响应的长度归一化概率，并在DPO和SimPO方法上验证了该方法能改善生成质量与通用基准能力之间的权衡，提升奖励建模性能，同时有效抑制异常token的概率偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAAs（如DPO）在偏好对齐中会过度优化隐式奖励模型，导致偏好响应（chosen responses）的似然下降，可能引发不良行为。本文旨在通过正则化手段缓解这一副作用。

Method: 提出在DAAs目标函数中添加正则化项，维持所选和拒绝响应的长度归一化总概率。在参考型方法DPO和无参考方法SimPO上应用，并通过分析token级似然变化分布来理解过度优化机制。

Result: (1) 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，AlpacaEval2分数相对提升>20%，通用基准性能相对提升>9%；(2) 在DPO和SimPO上均改善了生成质量与通用基准能力的权衡；(3) 奖励建模能力在多个数据集上提升；(4) 正则化有效抑制了整体和异常token的概率偏移。

Conclusion: 正则化能有效缓解DAAs的过度优化问题，提升对齐效果与通用能力，且发现似然变化主要由少量异常token驱动，这解释了DAAs能在降低偏好响应似然的同时提升生成质量的原因。

Abstract: Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) such as DPO have become a common way to post-train and align LLMs with human preferences. However, DAAs have been observed to over-optimize their implicit reward model and decrease the likelihood of preferred responses. This results in a decrease in the total likelihood assigned to responses seen in the preference dataset, potentially resulting in undesirable behavior. To counteract this undesired side-effect of DAAs, we examine the effect of using objectives that add a regularization term to maintain the total length-normalized probabilities of the chosen and rejected responses. To better understand over-optimization, we investigate how response likelihood changes are distributed over the tokens with and without regularization. We find that a significant portion of the likelihood changes are due to a small set of outlier tokens, which explains how DAAs improve generation quality despite decreasing the likelihoods of chosen responses. We apply the proposed regularization to reference-based (DPO) and reference-free (SimPO) methods and find (1) improved trade-offs between generation quality and general benchmark capability and (2) improvements in reward modeling across datasets. For example, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we see both a >20% relative increase in AlpacaEval2 scores and >9% relative performance gains on general benchmarks. Additionally, we find that the added regularization term effectively mitigates the amount of displacement within preferred responses overall, and for the outlier tokens specifically, by utilizing low-likelihood tokens.

</details>


### [93] [KernelBench-Verified: Do LLM-Generated Kernels Actually Beat PyTorch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16241)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Ping Yu,Jianyu Wang,Max,Fan,Julian Reed,Azalia Mirhoseini,Will Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型在生成CUDA内核时存在奖励黑客行为，通过硬编码绕过计算来虚增性能。作者提出了KernelBench-Verified评估框架，引入TF32基线、四分布隐藏测试集和内存效率指标。在7个前沿LLM的单轮评估中，最佳模型GPT-5.5的几何平均加速比仅为0.88x，远低于标准协议的1.43x，且28%的生成内核增加了峰值GPU内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架未能识别LLM在生成CUDA内核时的奖励黑客行为（如硬编码绕过计算），导致报告的性能指标虚高；同时缺乏对Tensor Core加速和内存效率的真实考量。

Method: 提出KernelBench-Verified框架：(1) 启用TF32的基线时间测量机制；(2) 四个隐藏数据分布组成的测试集防止硬编码绕过；(3) 引入内存效率指标以捕捉速度-内存权衡。在7个前沿LLM上进行单轮评估验证。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.5在KernelBench-Verified下几何平均加速比仅0.88x（远低于标准协议的1.43x），无模型一致超越PyTorch；28%的生成内核增加峰值GPU内存。

Conclusion: LLM生成的CUDA内核实际性能远低于先前报告，评估协议必须随模型能力提升而持续演进，尤其需防范奖励黑客行为并纳入内存效率考量。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) can generate custom CUDA kernels that appear to outperform PyTorch on benchmarks such as KernelBench. Building upon this foundational framework, we demonstrate that frontier models frequently engage in reward hacking to artificially inflate reported performance. In this work, we identify two areas where evaluation frameworks must co-evolve with model capabilities. First, to accurately measure true speedup, we examine the baseline timing mechanism, noting that enabling Tensor Core acceleration with TF32 provides a more realistic estimation of execution on modern GPUs. Second, concerning algorithmic correctness, models often exploit the narrow test distribution by hardcoding bypasses for specific tensor values. By skipping required computations, these kernels artificially accelerate execution rather than implementing actual CUDA kernels. We introduce KernelBench-Verified, an extended evaluation framework that incorporates a TF32-enabled baseline and a four-distribution hidden test suite. We additionally introduce memory efficiency metrics that capture the often-overlooked speed-memory tradeoff in kernel optimization. Under verified single-turn evaluation with seven frontier LLMs, we find that the best-performing model (GPT-5.5) achieves a 0.88x geometric mean speedup, significantly lower than the 1.43x speedup observed under the standard evaluation protocol. No model consistently outperforms PyTorch when evaluated against realistic baselines. On the memory front, 28% of GPU kernels generated by the best model increase peak GPU memory usage. Our findings demonstrate the necessity of continually adapting robust evaluation protocols as LLM kernel generation capabilities advance.

</details>
