We aim to utilize ML approaches, incorporating unique feature selection algorithms, to predict PCOS. This paper introduces a data-driven approach to PCOS diagnosis, combining Feature Engineering and ML. Several feature selection approaches have been considered to select sets of features for training the ML model, including CatBoost, Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LGBM), AdaBoost, Random Forest (RF). Results demonstrate that AdaBoost, with ten features selected by RF Feature Importance and Highest Correlation (HC), provides the highest test accuracy.

</details>


### [164] [LLM-as-a-Coach: Experiential Learning for Non-Verifiable Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18110)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Guanheng Chen,He Zhu,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出经验学习（EL），将LLM裁判模型重新用作教练模型，通过传递可迁移的经验知识（而非标量奖励）来指导策略模型，在开放任务上优于传统的基于规则的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL将基于规则的评估压缩为标量奖励，丢失了丰富的文本反馈，且无法区分具有不同质量特征的响应。

Method: 将LLM裁判模型改用作教练模型，对策略生成的每个在线响应进行评估并提炼为可迁移的经验知识，这些知识用于条件化教师模型，并通过在线上下文蒸馏被策略内化。

Result: 在两种策略族上，无论使用策略自身反馈还是专有模型反馈，EL在保留和未见过的开放任务上均优于基于规则的RL，且泛化性更好，能缓解奖励破解问题。

Conclusion: 经验知识作为更丰富、更可泛化的学习信号，适用于非可验证任务的后训练阶段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on open-ended tasks compresses an LLM's rubric-based evaluation into a scalar reward, discarding rich textual feedback and conflating responses with distinct quality profiles. We propose Experiential Learning (EL), which repurposes the feedback model from an LLM-as-a-Judge into an LLM-as-a-Coach. The coach distills its assessment of each on-policy response into transferable experiential knowledge, which conditions a teacher model and is internalized by the policy through on-policy context distillation. Compared with scalar rewards, this higher-bandwidth feedback channel provides dense supervision and preserves fine-grained preferences among high-quality responses. Across two policy families, with feedback from the policy itself or a proprietary model, EL consistently outperforms rubric-based RL on held-out and unseen open-ended tasks. Notably, EL generalizes better beyond the training distribution, and mitigates reward hacking. These findings establish experiential knowledge as a richer and more generalizable learning signal for post-training on non-verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [165] [CADENCE: Closing the Reasoning Gap via Coverage-Adaptive On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16955)
*Satyam Kumar,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CADENCE提出了一种统一的知识蒸馏框架，通过DRIFT机制（逐token前向/反向KL凸混合）及六个扩展组件（COVA、FTB、CCD、LAP、EMR、BSD），分别解决冷启动崩溃、状态无关的散度调度和二元奖励稀疏三个问题。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，将0.5B学生模型从1.5B教师蒸馏至69.8%准确率，缩小了76.2%的师生差距，且仅需单台Apple Mac Studio即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy知识蒸馏存在三个复合失败模式：(i) 冷启动崩溃——新初始化的学生模型对教师偏好token赋予接近零的概率；(ii) 状态无关的散度调度——仅依赖时间步的前向/反向KL插值，忽略学生对当前分布的覆盖状态；(iii) 二元奖励稀疏——仅用pass/fail信号丢弃部分正确轨迹中的有用信息。亟需一种统一框架同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出CADENCE框架，核心为DRIFT机制：在学生采样轨迹上构造逐token的前向KL和反向KL代理目标的凸混合（而非序列级KL梯度估计器）。六个扩展组件：(A) COVA：覆盖自适应的β调度，加速前向到反向的过渡；(B) FTB：分叉token增强，通过全局归一化熵参考在高熵位置集中梯度；(C) CCD：密集奖励，为不正确但接近的轨迹提供数值邻近部分信用；(D) LAP：偏好简洁的正确轨迹强化；(E) EMR：熵匹配校准正则化器；(F) BSD：自举式自蒸馏阶段。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH-500上（512-token校正协议，5个种子，报告标准差），将0.5B学生从1.5B教师蒸馏至69.8±0.5% GSM8K pass@1（预训练基线的48.7%；缩小63.2%师生差距）；使用3B教师时达72.1±0.4%（缩小76.2%），比最强匹配计算量的标签使用基线（DRIFT+二元奖励）高出+4.4±0.7个点。所有实验在单台Apple Mac Studio（M系列，64GB统一内存）上完成。

Conclusion: 原理性知识蒸馏可以在无需数据中心级硬件的前提下达到强推理质量。CADENCE通过针对性的三项修复统一解决了冷启动崩溃、状态无关调度和奖励稀疏问题，验证了逐个失败模式改进的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy knowledge distillation transfers reasoning from large teachers to compact students, but existing approaches suffer three compounding failure modes: (i) cold-start collapse, where a fresh student assigns near-zero mass to teacher-preferred tokens; (ii) state-agnostic divergence scheduling, where time-only forward/reverse-KL interpolation ignores the student's coverage state; and (iii) binary reward sparsity, where pass/fail signals discard information from partially correct traces.
  We present CADENCE, a unified framework with a targeted fix for each. Its DRIFT mechanism schedules a per-token convex mixture of forward-KL and reverse-KL surrogate objectives on student-sampled trajectories (per-token surrogates, not sequence-level KL gradient estimators). Six components extend it: (A) COVA, a coverage-adaptive $β$ schedule accelerating the forward-to-reverse transition; (B) FTB, a forking-token boost concentrating gradient at high-entropy positions via a globally-normalized entropy reference; (C) CCD, a dense reward adding numerical-proximity partial credit for incorrect-but-close traces; (D) LAP, brevity-preferential correct-rollout reinforcement; (E) EMR, an entropy-matching calibration regularizer; (F) BSD, a bootstrapped self-distillation phase.
  On GSM8K and MATH-500 (corrected 512-token protocol, 5 seeds, reported std), CADENCE distills a 0.5B student from a 1.5B teacher to 69.8 $\pm$ 0.5% GSM8K pass@1 (from 48.7% pretrained; 63.2% of the teacher gap closed) and to 72.1 $\pm$ 0.4% with a 3B teacher (76.2% closed), beating the strongest matched-compute label-using baseline (DRIFT+binary reward) by +4.4 $\pm$ 0.7 points. All experiments run on a single Apple Mac Studio (M-series, 64GB unified memory), showing principled distillation reaches strong reasoning quality without datacenter-scale hardware.

</details>


### [166] [Do Language Models Dream of Binding Molecules? Benchmarking LLMs under Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18144)
*Thomas MacDougall,Maksim Kuznetsov,Roman Schutski,Rim Shayakhmetov,Maxim Malkov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用大语言模型（LLM）在基于结构的药物设计（SBDD）中处理3D空间约束的能力，与专用扩散模型进行对比。作者提出了3D-Fit基准测试策略，发现LLM虽落后于最先进方法，但能同时处理多种空间约束（如锚定片段、药效团点和口袋-配体相互作用），展现出在异构场景下的可扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在3D分子生成中占据主导地位，LLM在分子设计领域展现出竞争力，但其在物理推理和3D空间约束理解方面的能力尚未被系统探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，全面评估LLM在复杂3D约束下的表现。

Method: 提出了3D-Fit基准测试策略，用于评估LLM在多条件空间分子生成中的表现。条件包括：蛋白口袋条件、锚定片段、药效团点和强制性口袋-配体相互作用。将通用LLM与专用扩散模型进行系统对比。

Result: LLM在处理3D空间约束方面仍落后于最先进的扩散模型，但展现出有前景的能力：能够同时处理多种空间约束，并支持异构场景下的可扩展应用。

Conclusion: 通用LLM在基于结构的药物设计中具有处理多条件空间约束的潜力，未来有改进空间，但当前在3D分子生成质量上仍不及专用扩散模型。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) leverages the 3D structure of protein targets, often complemented by other spatial constraints, to generate candidate binding molecules. While diffusion models have dominated as a leading paradigm for high-quality 3D molecule generation, LLM-based methods are rapidly emerging in molecular design and have shown competitive performance in pocket-conditioned molecular generation. However, their ability to reason about physics and 3D spatial environments is largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze whether current general-purpose LLMs are capable of navigating complex 3D constraints compared to established baselines such as specialized diffusion models. We consider 3D ligand generation conditioned on protein pockets together with ligand- and interaction-derived spatial constraints, including anchor fragments, pharmacophore points, and mandatory pocket-ligand interactions. To enable this evaluation, we introduce 3D-Fit - a token-efficient benchmarking strategy for assessing LLM performance on multi-conditioned spatial molecule generation. Our findings reveal a clear pattern in LLM spatial capabilities: while they still lag behind state-of-the-art approaches, they are promising and can handle multiple spatial constraints simultaneously, enabling scaling to heterogeneous setups.

</details>


### [167] [SurvCF(t): Counterfactual Explanations for Survival Analysis in Predictive Maintenance Multivariate Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16969)
*Zara Karazian,Panagiotis Papapetrou,Sindri Magnússon,Erik Frisk,Tony Lindgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SurvCF(t)，首个为时序生存模型生成反事实解释的框架，通过优化有效性、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性，找到最小且合理的操作历史修改方案以延长设备寿命，在C-MAPSS、N-CMAPSS和Scania数据集上验证了其可解释性和可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型预测性能强但缺乏可解释性，无法在安全关键场景中提供可操作的洞察，亟需一种能够为时序生存模型生成反事实解释的方法来桥接预测性维护与规范性维护之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将反事实解释构建为约束优化问题，目标函数同时优化有效性（延长预测寿命）、邻近性（改动幅度小）、稀疏性（改动特征少）和合理性（改动符合现实），并保证时序一致性，从而找到对资产运行历史的最小干预方案。

Result: 在C-MAPSS、N-CMAPSS及真实Scania Component_X数据集上验证，SurvCF(t)能够生成可解释、可操作的干预建议，有效延长预测的剩余使用寿命。

Conclusion: SurvCF(t)成功弥合了生存预测与规范性维护之间的差距，为维护策略提供了可解释且面向决策的AI支持。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance relies on accurate Remaining Useful Life estimation, often formulated using survival analysis over multivariate time-series data. While modern deep survival models achieve strong predictive performance, their black-box nature limits their use in safety-critical settings where actionable insight is required. In this work, we introduce \textit{SurvCF(t)}, the first framework for generating counterfactual explanations for survival models operating on time-series data. \textit{SurvCF(t)} identifies minimal, plausible, and temporally consistent changes to an asset's operational history that increase its predicted life time, framing explanation as a constrained optimization problem combining validity, proximity, sparsity, and plausibility. We evaluate the method on multiple benchmarks, including C-MAPSS, N-CMAPSS, and a real-world case study of the Scania Component\_X dataset, demonstrating its ability to produce actionable and interpretable interventions. Our results show that \textit{SurvCF(t)} bridges the gap between survival prediction and prescriptive maintenance, enabling explainable and decision-oriented AI for maintenance strategies.

</details>


### [168] [TurboVec: A Case Study in Cost-Efficient Private Retrieval for Enterprise RAG via Codebook-Oblivious Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16973)
*Navnit Shukla,Kamal Pandey,Omsankar Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TurboVec，一种基于码本无关标量量化器TurboQuant的开放源码向量索引，解决了RAG系统中向量检索层的两个挑战：多租户部署中的语料统计泄露和租户隔离的后过滤召回率下降。在DBpedia基准上，4-bit TurboQuant在相同内存下Recall@5比FAISS PQ高8.5-8.9个百分点；部署在Snowpark上实现11ms中位延迟；内核级白名单过滤在多租户场景下维持0.86-0.93 Recall@10。局限性包括单一数据集评估和仅基于合成数据的隐私评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中向量检索层存在两个未被充分研究的问题：(1) 有监督训练得到的码本量化器在索引构建过程中可能暴露语料统计信息，在多租户部署场景下形成泄露通道；(2) 为租户隔离而进行的后过滤(post-hoc filtering)会降低选择查询的召回率。现有方法如FAISS Product Quantization (PQ)需要训练码本，存在隐私风险。

Method: 提出TurboVec开放源码向量索引，核心组件为TurboQuant——一种码本无关的标量量化器，无需依赖语料库的训练过程。采用内核级白名单过滤(kernel-level allowlist filtering)替代后过滤方式实现租户隔离，避免召回率下降。量化方案为4-bit标量量化，无需像PQ那样学习码本。

Result: 在DBpedia OpenAI嵌入基准(d=1536, 100K-999K向量)上：TurboQuant 4-bit在相同内存预算下Recall@5比FAISS PQ高8.5-8.9个百分点；相比HNSW(R@5=0.991)和IVF-PQ(R@5=0.840)，TurboQuant无需训练且内存仅为HNSW的1/4-1/8；在Snowpark上100K向量中位延迟11ms vs 暴力扫描707ms；内核白名单过滤在10-1000租户下Recall@10为0.86-0.93 vs 后过滤基线0.09-0.19；码本无关设计使成员推理准确率降至随机水平(50.0%) vs PQ的57.3%。

Conclusion: TurboVec/TurboQuant在召回率、延迟、内存效率和隐私保护之间取得了良好的权衡，特别适合多租户RAG场景。码本无关量化有效防止了语料统计泄露，内核级过滤机制解决了后过滤带来的召回率下降问题。但存在局限性：仅在单一数据集上评估、未与压缩后的HNSW对比、隐私评估仅基于合成数据。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly power enterprise LLM applications, yet the vector retrieval layer introduces two underexplored challenges: (1) trained codebook quantizers may expose corpus statistics during index construction, creating a leakage channel in multi-tenant deployments, and (2) post-hoc filtering for tenant isolation degrades recall on selective queries. We study TurboVec, an open-source vector index built on TurboQuant - a codebook-oblivious scalar quantizer requiring no corpus-dependent training. On the DBpedia OpenAI embeddings benchmark (d=1536, 100K-999K vectors), TurboQuant 4-bit outperforms trained FAISS Product Quantization at the same memory budget by 8.5-8.9 percentage points in Recall@5 across all scales. Compared to HNSW (R@5=0.991) and IVF-PQ (R@5=0.840), TurboQuant occupies a distinct design point: higher recall than IVF-PQ without training, at 4-8x less memory than HNSW. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services, TurboVec achieves 11ms median query latency at 100K vectors versus 707ms for warehouse brute-force scan. Kernel-level allowlist filtering maintains 0.86-0.93 Recall@10 across 10-1000 tenant workloads versus 0.09-0.19 for post-filter baselines. Codebook-oblivious design reduces membership inference accuracy to near-random (50.0%) versus 57.3% for PQ codebooks. Limitations include single dataset evaluation, uncompressed HNSW comparison, and privacy evaluation on synthetic data only.

</details>


### [169] [Periodic Bootstrap Thompson Sampling For Periodically Non-Stationary Bandit Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16986)
*Boning Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出周期自助汤普森采样（PBTS），通过信念重置和自助探索阶段解决周期非平稳环境下的多臂赌博机问题，相比传统汤普森采样显著降低累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统汤普森采样在周期非平稳环境中累积所有历史观测，导致后验分布因过时数据而产生偏差，无法适应奖励分布的周期性变化。

Method: PBTS将信念重置与已知或推断的周期对齐，并嵌入结构化的自助探索阶段，从而清除过时数据同时保留不确定性估计。

Result: 在人工构造的周期非平稳环境中（包括偏斜与平衡奖励分布、不同自助比例及错位周期区间），PBTS相较于传统TS在累积遗憾上取得统计显著的降低。

Conclusion: PBTS通过记忆重置与自助阶段为周期奖励环境下的赌博机算法优化提供了新思路，未来研究方向包括自适应周期识别等。

Abstract: This paper introduces Periodic Bootstrap Thompson Sampling (PBTS), an innovative extension of the classic Thompson Sampling (TS) algorithm tailored for bandit problems with periodic non-stationarity. Conventional TS accumulates all past observations, leading to biased posteriors when reward distributions cycle over time. PBTS overcomes this by synchronizing belief resets with known or inferred period intervals and embedding structured bootstrap exploration phases, effectively purging obsolete data while preserving uncertainty estimates. PBTS is tested in artificially constructed environments, which include skewed and balanced reward distributions, along with different bootstrap proportions and misaligned periodic intervals. Results indicate that PBTS generally achieves statistically significant reductions in cumulative regret against traditional TS in periodic non-stationary environments. Subsequent discussion further articulates the potential of PBTS's real-world deployment. The study mentions limitations like extreme periodic misalignment and proposes future research such as self-adjusting cycle-recognition. With memory reset and bootstrap phase, PBTS introduces a novel approach to optimizing bandit algorithms in periodic reward contexts.

</details>


### [170] [Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16999)
*Mingxuan Li,Kaizhan-Lee,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实沙普利信用分配框架（φ-value），通过因果理论中的反事实沙普利值重新分配环境奖励，解决强化学习中技能与运气的归因问题，并在稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励场景下实现更优的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用分配方法（时间邻近性、事后条件奖励重加权）无法正确区分智能体策略（技能）与环境随机性（运气）对观察结果的因果贡献，亟需一种从因果理论出发的原则性框架来隔离真实因果驱动因素。

Method: 基于反事实沙普利值（Counterfactual Shapley Value）重新分配环境奖励，并提出一致的估计器高效计算φ-values；将其与PPO结合得到φ-PPO算法，并引入优先轨迹回放（PTR）机制。

Result: 在稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励的挑战性环境中，φ-values能够与任务奖励的真实因果原因精确对齐，φ-PPO在样本效率上优于现有最先进方法，而基线方法无法收敛。

Conclusion: 反事实沙普利信用分配提供了一种理论严谨的信用归因方法，能够同时提升强化学习在多个困难维度上的性能，且不改变最优策略。

Abstract: The Credit Assignment Problem (CAP) is fundamental to developing efficient and explainable Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents. Existing frameworks, whether relying on temporal contiguity or hindsight-conditioned reward reweighting, frequently fail to attribute properly between an agent's policy (skill) and environmental stochasticity (luck). A principled approach to CAP must isolate the true causal drivers of observed outcomes from spurious correlations and environmental randomness. We introduce Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment, a novel framework grounded in causal theory that attributes credit and blame via the Counterfactual Shapley Value ($φ$-value). By redistributing environmental rewards, $φ$-values enhance temporal credit assignment across three critical dimensions: sparse causality, high stochasticity, and delayed rewards, all while preserving the optimal policy. We derive a consistent estimator that computes $φ$-values efficiently, enabling a new class of policy gradient methods, $φ$-PPO, combined with Prioritized Trajectory Replay (PTR). Empirical results demonstrate that $φ$-values align precisely to the ground truth causes of task rewards with superior sample efficiency in challenging environments where prior state-of-the-art methods fail to converge.

</details>


### [171] [Scalable Causal Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17003)
*Eylam Tagor,Mingxuan Li,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的离策略因果模仿学习算法——Causal SQIL和Causal IQ-Learn，结合因果调整框架与逆强化学习目标，通过高效近似序贯π-后门准则处理连续控制任务中观测不匹配和未观测混杂问题，在长时域任务上显著优于现有因果模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果模仿学习方法（Causal BC和Causal GAIL）仅适用于短时域、低维环境，在长时域、高维状态-动作空间的连续控制任务中表现不佳——Causal BC存在复合误差累积，Causal GAIL不稳定且样本效率低，序贯π-后门调整计算不可行。

Method: 提出Causal SQIL和Causal IQ-Learn两种离策略因果模仿学习算法，将因果调整框架与软Q模仿学习（SQIL）和逆软Q学习（IQ-Learn）目标相结合；利用连续控制环境的因果结构，通过固定大小的滑动窗口高效近似序贯π-后门准则，生成因果调整后的状态表征。

Result: 在混杂环境套件上的评估表明：Causal SQIL和Causal IQ-Learn在长时域任务上显著优于现有CIL算法，部分任务甚至超越专家表现；而所有未考虑因果关系的模仿学习方法均无法学习有效行为。

Conclusion: 将因果调整与离策略逆强化学习目标相结合能有效解决连续控制任务中混杂数据带来的模仿学习挑战，为长时域、高维决策场景提供可行的因果模仿学习方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables learning a policy in an unknown environment with a latent reward signal using expert demonstrations, but it struggles when the imitator's and expert's observations are mismatched and unobserved confounders are present in expert demonstrations. By identifying appropriate adjustment sets via the sequential $π$-backdoor criterion, causal imitation learning (CIL) provides a framework for approximating the expert's policy from confounded data. However, existing CIL methods, Causal Behavioral Cloning (Causal BC) and Causal Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (Causal GAIL), are designed for short-horizon, low-dimensional settings. When applied to continuous control tasks with long horizons and high-dimensional state-action spaces, these methods exhibit poor performance: Causal BC suffers from compounding errors, Causal GAIL is unstable and sample-inefficient, and sequential $π$-backdoor adjustment becomes impractical. We introduce Causal Soft Q Imitation Learning (SQIL) and Causal Inverse soft-Q Learning (IQ-Learn), two off-policy causal imitation learning algorithms that combine the causal adjustment framework with state-of-the-art inverse reinforcement learning objectives. Both algorithms operate on causally-adjusted state representations produced by an efficient approximation of the sequential $π$-backdoor criterion, exploiting the causal structure of continuous control environments to reduce the full-horizon adjustment to a fixed-size sliding window. We evaluate all methods in a suite of confounded environments and find that Causal SQIL and Causal IQ-Learn substantially outperform prior CIL algorithms on long-horizon tasks, sometimes surpassing the expert, whereas all causally unaware imitation methods fail to learn meaningful behavior.

</details>


### [172] [Regularize or Localize: When Training-Time KV-Cache Geometry Pays Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17019)
*Libo Sun,Po-Wei Harn,Zewei Zhang,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究σ-Reg（LeJEPA的抗坍塌目标）能否重塑标准自回归语言模型预训练中的表示，以及这种几何结构如何帮助KV缓存量化。在1.1B参数模型、10B FineWeb tokens上实验发现：(1) λ=0.01时σ-Reg将隐藏状态成对余弦各向异性降低38%，困惑度增加<0.35%；(2) 这种变化不传播到KV缓存，但直接对K和V应用σ-Reg可将缓存各向异性降低94%；(3) 在粗粒度量化方案下直接KV正则化有优势，但在KIVI风格配置下所有模型达到近似的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索σ-Reg（原用于视觉表示的自监督学习目标）能否应用于标准自回归语言模型预训练，以改善表示几何结构并提升KV缓存量化的鲁棒性。

Method: 在1.1B参数模型上使用10B FineWeb tokens进行预训练，在隐藏状态和KV缓存上分别施加σ-Reg正则化；对比不同量化方案（对称无分组量化、每通道缩放、KIVI风格混合配置）下的效果。

Result: (1) σ-Reg降低隐藏状态各向异性38%且几乎不损失困惑度；(2) 隐藏状态正则化不影响KV缓存，直接对K/V正则化可降低缓存各向异性94%；(3) 在粗粒度量化下直接KV正则化有优势（基线DNNL是正则化模型的4.3-7.9倍），但KIVI风格量化下所有模型性能接近。

Conclusion: 训练时对KV缓存的分布正则化有助于粗粒度量化场景，但在包含token局部分组、混合KV缩放和零点补偿的完整量化配置下优势消失。这是首次对标准KV缓存几何结构进行训练时分布正则化并评估其后验缓存量化效果的工作。

Abstract: We study whether \sigreg -- LeJEPA's anti-collapse objective -- can reshape representations during standard autoregressive language-model pretraining, and when the resulting geometry helps \kv-cache quantization. We train 110M-parameter models on 10B FineWeb tokens and report three findings. \textbf{(1)} At $λ{=}0.01$, \sigreg reduces hidden-state pairwise-cosine anisotropy by $38\%$ across three paired seeds. Perplexity increases by less than $0.35\%$ in every pair, with no consistent zero-shot loss. \textbf{(2)} This change does not propagate from hidden states to the \kv cache. Applying \sigreg directly to K and V during continued training, however, reduces mean cache anisotropy by $94\%$ across four checkpoints. A matched continuation without the \kv term leaves cache geometry nearly unchanged, and the frozen-trunk retrofits we tested do not reproduce the effect. \textbf{(3)} Under untransformed symmetric group-free quantization, direct \kv regularization is the only training condition that prefers per-channel scaling in all three seeds, and under that same 3-bit per-channel scheme the baseline incurs $4.3$--$7.9\times$ the directly regularized model's \dnll. Under the full simulated KIVI-style configuration (mixed arrangement, zero-points, grouped scales), however, all models reach near-parity, including when storage overhead is approximately matched. In this 110M regime, the training intervention helps when quantizer scales are coarse; the advantage vanishes under the tested combination of token-local grouping, mixed \kv scaling, and zero-points. To our knowledge this is the first training-time \emph{distributional} regularization of standard \kv-cache geometry evaluated against post-hoc cache quantization.

</details>


### [173] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Air Pollution and Respiratory Health Prediction: A Socioeconomic Subgroup Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17024)
*Maede Azani Hassan Abadi,Shouyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于结构化国家层面周数据，构建可解释机器学习框架预测呼吸系统疾病发病率与空气质量状态。结果表明PM2.5是呼吸系统疾病率的主要预测因子，线性模型表现最优；空气质量分类高度依赖PM2.5信息，移除后性能显著下降；SHAP分析揭示了社会经济与气象变量的替代重要性；亚组分析显示低收入国家PM2.5贡献更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染与气候相关压力因素对呼吸健康的影响日益严重，尤其在环境暴露和医疗能力不平等的背景下，需要可解释的预测模型来理解驱动因素及其在不同社会经济群体中的差异。

Method: 使用国家层面周度结构化数据，构建9种回归模型（预测呼吸系统疾病发病率/10万人）和9种分类模型（预测空气质量状态），采用嵌套交叉验证进行比较，运用SHAP值进行模型解释，并按收入水平和地理区域进行亚组分析。

Result: PM2.5是呼吸系统疾病率的最强预测因子，线性和正则化线性模型回归性能最佳；空气质量分类在包含PM2.5时准确率高，移除后性能大幅下降；无PM2.5时，人均GDP、降水和医疗可及性等社会经济与气象变量重要性上升；不同收入组回归误差相似，但PM2.5对中低收入国家预测贡献更强。

Conclusion: 仅靠模型精度不足以应对气候-健康预测问题，可解释模型能够识别主要污染信号、检验结果对关键污染物变量的依赖性，并揭示预测模式在不同社会经济群体间的差异。

Abstract: Air pollution and climate-related stressors are increasingly important concerns for respiratory health, especially in settings with unequal environmental exposure and healthcare capacity. This study evaluates an interpretable machine learning framework for predicting respiratory disease rates and air-quality status using structured country-level weekly data. Two supervised learning tasks were considered: regression of respiratory disease rate per 100,000 population and binary classification of air-quality status. Nine regression models and nine classification models were compared using nested cross-validation. Model interpretation was conducted using SHAP values, and subgroup analysis was performed across income levels and geographic regions. The results showed that PM2.5 concentration was the dominant predictor of respiratory disease rate, with linear and regularized linear models achieving the strongest regression performance. For air-quality classification, models achieved high balanced accuracy when PM2.5 was included, but performance decreased substantially when PM2.5 was removed, indicating strong dependence on pollutant-related information. SHAP analysis showed that, without PM2.5, socioeconomic and meteorological variables such as GDP per capita, precipitation, and healthcare access became more influential. Subgroup analysis showed similar aggregate regression error across income groups, but PM2.5 contributed more strongly to predictions in lower-middle-income countries. These results show that model accuracy alone is not sufficient for climate-health prediction. Interpretable models can help identify dominant pollution-related signals, test whether results depend on key pollutant variables, and show whether prediction patterns differ across socioeconomic groups.

</details>


### [174] [ChemFusion: A Multimodal Cross-Attention Network for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17033)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChemFusion混合神经网络，通过交叉注意力机制融合电子描述符与3D原子坐标，在过渡金属催化反应预测中显著超越传统单模态方法，并自动学习识别空间位阻效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以有效融合全局电子描述符与反应位点的局部三维几何信息，导致过渡金属催化反应预测存在计算瓶颈。

Method: 构建混合神经网络ChemFusion，利用交叉注意力机制将全局电子状态与未池化的分子点云中的3D原子坐标进行动态对齐与融合。

Result: 在多样化的交叉偶联反应库上取得卓越预测性能，显著超越传统单模态框架；注意力矩阵揭示模型能自主学习识别并惩罚空间位阻障碍。

Conclusion: 空间感知网络能够导航复杂反应中的位阻效应，提供物理可解释性，弥补了标准统计模型的不足。

Abstract: Forecasting the outcomes of transition-metal-catalyzed reactions is notoriously complex due to the interplay of diverse physical and chemical variables. A persistent computational bottleneck has been effectively merging broad electronic descriptors with the localized, three-dimensional geometry of the reactive site. To bridge this representation gap, we present ChemFusion, a hybrid neural network that fuses conventional electronic features with explicit 3D atomic coordinates. Using a cross-attention mechanism, the model enables global electronic states to dynamically attend to specific spatial constraints within un-pooled molecular point clouds. When benchmarked against a diverse library of cross-couplings, this approach delivers exceptional predictive performance, decisively surpassing traditional single-modality frameworks. Importantly, extracting the attention matrices reveals that the architecture autonomously learns to identify and penalize restrictive steric hindrances. This provides a physically grounded interpretability, demonstrating that spatially aware networks can navigate complex reaction sterics that standard statistical models typically miss.

</details>


### [175] [Apeliotes: A Diffusion-Based Modeling Framework for km-scale Multi-Level Atmospheric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17037)
*Evangelia Rafaela Frastali,Achyut Paudel,Maryam Golbazi,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Apeliotes是一个基于生成式扩散模型的高分辨率天气降尺度框架，结合全球再分析数据、预训练全球天气基础模型和区域训练模块，能以较低计算成本生成公里级多变量气象场，在风速、温度等指标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率天气数据在许多地区仍然有限，传统动力降尺度方法计算成本高昂且难以跨位置、变量和预报场景扩展，亟需一种高效、可扩展的机器学习降尺度方案。

Method: 构建Apeliotes框架，整合全球再分析大气数据、预训练全球天气基础模型和区域训练的生成式扩散模型，实现随机生成多个天气变量的高分辨率场并直接输出。

Result: 垂直风廓线预测误差小于3%，10米风速相关性达0.91，2米温度相关性达0.99，NRMSE分别为0.42和0.17。

Conclusion: Apeliotes在公里级天气变量预测及多层级大气场生成上取得了有竞争力的性能，有效克服了传统动力降尺度在计算效率与可扩展性方面的局限。

Abstract: High-resolution atmospheric data are required to resolve mesoscale and localized meteorological structures, however such datasets remain limited in many regions of the world. Existing high-resolution weather products are typically produced through dynamical downscaling, which is computationally expensive and difficult to scale across locations, variables, and forecast scenarios. These limitations motivate machine-learning-based downscaling systems that can generate multiple weather variables stochastically while producing new high-resolution fields directly. In this paper we present Apeliotes, a framework for high-resolution weather forecasting. Built on the global re-analysis atmospheric data, a pre-trained global weather foundation model, and a regionally trained generative diffusion model, Apeliotes not only provides accurate kilometer-scale weather variables, but also multi-level atmospheric fields which are not directly available in the existing global atmospheric data. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Apeliotes achieves highly competitive performance. The model predicts vertical wind profile with less than 3\% error between truth and predicted fields, achieving correlations of 0.91 for 10-m wind speed and 0.99 for 2-m temperature, with NRMSE values of 0.42 and 0.17, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [Solver-Hard Is Not Model-Hard: A Hardness-Controlled Diagnostic for LLM Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17047)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文评估了LLM约束求解器在近匹配子句密度下的实例级迁移能力，发现准确率与求解器难度代理之间呈现错误符号的相关性，并且证明保持重标定会显著降低某一模型的准确率。预注册扩展表明，推理模型的token花费与求解器难度代理之间没有一致关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常在随机SAT相变区域附近进行，混淆了公式密度与求解器硬度，因此需要控制密度变量来单独考察LLM推理器的实际推理能力。

Method: 在匹配的子句密度和最大子句宽度条件下，对比了证明难度差异大的扩展-Tseitin公式和阶梯-Tseitin公式、鸽巢原理公式及密度不匹配的控制组；使用六个求解器的冲突代理指标，并在三个LLM模型（各243个实例）上进行评估。

Result: Glucose求解器的平均冲突代理差异高达51倍；近匹配密度下准确率差距范围为-32到+20个百分点，汇总差距为+1.7个百分点（p=0.74），且正确性与冲突代理呈正相关（r=+0.15，方向错误）。证明保持重标定导致某一模型所有五个聚类准确率平均下降93个百分点，但对另一模型无效。16k预算下，推理模型在证明容易的匹配公式上花费更多，并耗尽了求解器最容易的UNSAT族预算；32k C1差距消失。

Conclusion: LLM推理器存在准确率与求解器难度之间的关联不一致、对模型表面敏感性高、token花费与公式硬度解离等现象，表明其推理能力尚不可靠，且不应仅以准确率或token消耗作为评估标准。

Abstract: LLM constraint reasoners are often evaluated near the random-SAT phase transition, confounding density and solver hardness. We test instance-level transfer while near-matching clause density. At aligned size bins, with near-matched density and matched maximum clause width, we compare proof-hard expander-Tseitin and proof-easy ladder-Tseitin formulas, pigeonhole anchors, and density-mismatched controls. Theory separates their resolution hardness; a solver-specific Glucose mean-conflict proxy differs by up to $51\times$, and five other solvers preserve the direction. Across three included models (243 instances each; a fourth is excluded for abstention), the near-matched-density accuracy gaps range from $-32$ to $+20$ points, with a pooled gap of $+1.7$ points ($p=0.74$) and a wrong-signed correctness-versus-conflict association ($r=+0.15$). A proof-preserving relabeling lowers accuracy in all five clusters for one model (mean $-93$ points) but not another, exposing model-surface sensitivity. In a preregistered extension, provider-reported completion-token spend does not consistently increase with the proxy after accounting for formula length and censoring. At 16k, the reasoning model spends more on proof-easy matched formulas and exhausts its budget on the solver-easiest UNSAT family; the 32k C1 gap is absent. These scoped dissociations concern verdict accuracy and observed token spend, not certificate solving, exact proof length, or allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [177] [What does a Bayes-filtered transformer believe? A predictive Monte Carlo approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17060)
*Afiq Abdillah Effiezal Aswadi,Haotong Ma,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）方法作为贝叶斯滤波变换器（BFT）的可解释性工具，通过在潜在空间而非预测空间近似BFT内隐的先验和后验分布，解决现有方法依赖预测比较的脆弱性问题。实验在三个风格化任务族上验证了该方法能在潜在空间中再现先前报道的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过比较BFT的预测与各种"参考"后验的预测来解释BFT内隐的先验和后验，但不同后验可能共享相同的后验均值预测，导致这种基于预测空间的比较方法具有脆弱性。因此需要一种直接在潜在空间进行解释的方法。

Method: 提出预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）方法：仅利用BFT的下一标记生成能力，近似其隐含的关于潜在任务的先验和后验分布，从而直接在潜在空间回答解释性问题。

Result: 在三个涵盖0-Markov和1-Markov可交换性的风格化任务族上应用PMC，先前在这些设置中报告的现象在潜在空间中仍然可见。

Conclusion: PMC是一种通用的BFT可解释性工具，能够直接在潜在空间揭示BFT内隐的信念分布，避免了预测空间比较的局限。

Abstract: A Bayes-filtered transformer (BFT) is a transformer trained on sequences that are generated in two steps: first a latent task is drawn from a prior, then observations are drawn conditional on that task. Trained under autoregressive log loss, the BFT's next-token prediction, in the idealized limit, is the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution (PPD) induced by that prior and that conditional law. In practice the trained BFT is only an approximation of this ideal PPD, raising an interpretive question: what prior and posterior over the latent task has the trained BFT actually internalized? Existing work answers this question by comparing the trained BFT's predictions against the predictions of various "reference" posteriors, each standing in for a different candidate algorithm or computation the BFT might be implementing. This prediction-space comparison is fragile: different posteriors can share the same posterior-mean predictions. We use predictive Monte Carlo (PMC) as a general interpretability tool for any BFT: using only next-token generation, PMC returns an approximation to the implicit prior and posterior over the latent task, answering the interpretive question directly in latent space. We apply PMC to three stylized task families spanning 0-Markov and 1-Markov exchangeability. The phenomena previously reported in these settings remain visible in latent space. Code is available at https://github.com/afiq-aswadi/bft-pmc

</details>


### [178] [Robust Assamese Speech Recognition through Controlled Fine-Tuning of Whisper Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17164)
*Ganapati Das,Dwipen Laskar,Hasin Afzal Ahmed,Sanjib Kr Kalita,Kshirod Sarmah,Hem Chandra Das,Manjula Kalita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对资源受限环境下阿萨姆语（低资源、形态丰富语言）的Whisper模型微调方法，通过混合精度训练和梯度累积技术在T4 GPU上实现，在Mozilla Common Voice数据集上显著优于零样本基线，词错误率（WER）降至43.17%，相对改进78.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿萨姆语作为形态丰富的低资源语言，缺乏足够的标注语音数据，预训练Whisper模型在该语言上的语音识别任务表现不佳。

Method: 基于Whisper模型进行受控微调，使用Mozilla Common Voice 24.0-Assamese语料库，在资源受限环境下采用硬件感知的优化训练流程，包括混合精度训练和梯度累积技术，在T4 GPU上实施。

Result: 微调模型在WER、CER、MER、WIL上分别达到43.17%、13.18%、43%、64.81%，相对于零样本基线分别实现78.26%、93.10%、57.0%、35.19%的相对改进；BLEU和METEOR得分分别达到30.81和0.5262；幻觉率降低96.70%，实时因子（RTF）改善32.38%。

Conclusion: 所提出的微调Whisper模型在阿萨姆语ASR任务上显著优于零样本基线，证明了在低资源、形态丰富语言场景下进行受控微调的有效性。

Abstract: Developing Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) for morphologically rich, low-resource languages such as Assamese is challenging due to insufficient annotated speech data. The pretrained Whisper model performs poorly on Assamese speech recognition tasks. This paper presents a controlled, fine-tuned Whisper-based Assamese ASR system trained on the Mozilla Common Voice 24.0-Assamese corpus. A hardware-aware optimized training pipeline is implemented for resource-constrained environments, employing mixed-precision training and gradient accumulation on Tesla 4 Graphics Processing Units (T4 GPUs). The proposed fine-tuned model significantly outperformed the Zero-shot baseline, yielding Word Error Rate (WER), Character Error Rate (CER), Match Error Rate (MER), and Word Infomation Loss (WIL) of 43.17\%, 13.18\%, 43\%, and 64.81\%, respectively, achieving significant relative improvements of 78.26\%, 93.10\%, 57.0\%, and 35.19\% over the baseline. Semantic evaluation of the fine-tuned model also demonstrates notable improvement over a zero baseline, attaining Bilingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and Metric for Evaluation of Translation with Explicit ORdering (METEOR) scores of 30.81 and 0.5262, respectively. Additionally, the predicted hallucination rate and Real-Time Factor (RTF) are substantially improved by 96.70\% and 32.38\%, compared to the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [179] [Explaining and Tuning Transformer-based LLMs in Arithmetic Tasks with Human Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17166)
*Luyu Qiu,Jianing Li,Hwanhee Kim,Xiaoyong Wei,Yueyuan Zheng,Janet Hsiao,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer大模型在整数算术任务上表现出与人类学习者相似的认知模式，即简单子任务学习更快、复杂子任务学习更慢，并成功将人类有效的问题解决策略和认知增强方法应用于提升LLM的算术性能，通过可解释AI验证了这种相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在基础算术任务上表现不佳，这引发了对模型可靠性、安全性和伦理部署的担忧。作者希望探索是否能借鉴人类学习方法来改进LLM的算术能力，并验证两者间是否存在认知过程的相似性。

Method: 1) 将算术任务分解为明确定义的子任务；2) 对每个子任务进行损失收敛顺序分析与消融研究；3) 应用对人类学习者有效的问题解决策略和认知增强方法；4) 通过准确性实验、可视化验证和基于解释的分析进行效果评估。

Result: LLM表现出与人类学习者相似的学习模式：简单子任务学习速度更快，复杂子任务学习速度更慢。应用人类有效的问题解决策略和认知增强方法显著提升了LLM在算术任务上的准确性。

Conclusion: Transformer-based LLM可能与人类学习者在算术学习中共享认知过程，这一发现通过可解释AI验证得到了支持，有助于建立对LLM在高风险关键应用中的信任。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) continue to achieve state-of-the-art performance across various natural language processing tasks. However, their subpar performance on seemingly elementary problems, such as basic arithmetic, raises concerns about model reliability, safety, and ethical deployment. In this study, we demonstrate that the performance of a vanilla Transformer model trained on integer arithmetic tasks can be improved using methods effective for human learners. We begin by decomposing the arithmetic task into well-defined subtasks and conducting loss convergence order analysis together with ablation studies for each subtask. Our findings reveal that LLMs exhibit learning patterns similar to those of human learners, with a faster learning speed for simpler subtasks compared to more complex ones. In addition, we successfully improved the accuracy of LLMs by applying problem-solving strategies and cognitive empowerment methods shown to enhance the performance of human learners. This suggests that transformer-based LLMs may share cognitive processes with human learners in arithmetic. Lastly, we provide a comprehensive demonstration of our method's effectiveness, including significant accuracy improvement experiments, visualization verification, and explanation-based analysis to illuminate the intricacies of LLMs in arithmetic learning. In general, this work explores the potential similarities between transformer-based LLMs and human learners, supported by explainable AI (XAI) verifications, ultimately fostering trust in LLMs for critical and high-stakes applications.

</details>


### [180] [DADIR: Density-Aware Data-level Imbalanced Regression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17178)
*Shermin Shahbazi,Hossein Mohammadi,Mohsen Afsharchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DADIR提出了一种密度感知的数据级不平衡回归框架，通过自适应目标空间划分、密度正则化条件变分自编码器和潜空间数据平衡三个组件，有效识别少数区域、保留稀疏区域信息并生成结构一致的合成样本，在多个不平衡回归数据集上显著提升了预测性能，特别是欠表示区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡回归因连续目标变量和异构密度分布而极具挑战性，现有数据级方法往往依赖固定的目标划分或合成样本生成，未能联合考虑密度变化和局部特征空间结构。

Method: DADIR包含三个组件：(1) 密度感知自适应划分(DAAP)，根据密度变化递归划分目标空间；(2) 密度正则化条件变分自编码器(DR-CVAE)，在学习潜在特征时保留稀疏区域表示；(3) 潜空间数据平衡，结合特征级聚类和过采样生成结构一致的合成样本。

Result: 在多个不平衡回归数据集上，DADIR在欠表示区域和整体准确性方面均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: DADIR生成的平衡数据集可直接用于现有回归模型，无需修改其架构或学习目标，有效解决了不平衡回归问题。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning addresses predictive modeling problems with underrepresented regions of the data distribution. Although widely studied in classification, imbalanced regression remains challenging because of continuous target variables and heterogeneous density distributions. Existing data-level methods often rely on fixed target partitioning or synthetic sample generation without jointly considering density variations and local feature-space structure. We propose DADIR, a Density-Aware Data-level Imbalanced Regression framework that exploits density information throughout the balancing process. DADIR comprises three components: (1) Density-Aware Adaptive Partitioning (DAAP), which recursively partitions the target space according to density variations; (2) a Density-Regularized Conditional Variational Autoencoder (DR-CVAE), which preserves sparse-region representations while learning latent features; and (3) latent-space data balancing, which combines feature-level clustering with oversampling to generate structurally consistent synthetic samples. Together, these components identify minority regions more effectively, preserve sparse-region information, and generate realistic synthetic data. The resulting balanced dataset can be used directly with existing regression models without modifying their architecture or learning objective. Experiments on diverse imbalanced regression datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in predictive performance, particularly in underrepresented regions, while also improving overall accuracy.

</details>


### [181] [DynImmune-BERT: Dynamic Immune Repertoire Modeling with Neural ODE Driven Continuous Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17244)
*Rong Fu,Yongtai Liu,Xiaowen Ma,Haoyu Zhao,Shuo Yin,Yiqing Lyu,Long Zhang,Wangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynImmune-BERT，一种基于连续时间T细胞受体库建模的患者免疫状态预测方法，通过深度自适应中心对数比初始化、克隆存在门控神经ODE动态、有界邻域自注意力、事件状态重置和混合传输目标等技术创新，实现了对纵向免疫库中克隆扩增、收缩、消失和重现信号的有效建模，在多个评估维度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态库语言模型将样本视为序列的集合，忽略了采样间隔、测序深度和克隆存在模式等时间信息，无法充分利用纵向T细胞受体库中蕴含的克隆动态信号（扩增、收缩、消失、重现）。

Method: 1) 深度自适应中心对数比初始化；2) 克隆存在门控神经常微分方程动态；3) 有界邻域自注意力；4) 事件状态重启；5) 混合传输目标（同时监督主导克隆和稀有克隆质量分布）；6) 低秩元适配器初始化重现克隆型，保持参数数量与观测克隆数无关。

Result: 事件感知的时间建模能够互补强静态编码器；在存在纵向库结构时表现更优；小规模外部队列和协议差异需谨慎解读；提供了校准和阈值诊断分析。

Conclusion: DynImmune-BERT在纵向TCR库免疫状态预测任务上展现出有效性，但小规模外部队列和协议差异对结果解读构成挑战，未来需要在更大规模、更标准化的数据集上进一步验证。

Abstract: Longitudinal T cell receptor repertoires contain signals of clonal expansion, contraction, disappearance, and reappearance after immune perturbation. Static repertoire language models usually summarize a sample as a bag of sequences, so the sampling interval, sequencing depth, and clone presence pattern are only weakly represented. This paper presents DynImmune-BERT, a continuous time repertoire model for patient level immune status prediction. The method combines depth adaptive centered log ratio initialization, clone presence gated Neural ordinary differential equation dynamics, bounded neighborhood self attention, event based state restart, and a hybrid transport objective that supervises dominant and rare clone mass. A low rank meta adapter initializes reappearing clonotypes while keeping the parameter count independent of the number of observed clones. The evaluation separates literature reported baselines from internally controlled temporal comparisons, reports uncertainty for small external cohorts, adds calibration and threshold diagnostics, and visualizes latent clone trajectories and attention neighborhoods. The results indicate that event aware temporal modeling can complement strong static encoders when longitudinal repertoire structure is available, while small external cohorts and protocol differences require cautious interpretation.

</details>


### [182] [Distilled Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17247)
*Chen Wang,Zhaochun Li,Jionghao Bai,Yining Zhang,Hexuan Deng,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Distilled RL方法，将教师监督整合到RL目标中，通过反向重要性采样裁剪、负样本重置和序列级几何归一化，解决RL信用分配困难与OPD无条件模仿的缺陷，在族内和跨族蒸馏中均优于标准RL和OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法中，RL依赖粗粒度结果监督导致信用分配困难且难以获取新知识；OPD无条件匹配教师logits，相似教师提供新知识有限，差异大的教师指导效果差，限制了OPD主要在族内蒸馏。需要一种既能提供细粒度指导、选择性传递新知识、又能避免无条件模仿的方法。

Method: 提出Distilled RL，包含三个组件：1) 反向重要性采样与裁剪（reverse importance sampling with clipping），2) 负样本重置（negative sample reset），3) 序列级几何归一化（sequence-level geometric normalization）。将教师监督整合到RL目标中，提供细粒度指导并选择性传递新知识。

Result: 通过简洁可解释的案例研究证明Distilled RL能有效从教师模型向学生模型传递先前不可用的知识。在族内和跨族蒸馏设置的广泛实验中，Distilled RL在pass@1和pass@k指标上均显著优于标准RL和OPD。

Conclusion: Distilled RL有效融合了RL和教师指导的优势，解决了RL信用分配困难和OPD无条件模仿的局限，在多种蒸馏场景下均取得更优性能，是一种更有效的LLM后训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training is essential for improving reasoning, adaptation, and alignment. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, RL relies on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in difficult credit assignment and limited capability to acquire new knowledge. OPD, meanwhile, unconditionally matches teacher logits through KL divergence, which creates a dilemma: similar teachers provide little new knowledge, while substantially different teachers often yield ineffective guidance, largely restricting OPD to within-family distillation. We propose Distilled Reinforcement Learning (Distilled RL), which integrates teacher supervision into the RL objective to provide fine-grained guidance, selectively transfer new knowledge and avoid unconditional imitation. Distilled RL contains three components: reverse importance sampling with clipping, negative sample reset, and sequence-level geometric normalization. Through a concise and interpretable case study, we demonstrate that Distilled RL can effectively transfer previously unavailable knowledge from a teacher model to a student model. Extensive experiments across both within-family and cross-family distillation settings show that Distilled RL substantially outperforms standard RL and OPD in terms of both pass@1 and pass@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL.

</details>


### [183] [Node4All: Learning Node Representation Beyond Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17272)
*Dooho Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Node4All提出了一种无需针对特定数据集优化的通用节点表示学习方法，通过通道图变换器（CGT）和基于合成图的自监督学习，在25个基准测试中取得有竞争力的结果，并支持一次性学习和上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节点表示学习方法大多需要针对每个数据集单独训练和调参，缺乏跨数据集的通用性和可复用性。

Method: 架构层面提出通道图变换器（CGT），使单一固定参数化能处理任意图数据集；学习层面提出基于合成图的自监督学习方法。

Result: 在25个基准测试、21个基线方法的节点分类评估中，单一Node4All模型统一应用于所有数据集，排名第5；在一次性学习和上下文学习设置中优于最近的图基础模型。

Conclusion: Node4All实现了跨任意图数据集的可复用性，同时保持实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Node representation learning has advanced rapidly, yet most existing methods rely on per-dataset training and hyperparameter tuning. This dataset-specific optimization comes from the difficulty of designing reusable graph models that generalize across diverse graph datasets. In this work, we introduce Node4All, a node representation learner applicable to arbitrary graph datasets without any dataset-specific optimization.
  Node4All is built on two complementary ideas. At the architectural level, we introduce the Channel Graph Transformer (CGT), which enables a single fixed parameterization to process arbitrary graph datasets. At the learning level, we propose a self-supervised learning based on a series of synthetic graphs. Together, these components enable generalization beyond individual datasets, which is infeasible with existing architectures and learning frameworks. We extensively evaluate Node4All on node classification across 25 benchmarks against 21 baselines, covering both supervised and self-supervised methods. Despite all baselines being trained and optimized for each dataset, a single Node4All, applied uniformly across the datasets, achieves a competitive ranking of 5th among 21 baselines. Moreover, Node4All supports one-shot and in-context learning with an appropriate predictor and outperforms recent graph foundation models (GFMs) in these settings. These results demonstrate that Node4All not only achieves reusability across arbitrary graph datasets, but also remains an effective solution in practice. Code and model checkpoints are available in https://github.com/dooho00/node4all.

</details>


### [184] [AIGB-R1: Self-Evolving Generative Auto-Bidding via Hierarchical Planner-Executor Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17281)
*Yuejia Dou,Hesong Wang,Xinyu Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhilin Zhang,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIGB-R1，一种分层自进化自动竞价框架，利用LLM的推理能力增强AI生成竞价，通过高层规划器与低层执行器的分离设计，结合经验驱动的自进化循环和去耦组相对策略优化（D-GRPO），在离线预训练与后训练对齐的两阶段流程下，有效解决了现有AIGB方法中离线数据模式覆盖有限、任务状态理解不足以及直接应用LLM面临的数值精度、幻觉和推理延迟问题。大规模公开数据集上的实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成竞价（AIGB）范式受限于离线数据集的有限模式覆盖和任务状态理解不足，难以有效探索最优策略；直接应用大语言模型（LLM）到自动竞价任务面临数值精度不足、幻觉问题和推理延迟等固有挑战。

Method: 提出AIGB-R1分层自进化竞价框架：1）高层Planner模块负责宏观策略规划，低层Executor模块负责细粒度决策；2）设计经验驱动的自进化循环，实现自主策略探索与优化；3）采用离线预训练+后训练对齐的两阶段流程，构建交互式竞价仿真环境用于策略推演；4）提出去耦组相对策略优化（D-GRPO）方法，通过优势去耦实现端到端优化。

Result: 在大型公开数据集上的实验证明了AIGB-R1的有效性。

Conclusion: AIGB-R1通过将LLM的推理能力引入自动竞价任务，结合分层架构与自进化机制，有效克服了传统AIGB和直接应用LLM的局限性，为在线广告中的自动竞价优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Auto-bidding plays an essential role in online advertising, automatically adjusting bids for advertisers to optimize their commercial goals. The emerging AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) paradigm widely adopts generative modeling to optimize bidding strategies, yet suffers from the limited mode coverage of offline datasets and inadequate task-state understanding, hindering effective exploration of optimal strategies. Large Language Models (LLMs), with prior world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, offer a promising approach to overcome these limitations. However, directly applying LLMs to auto-bidding tasks faces inherent challenges in limited numerical precision, hallucinations, and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose AIGB-R1, a hierarchical self-evolving auto-bidding framework aiming to enhance AI-Generated Bidding via LLMs' Reasoning capabilities, comprising a high-level Planner module for macro-level strategy planning and a low-level Executor module for fine-grained decision-making. Building upon this, we design an experience-driven self-evolving loop, enabling autonomous strategy exploration and optimization from accumulated experience. We adopt a two-stage pipeline of offline pre-training and post-training alignment, and build an interactive bidding simulation environment for strategy rollout. Furthermore, we propose Decoupled Group Relative Policy Optimization (D-GRPO) to achieve end-to-end optimization via advantage decoupling. Experimental results on a large-scale public dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of AIGB-R1.

</details>


### [185] [An Iterative Geometric Approach to Optimizing Separating Hyperplanes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17282)
*Akos Hajnal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何方法，从初始分离超平面出发，通过逐步优化对齐方式并持续增加间隔，最终收敛到最大间隔超平面（硬间隔SVM）。该方法每次迭代仅利用当前活动集的局部信息，将原凸二次优化问题分解为一系列更小的子问题。实验表明，当存在初始分离超平面时，该方法在大数据集上具有竞争力，有时能超越直接求解优化问题的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当给定一个初始分离超平面时，能否利用它来更高效地计算最大间隔分离超平面（硬间隔SVM分类器）。

Method: 提出一种几何方法：从初始分离超平面开始，在保持分离性的同时逐步改善超平面对齐方式，持续增加间隔直至收敛到全局最优。每次迭代仅利用当前活动集的局部信息，根据该缩减子集的最优分离超平面对当前超平面进行重新对齐，从而将原凸二次优化问题分解为一系列更小的子问题。

Result: 当存在初始分离超平面时，该方法在大数据集上具有竞争力，在某些情况下能够超越直接求解优化问题的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 基于初始分离超平面的渐进式几何对齐方法能够高效求解硬间隔SVM，在特定场景下具备优于传统方法的性能。

Abstract: Given a binary-labeled linearly separable dataset, and the objective is to compute the maximum-margin separating hyperplane, also known as the hard-margin Support Vector Machine (SVM) classifier. This paper investigates whether, if given an initial separating hyperplane, can it be exploited to reach this unique optimum more efficiently. We present a geometric approach that gradually improves the alignment of the hyperplane, starting from an initial separating hyperplane, while preserving separation and continuously increasing its margin until convergence to the global optimum. At each iteration, the method considers only local information, namely the current active set, and aims to re-align the hyperplane according to the optimal separating hyperplane of this reduced subset. Consequently, the original convex quadratic optimization problem is addressed through a sequence of smaller subproblems. The paper presents the algorithm in detail, together with a preliminary experimental evaluation and several theoretical findings. The results suggest that, when an initial separating hyperplane is available, the proposed method can be competitive on larger datasets and, in some cases, can outperform state-of-the-art approaches that solve the optimization problem directly.

</details>


### [186] [Lookahead Branching for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17290)
*Liam Davis,Duo Zhou,Huan Zhang,Guy Katz,Clark Barrett,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前瞻分支策略在神经网络验证中的效果，提出了一种将前瞻技术集成到任何分支定界验证器中的通用方法，证明了当前最先进的分支启发式算法FSB可被视为前瞻分支策略的特例，并在两个代表性验证器（Marabou和α-β-CROWN）上实现了高达57%的实例求解提升和一致的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证中分支定界算法的效率受分支策略质量影响显著，现有启发式方法（如FSB）性能有限，需探索更优的分支策略以加速验证过程。

Method: 提出一种通用前瞻分支策略框架，可集成到任意分支定界验证器中；将FSB重新解释为前瞻策略的特例；利用前瞻过程不仅改进分支决策质量，还生成额外引理以加速验证。在Marabou和α-β-CROWN两个验证器上实现该框架。

Result: 前瞻分支策略在多个基准上实现一致的验证时间加速，最高能多解决57%的实例。

Conclusion: 前瞻分支策略是一种有效且通用的方法，能显著提升神经网络分支定界验证器的性能，且现有先进启发式可纳入该框架。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the effect of lookahead branching strategies in neural network verification. We present a general recipe to integrate lookahead into any branch-and-bound verifier and demonstrate how one of the current state-of-the-art branching heuristics, FSB, can be viewed as a special instantiation of the lookahead branching strategy. We also describe how, in addition to improving the quality of branching decisions, lookahead can generate additional lemmas that accelerate verification. We instantiate the method in two representative branch-and-bound-based verifiers (Marabou and $α$-$β$-CROWN), and demonstrate that lookahead leads to consistent speedups in verification time and up to $57\%$ more solved instances. Code is available at https://github.com/ai-ar-research/lookahead-branching.

</details>


### [187] [Rationalizing Boltzmann Rationality: An Axiomatic Characterization of Entropy-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17316)
*Silviu Pitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过区分“机会”与“选择”两种随机性，证明在策略和价值函数上施加IIA和单调性公理能唯一确定softmax策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程，从而解决了softmax策略与MDP奖励结构独立性公理之间的张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: softmax策略是RL中随机选择的默认模型，但现有文献未能从第一性原理唯一推导出softmax形式；soft Bellman方程中的熵奖励项违反了支撑MDP奖励结构的独立性公理，这一基本张力未得到解决。

Method: 区分环境抽彩（chance）和策略选择（choice）两类随机性，将VNM独立性公理限制于环境抽彩；在策略和价值函数的选择节点处施加IIA和单调性公理，从而公理化地推导出Boltzmann策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程。

Result: IIA和单调性公理唯一确定了softmax策略形式；软/硬贝尔曼方程的选择简化为一个设计决策——智能体是否重视自身的选择能力；证明了在广义折扣下的回报单调性和收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文为softmax策略提供了规范的公理化基础，将经济学和信息论中独立发展出的相同结构进行了综合，并对智能体设计中何时适用IIA给出了规范性评估。

Abstract: The softmax policy $π(a \mid s) \propto \exp(βQ(s,a))$ is the default model of stochastic choice in reinforcement learning (RL). Various justifications based on robustness, exploration, and optimization have been offered in the RL literature, but none uniquely derives the softmax form from first principles. This leaves a basic tension unresolved: the entropy bonus in the soft Bellman equation violates the Independence axiom that underwrites the Markov decision process (MDP) reward structure. We dissolve this tension by distinguishing two kinds of randomness: chance and choice. By restricting von Neumann-Morgenstern (VNM) Independence to environmental lotteries over base prospects, we show that imposing independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA) and monotonicity on the policy and value functions at choice nodes uniquely determines the Boltzmann policy, the entropy-regularized representation, and the soft Bellman equation. The choice between the soft and hard Bellman equations thus reduces to a design decision: whether the agent values its own ability to choose. We develop RL-specific consequences, including return monotonicity and convergence under generalized discounting, and synthesize the independent lines from economics and information theory that arrive at the same structure, offering a normative assessment of when IIA is appropriate for agent design.

</details>


### [188] [TAPAS: Throughput-adaptive Perception for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17317)
*Aman Vyas,Vasista Kodumagulla,Zain Taufique,Pasi Liljeberg,Anil Kanduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TAPAS的吞吐量自适应感知策略，通过强化学习和门控循环单元实现运行时资源分配，在移动/边缘平台上根据场景复杂度动态调整FPS目标，在满足吞吐需求的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知策略采用固定FPS和静态模型到集群映射，无法适应运行时场景复杂度的变化，导致吞吐量过度/不足供给或不必要的高能耗。

Method: 采用强化学习结合奖励推理模型（RRM）和门控循环单元（GRU）代理，在异构移动/边缘平台上编排感知任务，实现基于变化FPS目标的智能运行时资源分配。

Result: 在KITTI测试序列上达到93-100%吞吐量满足率并节省76%能耗；在未见过的nuScenes数据集上保持97%吞吐量满足率，能耗比SOTA低64%。

Conclusion: TAPAS通过场景复杂度感知和动态模型-集群映射，在保证吞吐需求的同时大幅降低能耗，且具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous systems rely on a perception module to navigate through dynamic environments. In real-world scenarios, the perception module's throughput requirements vary at runtime due to changes in scene complexity. However, existing perception strategies assume a fixed FPS and static model-to-cluster mapping, resulting in either over/under provision of throughput requirements or unnecessary energy consumption across diverse scenes. Addressing this challenge requires tightly coupled \textit{scene complexity awareness} to estimate an appropriate FPS target and \textit{dynamic model-to-cluster mapping} to deliver the required throughput at minimum energy. We propose a throughput-adaptive perception strategy for mobile/edge platforms, enabling intelligent runtime resource allocation based on varying FPS targets. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) with RRM (Reward Reasoning Model) and a GRU (Gated Recurrent Unit) agent to orchestrate perception tasks across heterogeneous mobile/edge platforms. We evaluate TAPAS on Jetson Orin NX across KITTI and unseen nuScenes. On the \textit{KITTI} dataset's test sequences, TAPAS achieves 93-100% throughput met rate while saving energy by 76%. On the unseen \textit{nuScenes} dataset, TAPAS maintains 97% throughput met rate with 64% lower energy compared to \textit{SOTA} approaches, proving its robustness.

</details>


### [189] [Rethinking the Suitability of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms Under Practical Transfer Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17326)
*Hany Hamed,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了迁移强化学习中两个被忽视的评估维度：实际效率（基于真实时间而非交互步数的预算）和动态不匹配下的鲁棒性（领域随机化对不同RL范式的影响）。主要发现：PPO虽然样本效率低，但在大规模并行训练下实际训练时间更快；领域随机化对PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2三种不同范式算法的影响方式相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移式强化学习需要超越标准样本效率的评估维度。现有研究缺乏对实际训练时间和领域随机化下不同RL范式鲁棒性的系统比较。

Method: 在迁移学习场景下，对比三类代表性算法：PPO（在策略）、SAC（离策略）和TD-MPC2（基于模型的规划），分别从真实时间效率和领域随机化鲁棒性两个维度进行受控实验。

Result: 1) 样本效率低的PPO在真实训练时间上反而更快，验证了大规模并行训练范式的有效性；2) 领域随机化对三种不同范式算法的影响表现出相似性。

Conclusion: 评估RL算法不应仅看样本效率，还需考虑实际训练时间等实用因素以及算法生成可用策略的能力。这是首个在相同迁移协议下对PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2进行领域随机化覆盖效果受控比较的研究。

Abstract: Transfer-oriented reinforcement learning requires evaluating algorithms along dimensions that go beyond standard sample efficiency. We focus on two dimensions: practical efficiency, which asks whether conclusions about algorithm suitability change under wall-clock rather than interaction-based budgets, and robustness under dynamics mismatch, which asks how different learning paradigms respond to variability in the training distribution induced by domain randomization. We provide two insights to reinforcement-learning practitioners. First, comparing the sample efficiency of different algorithms is often an insufficient criterion in transfer-oriented settings. The wall-clock time required to train a decent policy is an important consideration for practitioners, and we find that the sample-inefficient PPO algorithm can produce a performant policy faster than relatively more sample-efficient algorithms such as SAC and TD-MPC2, validating the common understanding of massively parallel training paradigms. Second, domain randomization can help different kinds of algorithms learn robust policies. In particular, although PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 represent different RL paradigms - on-policy, off-policy, and model-based learning and planning, respectively - we find that domain randomization affects all three algorithms in a similar way. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first controlled comparison of the effect of domain-randomization coverage on PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 under the same transfer protocol. Taken together, these two insights highlight the importance of evaluating RL algorithms not only by sample efficiency, but also by practical considerations such as training time and the algorithms' ability to produce usable policies.

</details>


### [190] [When Drift Detectors cry Wolf: False Alarm Rates in continuous ML Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17336)
*Raj Shekhar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种常用漂移检测器（PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD和对抗验证）在连续监控条件下的假阳性行为，发现PSI在小批量时易产生频繁误报，但批量超过200后显著改善；KS、MMD和LSDD在不同批量下表现更稳定但仍有波动；Bonferroni校正可降低假阳性率但牺牲灵敏度。研究为生产环境中的漂移检测器选择和校准提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的漂移检测研究多聚焦于合成偏移下的检测准确性，忽视了生产环境中连续监控导致的小假阳性率累积引发警报疲劳的问题。本文旨在系统分析五种常用漂移检测器在连续监控条件下的假阳性行为，为生产ML系统提供实际指导。

Method: 对PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD和对抗验证五种漂移检测器进行实证分析，在不同批量大小下比较其假阳性率表现，并测试Bonferroni校正对假阳性率与真阳性灵敏度之间权衡的影响。

Result: PSI对批量大小高度敏感，小样本时频繁假报警，但批量超200后稳定性大幅提升；KS、MMD和LSDD在不同批量下持续波动但低数据量下比PSI更可靠；Bonferroni校正降低假阳性率但以牺牲真阳性灵敏度为代价。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了漂移检测中稳定性与灵敏度之间的固有权衡，并基于实证结果提供了生产环境中漂移检测器的选择与校准实用指南。

Abstract: Drift detection is a core component of production machine learning monitoring systems, where detectors are used to compare incoming data with a reference distribution and trigger alerts when changes occur. However, these detectors are often evaluated in research settings that emphasize detection accuracy under synthetic shifts, while overlooking false alarms under continuous monitoring. In production environments, models are monitored repeatedly over time and across many features, and even small false positive rates can accumulate into frequent alerts, leading to alarm fatigue. We empirically analyze false positive behavior across five commonly used drift detectors: PSI, KS, MMD, LSDD, and adversarial validation. Consistent with existing literature, PSI exhibits strong sensitivity to batch size, producing frequent false alarms at small sample sizes; however, we further observe that its behavior stabilizes and improves substantially once batch sizes exceed approximately 200 samples. In contrast, KS, MMD, and LSDD display persistent fluctuations across batch sizes, while remaining comparatively more reliable than PSI in low-data regimes. Applying a Bonferroni correction reduces false positive rates, but often at the cost of reduced true positive sensitivity, reinforcing the well-known stability - sensitivity trade-off in drift detection. This work provides a systematic comparison of false positive behavior across multiple drift detectors under continuous monitoring conditions. We identify tradeoffs across detector families and provide practical guidelines for selecting and calibrating drift detectors in production ML systems.

</details>


### [191] [A multiverse-consensus pipeline for reproducible feature selection in untargeted LC-MS metabolomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17345)
*Mohammed Saeed Al-Huraibi,Ihsan Yozgat,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多宇宙分析(multiverse analysis)应用于非靶向LC-MS代谢组学的特征选择，通过配置驱动的可审计流程记录每个特征在十阶段质量控制过滤中的命运，并基于四种对比预处理策略和四种特征排序方法构建多宇宙分析，最终通过共识机制筛选出预处理鲁棒的特征。在乳腺癌细胞系数据集中，单管道间Jaccard系数低至0.05，而多宇宙共识保留了15个特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非靶向LC-MS代谢组学分析需要大量预处理决策，每个决策都有多种合理选项。分析人员通常只采用单一管道并报告最终特征短列表，但该列表对从未变化的决策的依赖性不可见。作者旨在将隐藏的分析自由度转化为显式、可检查的输出，提高分析的可重复性和可靠性。

Method: 提出可审计的配置驱动管道：(i)十阶段质量控制过滤级联，记录每个特征的去留；(ii)将下游分析构建为多宇宙，包含四种对比预处理策略×四种特征排序方法，结合bootstrap稳定性选择和标签置换检验。仅跨路径重复出现的特征进入分层共识。最后进行全管道标签置换检验控制假阳性。

Result: 在五种乳腺癌细胞系数据集(30,370个检测特征)上，四种单管道分别返回4-20个特征，配对Jaccard系数低至0.05。多宇宙共识保留15个特征(≥2/4路径)，其中1个特征在所有四条路径中出现。全管道标签置换检验在50次无效置换中未发现假阳性发现。

Conclusion: 仅报告预处理鲁棒的特征，并附带完整的保留/丢弃审计追踪，可将隐藏的分析自由度转化为显式、可检查的输出。讨论了范围和局限性，包括单批次设计和独立验证的必要性。

Abstract: Background: Untargeted LC-MS metabolomics requires a long chain of preprocessing decisions, each with several equally defensible options. Analysts typically commit to one pipeline and report the resulting feature shortlist. How strongly that shortlist depends on choices that were never varied stays invisible. Results: We adapt multiverse analysis to untargeted metabolomics feature selection. We present an auditable, configuration-driven pipeline that (i) applies a ten-stage quality-control filter cascade in which every feature's fate is logged, and (ii) runs the downstream analysis as a multiverse over four contrasting preprocessing philosophies, each combined with four feature-ranking methods under bootstrap stability selection and label-permutation testing. Only features recurring across paths enter a tiered consensus. On a demonstration dataset of five breast-cancer cell lines (30,370 detected features), the four single pipelines individually returned shortlists of 4-20 features whose pairwise agreement was as low as Jaccard = 0.05. The multiverse consensus retained 15 features (>=2/4 paths), of which one recurred across all four, although two paths (sharing normalization and drift-correction methods) dominate the consensus. A pipeline-wide label-permutation test found no false discoveries in 50 null permutations. Conclusions: Reporting only preprocessing-robust features, with a complete kept/dropped audit trail, converts hidden analytical degrees of freedom into an explicit, inspectable output. We discuss scope and limitations, including single-batch design and the need for independent validation.

</details>


### [192] [Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17377)
*Jiawen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ChebyMA（Chebyshev流形适配）的参数高效微调方法，通过切比雪夫多项式基的多曲面叠加来替代标准线性投影，在CV和NLP任务上取得了优于全参数微调、LoRA等方法的参数-准确率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）在表达能力和参数效率之间仍存在权衡，亟需一种更具表达力且理论基础坚实的适配方法。

Method: 通过可学习坐标上评估的切比雪夫多项式基的多曲面叠加，结合可训练系数矩阵，用连续函数逼近替代标准线性投影；理论上证明了单流形ChebyMA的Frobenius范数重构误差收敛性，并基于Kolmogorov n-宽度论证了多曲面叠加在高维复杂特征解耦中的表达优势。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、AG News、SST-2数据集上，ChebyMA在所有测试数据集上均优于全参数微调、LoRA、TLoRA和StelLA，取得了更优的参数-准确率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: ChebyMA具有坚实的函数逼近理论基础，通过纯向量化计算实现了通用性和高效性，是一种优秀的参数高效微调方法。

Abstract: The paper presents a new parameter-efficient adaptation method called ChebyMA (Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation). ChebyMA adopts weight matrices through a multi-surface superposition of Chebyshev polynomial bases evaluated on learnable coordinates and combined via trainable coefficient matrices, replacing standard linear projections with highly expressive continuous function approximation. Theoretically, we establish an Approximation Expressivity Theorem, proving from the perspective of function approximation theory that single-manifold ChebyMA guarantees convergence in Frobenius norm error of reconstruction. Besides, drawing on Kolmogorov $n$-width intuition, we demonstrate the expressive advantages of multi-manifold superposition ($S > 1$) in decoupling high-dimensional complex features. Experimental results on Computer Vision CIFAR datasets(CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100)\cite{CIFAR} and Natural Language Processing (AG News, SST-2) datasets demonstrate that ChebyMA consistently achieves a superior parameter-accuracy Pareto front compared to standard full-parameter fine-tuning, LoRA\cite{LoRA}, TLoRA\cite{TLoRA}, and StelLA\cite{StelLA}. ChebyMA significantly outperforms other tested methods in tested datasets, validating its solid theoretical foundation for generality with purely vectorized computations.

</details>


### [193] [CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17398)
*Ruogu Chen,Weihua Xiao,Ramesh Karri,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoEvoP&R 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够自动演化解析布局（analytical placement）的优化目标，通过结合布局、时序代理和布线工具的可读反馈，迭代改进可微目标函数。在多个设计上，该方法相比原生 DREAMPlace 显著降低布线后线长和拥塞，并改善时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的解析布局器依赖半周长线长（HPWL）和单元密度惩罚等代理指标，这些指标与最终布线质量和时序质量存在偏差。先前的工作要么需要人工重新调参，要么使用难以解释和部署的黑盒代理。为此，CoEvoP&R 旨在利用 LLM 自动、可解释地演化布局优化目标，弥合代理指标与下游实际质量之间的差距。

Method: 提出一个 LLM 驱动的框架 CoEvoP&R。每一代中，提示（prompt）包含受限目标接口、基线上下文、历史候选方案以及来自布局、时序代理和布线工具的路由相关反馈。LLM 生成可读的可微目标函数，嵌入 DREAMPlace 中进行验证，通过时序代理和实际路由器评估，并将结果与反馈存储，以指导后续代际的演化。

Result: 在八个 ChiP-Bench Nangate45 设计上，相比原生 DREAMPlace，CoEvoP&R 减少了 16.9% 的布线后线长和 36.7% 的拥塞，最差负时序裕量（WNS）提升 0.70 ns，总负时序裕量（TNS）绝对值减少 912 ns。在八个 ICCAD 2015 Superblue 设计上，布线后线长减少 5.4%，拥塞减少 23.2%。

Conclusion: CoEvoP&R 证明了利用 LLM 自动演化可解释、可微的布局目标函数是可行的，能够有效对齐代理指标与下游布线及时序质量，在多个 benchmark 上取得显著改进。

Abstract: Analytical placers rely on differentiable objective functions to guide placement, typically combining intermediate surrogate metrics such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) and cell-density penalties. However, these placement-stage surrogates remain misaligned with downstream routed and timing quality. Prior work reduces this gap with human-designed terms or learned black-box surrogates, but the former requires expert retuning and the latter is difficult to explain, debug, or deploy in analytical placement flows. CoEvoP&R addresses these limitations with a large language model (LLM)-based framework that automatically evolves analytical placement objectives. At each generation, the prompt combines the restricted objective interface, baseline context, and archived prior candidates with routing-related feedback from placement, timing proxy, and routing tools. The LLM proposes readable differentiable objectives, which are embedded and validated in DREAMPlace, evaluated through a timing proxy and an actual router, and stored with their feedback to guide later generations. Across eight ChiP-Bench Nangate45 designs and three seeds, CoEvoP&R reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 16.9% and 36.7%, with gains of 0.70 ns in worst negative slack and a 912 ns reduction in total negative slack magnitude over native DREAMPlace. Across eight ICCAD 2015 Superblue designs, it reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 5.4% and 23.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git.

</details>


### [194] [CORAL: Learning Amyloid Fibril Ligand Docking with Cooperative Binding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17412)
*Yasheng Sun,Bohan Li,Youqi Tao,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORAL是一个基于强化学习的生成式对接框架，专门针对淀粉样纤维的交叉β沟槽几何结构设计，通过协同配体-配体堆叠能量和蛋白质-配体对接亲和力的奖励机制，在实验解析结构和评估集上均优于现有对接基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对接模型无法有效处理淀粉样纤维配体结合的两个根本挑战：一是解析的共晶结构极度稀缺，无法进行监督训练；二是淀粉样纤维的结合模式与球状蛋白本质不同——配体嵌入纵向交叉β沟槽并沿纤维轴协同堆叠，现有对接模型无法捕捉这种几何特征。

Method: 采用强化学习框架训练生成式对接模型，奖励函数显式结合协同配体-配体堆叠能量和蛋白质-配体对接亲和力；同时构建了由领域专家验证的模型生成构象组成的淀粉样纤维-配体复合物评估集。

Result: 在实验解析结构和评估集上，CORAL在配体构象质量和结合亲和力相关性方面均优于现有对接基线方法。

Conclusion: CORAL成功解决了淀粉样纤维配体对接这一特殊且困难的分子识别问题，为神经退行性疾病相关配体的虚拟筛选和设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: A hallmark of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's and Parkinson's is the aberrant aggregation of proteins into amyloid fibrils, and small molecules that selectively bind to these fibrils hold promise as diagnostics, imaging probes, and therapeutics. Predicting how such ligands bind to fibril targets, however, presents two fundamental challenges. First, resolved co-crystal structures of amyloid-ligand complexes are exceptionally scarce; even with recent advances in cryo-EM only a handful have been structurally characterized, making supervised training of docking models impractical for this target class. Second, amyloid fibrils present a binding mode fundamentally different from globular proteins: ligands intercalate into longitudinal cross-$β$ grooves and stack cooperatively along the fibril axis, a geometry that existing docking models are not designed to capture. To address these challenges, we present CORAL (COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking), a reinforcement learning framework that trains a generative docking model to produce ligand pose distributions tailored to the cross-$β$ groove geometry. Our reward explicitly incorporates cooperative ligand-ligand stacking energy alongside protein-ligand docking affinity, directly capturing the distinctive binding geometry of amyloid fibrils. We further introduce a curated evaluation set of amyloid-ligand complexes constructed from model-generated poses validated by domain experts. Experiments on both experimentally resolved structures and this evaluation set demonstrate improved pose quality and binding affinity correlation over existing docking baselines.

</details>


### [195] [Grounded verification of chemical and materials reasoning: detection is the bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层验证器（tiered verifier），通过从LLM推理轨迹中提取可验证的化学主张，与权威数据库和物理规则进行核对，并采用门控修正循环（gated correction loop）来修复错误。实验表明，该方法将分子式错误率从22%降至4%，且检索次数比全面增强法减少3.2倍。修复成功率达80-97%，瓶颈在于环内检测召回率。仅在验证器范围覆盖最终交付物且存在可提取的长尾错误时，才能提升最终答案准确率（83%至90%），在物理常数等接近上限的实体上无提升，在同位素半衰期上提升显著（11%至0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在流畅的推理轨迹中会捏造化学对象（分子式、空间群、形成能），尤其集中在长尾实体上，这些领域模型的置信度最不可靠。已有方法如全局检索增强虽能缓解但开销过大。需要一种更高效的、基于确定性数据库验证的方法来检测和修复这类错误。

Method: 提出分层验证器（tiered verifier），流程包括：(1) 从LLM推理轨迹中提取每个可检查的主张；(2) 对照权威数据库和物理规则进行验证；(3) 如果检测到错误，通过门控修正循环（gated correction loop）将参考信息反馈给模型进行修正。该方法不依赖于全面检索，而是专注于检测而非修复，因为研究发现检测才是瓶颈。

Result: 在4种模型和528个条件固定提示上的实验显示：门控修正将分子式错误率从22%降至4%，检索次数比全面增强法减少3.2倍，性能优于对话式oracle。修复成功率在80-97%之间，瓶颈在于环内检测召回率。最终答案提升仅当验证器范围覆盖最终交付物时出现（83%到90%），且仅在存在可提取长尾错误的场景中有效：在物理常数上无提升，在同位素半衰期上从11%降至0%。

Conclusion: 确定性、基于数据库的验证可以有效捕获和修复LLM在化学推理中的捏造错误，且成本远低于全面检索增强。关键瓶颈在于检测而非修复，因此提升环内检测召回率是进一步改进的方向。验证范围对最终效果有决定性影响，且该方法在处理长尾实体错误时尤为有效。

Abstract: Large language models confabulate chemical objects (molecular formulas, space groups, formation energies) in fluent reasoning traces, concentrated on long-tail entities where confidence is least trustworthy. Deterministic, database-grounded verification can catch and repair such errors without the coverage cost of blanket retrieval; the binding constraint, we find, is detection, not repair. Our tiered verifier extracts each checkable claim, checks it against authoritative databases and physics, and feeds the reference into a gated correction loop. Across four models and 528 condition-pinned prompts, gated correction cuts committed-formula error from 22% to 4% at $3.2\times$ fewer retrievals than blanket augmentation, beating a conversational oracle. Repair succeeds wherever a flag fires (80--97%); the bottleneck is in-loop detection recall. Grounding improves the final answer only when the verifier's scope reaches the deliverable (83% to 90%), and the lift appears only where extractable long-tail error exists: absent on near-ceiling physical constants, large on isotope half-lives (11% to 0%).

</details>


### [196] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KATA框架，通过自对偶齐次锥体保证非负注意力权重，利用秩一PSD特征实现线性注意力。KATA在MQAR任务上接近softmax注意力，在131k token长度下前向吞吐量达到FlashAttention-2的约11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力虽然在推理时具有常数复杂度，但在关联召回任务上性能严重退化。现有方法缺乏从第一性原理出发设计特征映射的理论框架，无法在容量和干扰之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 将注意力召回建模为球面填充问题，通过自对偶齐次锥体认证非负注意力权重，推导出核化线性注意力激活函数(KATA)。利用秩一PSD特征实现容量-干扰权衡，引入Welch干扰下界刻画关联容量。实现为融合Triton内核，支持flash注意力风格前向和精确O(T)分块状态两种模式。

Result: KATA在131k token长度下前向吞吐量达到FlashAttention-2的约11倍；在16倍分布外长度上保留0.985 MQAR准确率；多个变体在长程MQAR和重复键覆写任务上优于Gated DeltaNet。

Conclusion: KATA通过理论驱动的特征映射设计，在线性注意力框架下实现了接近softmax的召回性能，同时显著提升了吞吐量。特征几何与位置编码、delta规则、衰减门控之间存在重要交互，影响语言模型的流畅度。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [197] [Decoder-Preserving Sparse Autoencoders: Which Readouts Survive Sparse Compression?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17425)
*Aniket Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DPSAE方法，通过结合重构损失与最优岭回归预测器之间的矩阵值失真，在保持解码器的情况下训练稀疏自编码器。实验表明，DPSAE在GPT-2 small block 8上将保留的解码器失真降低10.6-11.4%，同时匹配重构NMSE，但发现重构质量并不决定哪些线性读出在稀疏压缩后得以保留，且读出保留不同于学习更清晰的基准概念或保留冻结模型的每种行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)将模型激活压缩为稀疏编码时，相同的重构误差和稀疏度可以保留不同的线性可解码信号，这种歧义性需要被形式化解决。

Method: 本文形式化了最优岭回归预测器之间的矩阵值失真，通过结合该失真与重构损失来训练解码器保留的SAE（DPSAE）。在秩松弛中，各向同性的任务先验在保持PCA排序的同时饱和了每模态遗漏成本，而结构化先验可以改变被保留的模态。

Result: 在GPT-2 small block 8上，DPSAE在三次配对运行中将保留的解码器失真降低10.6-11.4%，同时匹配重构NMSE。相同检查点通过了平均自然文本输出KL非劣效性检验，但一个匹配的Pythia对显示在限制为少数稀疏特征的探针上无改进。

Conclusion: 重构质量并不决定哪些重新拟合的线性读出在稀疏压缩后得以保留，且读出保留不同于学习更清晰的基准概念或保留冻结模型的每种行为。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) compress model activations into sparse codes, but equal reconstruction error and sparsity can preserve different linearly decodable signals. We formalize this ambiguity as a matrix-valued distortion between optimal ridge-prediction operators and train decoder-preserving SAEs by combining this distortion with reconstruction loss. In a rank relaxation, an isotropic task prior saturates per-mode omission costs without changing PCA's ordering, whereas a structured prior can change which modes are retained. A controlled sparse experiment shows that a declared prior protects held-out combinations from its task subspace. On GPT-2 small block 8, DPSAE reduces held-out decoder distortion by 10.6--11.4% across three paired runs while matching reconstruction NMSE. The same checkpoints pass an average natural-text output-KL noninferiority test, but one matched Pythia pair shows no improvement in probes restricted to a few sparse features. These results show that reconstruction quality does not determine which refitted linear readouts survive sparse compression, and that readout preservation is distinct from learning cleaner benchmark concepts or preserving every frozen-model behavior.

</details>


### [198] [Calibrated Alzheimer's Conversion Risk in Mild Cognitive Impairment: Persistent Homology of Clinical Trajectories with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17442)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将持续同调（persistent homology）应用于MCI转AD的纵向临床轨迹点云，并引入分裂共形预测提供个体级不确定性估计。在ADNI 741名MCI受试者中，经泄漏审计后嵌套AUC为0.840，外部AUC达0.879。H0持续熵为关键拓扑生物标志物，共形覆盖率90.4%，跨亚组最大假阴性率公平差距0.092。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCI-to-AD转换预测模型缺乏个体级不确定性估计，且极少包含透明的泄漏审计。作者旨在填补这两方面空白，引入持续同调作为拓扑特征提取工具，并结合共形预测提供可靠的个体风险保证。

Method: 分析ADNI中741名MCI受试者（240名转换者，32.4%），设置统一4年随访上限。修正五种泄漏源；结合Vietoris-Rips持续同调、子水平集代理、轨迹斜率和工程特征（共76维），采用堆叠集成模型，5折交叉验证评估。使用分裂共形预测提供个体级风险保证。

Result: Cox和随机生存森林模型加入TDA特征后C指数分别提升+0.045和+0.014（0.799和0.826 vs 0.753和0.812）。嵌套AUC为0.840（同折上界0.866），外部AUC为0.879。H0持续熵为最显著的SHAP特征，与APOE4剂量显著相关。共形覆盖率90.4%（目标90%），外部覆盖率96.9%，最大公平差距0.092。

Conclusion: 提出H0持续熵作为认知衰退的拓扑生物标志物，证明了经泄漏审计和共形校准的管道在提供个体级不确定性量化的同时达到了有竞争力的预测精度，这是该任务此前未实现的。

Abstract: Background. Predicting conversion from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to Alzheimer's disease (AD) is central to trial enrichment and care planning, yet existing models provide no individual-level uncertainty estimates and rarely include transparent leakage audits. We introduce the first application of persistent homology to longitudinal clinical trajectory point clouds for this task, and the first split-conformal individual risk guarantee for any AD-conversion model. Methods. We analysed 741 MCI subjects (240 converters, 32.4%) from ADNI with a uniform 4-year follow-up cap.
