The system separates an evaluation harness from a profile-backed optimization controller: the harness enforces compilation, correctness, official-aligned timing, and artifact archival, while the controller turns profiler and workload evidence into bounded candidate-generation decisions. Human-authored skills capture operator constraints, references, profiling procedures, and promotion rules, while Codex and Claude Code agents generate candidate kernels inside those constraints. Across five operator definitions, the retained official-aligned artifacts achieved mean-latency speedups over supplied FlashInfer baselines of 1.62x, 18.05x, 29.68x, 1.12x, and 13.70x. The Agent-Assisted kernels outperform the Full-Agent artifacts across the evaluated definitions, indicating that expert-provided optimization directions, high-quality references, and workload context remain critical for reliable AI-driven kernel optimization.

</details>


### [234] [Information-Based Exploration via Random Features for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17981)
*Waris Radji,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机特征信息增益（RFIG），利用随机傅里叶特征近似信息增益，为深度强化学习中的乐观探索提供理论可解释且可扩展的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典探索策略扩展到深度强化学习时，通常使算法更复杂且难以建立理论保证。现有基于神经网络的不确定性估计存在黑箱问题，缺乏理论可解释性。

Method: 基于贝叶斯核方法理论，使用随机傅里叶特征近似信息增益，在不可数状态空间中计算探索奖励。提供信息增益近似的误差界，并给出使其可扩展到深度强化学习的实用细节。

Result: 在多种控制与导航任务中，RFIG在保持竞争性能的同时，相较于已有的深度探索方法提供了更优的理论可解释性。

Conclusion: RFIG提供了理论可解释的深度探索方法，避免了神经网络不确定性估计的黑箱问题，且实验性能与主流方法相当。

Abstract: Representation learning has enabled classical exploration strategies to be extended to deep Reinforcement Learning (RL), but often makes algorithms more complex and theoretical guarantees harder to establish. We introduce Random Feature Information Gain (RFIG), grounded in Bayesian kernel methods theory, which uses random Fourier features to approximate information gain and compute exploration bonuses in non-countable spaces. We provide error bounds on information gain approximation and avoid the black-box aspects of neural network-based uncertainty estimation, for optimism-based exploration. We present practical details that make RFIG scalable to deep RL scenarios, enabling smooth integration into standard deep RL algorithms. Experimental evaluation across diverse control and navigation tasks demonstrates that RFIG achieves competitive performance with well-established deep exploration methods while offering superior theoretical interpretation.

</details>


### [235] [fSRD: Fuzzy Spectral Region Decomposition -- Automated Multi Operator Koopman Representations via an Adaptive Spectral Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17990)
*Charles Bokor,Mark Cary,Denise Morrey,Fabrizio Bonatesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊谱区域分解（fSRD），一种全自动学习框架，通过多个Koopman算子估计有限维表示，利用全局模糊树模型构建局部不变嵌入，在混沌系统和高维真实数据上实现高精度线性重构与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度非线性混沌动力系统因复杂度、表达力和数据效率之间的根本性权衡而难以建模；现有数据驱动的Koopman方法寻求全局有效算子，但难以在有限约束下识别有用的有限维谱嵌入。

Method: 提出模糊谱区域分解（fSRD），一种基于多个Koopman算子的全自动学习框架，通过全局模糊树模型（受模糊神经架构启发）自适应构建局部不变嵌入（称为不变分解），实现数据自适应的有限维表示学习。

Result: 在Lorenz、Duffing等典型混沌系统以及高维真实数据上，fSRD在数据丰富和有限条件下均展现出强预测精度、可解释性和鲁棒表达力。

Conclusion: fSRD成功桥接了可解释的算子理论模型与表达性强的数据驱动序列学习，为非线性动力系统的高效建模提供了一种通用且自动化的解决方案。

Abstract: Highly nonlinear chaotic dynamical systems remain difficult to model due to fundamental trade-offs between complexity, expressivity, and data efficiency. Modern machine learning methods achieve strong predictive performance but often rely on a-priori system knowledge or curated data with limited interpretability. Koopman operator theory offers a promising direction via linear representation in an infinite-dimensional observable space. However, many data-driven Koopman methods seek globally valid operators for which useful finite-dimensional spectral embeddings remain difficult to identify under these constraints. To overcome associated limitations, we introduce Fuzzy Spectral Region Decomposition (fSRD), a fully automated learning framework for estimating finite Koopman representation via multiple operators. The proposed method realizes a data-adaptive framework for assembling locally invariant embeddings, termed Invariant Decomposition. fSRD achieves highly accurate linear reconstructions of nonlinear systems while learning finite-dimensional representations of their induced evolution operators, bridging interpretable operator-theoretic models with expressive data-driven sequence learning. These embeddings are adaptively constructed via a global fuzzy tree model, drawing inspiration from fuzzy neural architectures to learn the induced dynamics while prioritizing parsimonious solutions. Empirical results across canonical chaotic systems (e.g., Lorenz and Duffing) and high-dimensional real-world data demonstrate strong predictive accuracy, interpretability, and robust expressivity across data-rich and data-limited regimes, highlighting the method's generality.

</details>


### [236] [FlashPDE: A Drop-in Fused Triton Operator Library for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18020)
*Peiyu Zang,Bosen Xie,Ruoxiang Xu,Yongqiang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashPDE是一个基于Triton核的融合算子库，用于网格科学的机器学习，通过将碎片化的PyTorch有限差分执行替换为可微分的融合算子，在NVIDIA A100上实现了高达37.0倍的内存减少、3.5倍的内核启动减少以及2.30倍的端到端加速，同时保持与PyTorch参考的数值一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解大规模PDE问题时受限于自动微分的内存开销和网格PDE算子的低效执行，现有PyTorch实现存在碎片化的有限差分执行问题，亟需一种硬件高效的融合算子库来弥合可微分PDE求解器与GPU优化数值计算之间的鸿沟。

Method: FlashPDE将PyTorch的有限差分操作替换为可微分的Triton内核，每个算子整合了融合模板求值、解析离散伴随反向传播以及边界梯度校正，并封装在统一的torch.autograd.Function接口中；库提供了14个覆盖1D-3D椭圆型、抛物型和Navier-Stokes系统的可微分PDE算子，共17种配置，且独立于神经网络架构和训练策略。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100 GPU上，FlashPDE相比基于坐标的自动微分减少峰值内存使用高达37.0倍，相比即时PyTorch有限差分实现减少CUDA内核启动高达3.5倍；在六个代表性PDE基准测试中，实现最高2.30倍端到端时间加速和19.2倍内核级加速，同时保持与PyTorch有限差分参考的数值一致性。

Conclusion: FlashPDE提供了一个硬件高效的执行层，在PyTorch生态系统中桥接了可微分PDE求解器与GPU优化的数值计算，显著提升了大规模科学机器学习中PDE求解的内存效率和计算速度。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve PDEs by incorporating physical constraints into neural-network training, but large-scale problems are limited by automatic-differentiation memory overhead and inefficient execution of grid-based PDE operators. We present FlashPDE, a drop-in fused operator library for grid-based scientific machine learning. FlashPDE replaces fragmented PyTorch finite-difference execution with differentiable Triton kernels. Each operator integrates fused stencil evaluation, an analytic discrete-adjoint backward pass, and boundary-gradient correction within a unified torch.autograd.Function interface. The library provides 14 differentiable PDE operators covering 17 configurations across 1D--3D elliptic, parabolic, and Navier--Stokes systems, while remaining independent of neural architectures and training strategies. Experiments on an NVIDIA A100 GPU show that FlashPDE reduces peak memory usage by up to 37.0x compared with coordinate-based automatic differentiation and reduces CUDA kernel launches by up to 3.5x compared with eager PyTorch finite-difference implementations. Across six representative PDE benchmarks, FlashPDE achieves up to 2.30x end-to-end time-to-solution speedup and up to 19.2x kernel-level acceleration while maintaining numerical agreement with PyTorch finite-difference references. FlashPDE provides a hardware-efficient execution layer that bridges differentiable PDE solvers and GPU-optimized numerical computation within the PyTorch ecosystem.

</details>


### [237] [Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18043)
*Zeyuan Song,Zheyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Mamba神经算子（AMO），通过将再生核与状态空间模型（SSMs）集成，构建Takenaka-Malmquist系统，利用自适应傅里叶分解（AFD）理论，在多种几何形状和网格上精确求解偏微分方程（PDEs），在流体物理、固体物理和金融等基准问题上显著优于现有最先进求解器，并提供了可解释的神经算子框架新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意几何形状和多种网格上精确求解偏微分方程（PDEs）是科学与工程应用中的重要任务，现有方法在泛化性和可解释性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出自适应Mamba神经算子（AMO），将再生核集成到状态空间模型（SSMs）中，替代传统的核积分公式；通过构造Takenaka-Malmquist系统实现与自适应傅里叶分解（AFD）理论的对齐，从而在多种几何形状和网格上近似PDE解流形。

Result: 在点云、结构化网格、规则网格和不规则域上的多个挑战性基准PDE问题（涵盖流体物理、固体物理和金融领域）中，AMO在相对$L^2$误差指标上持续优于最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计可解释的神经算子框架提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Accurately solving partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries and a variety of meshes is an important task in science and engineering applications. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators (AMO), which integrates reproducing kernels for state-space models (SSMs) rather than the kernel integral formulation of SSMs. This is achieved by constructing Takenaka-Malmquist systems for the PDEs. AMO offers new representations that align well with the adaptive Fourier decomposition (AFD) theory and can approximate the solution manifold of PDEs on a wide range of geometries and meshes. In several challenging benchmark PDE problems in the fields of fluid physics, solid physics, and finance on point clouds, structured meshes, regular grids, and irregular domains, AMO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art solvers in terms of relative $L^2$ error. Overall, this work presents a new paradigm for designing explainable neural operator frameworks.

</details>


### [238] [SEE: Structure-aware Exploring \& Exploiting for Long-horizon GUI Agent Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18046)
*Zhuohang Fan,Beichen Zhang,Yuanfa Li,Changqiao Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEE提出了一种两阶段数据合成框架，通过构建显式UI转换图并在图上进行规划与可控采样，生成高覆盖、长时序的移动GUI交互轨迹，解决了现有方法依赖人工演示、对稀有转换和复杂多步流程覆盖不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体数据合成管道过度采样常见流程，缺失稀有转换和复杂多步程序，且依赖昂贵的人工演示或在线策略框架，限制了模型在元素丰富、快速演进的移动应用上的表现。

Method: 两阶段框架：(i) 高效探索阶段，在屏幕和元素上构建显式UI转换图；(ii) 基于图的合成阶段，通过规划和受控采样组合多样化的多步轨迹，防止虚假循环并支持长时序组合。

Result: 在多个真实应用上，SEE生成了平均长度14.8步且无虚假循环的轨迹；基于SEE微调的智能体在任务成功率和未见屏幕泛化能力上均有提升。

Conclusion: SEE通过可复现、可解释的数据生成方式，有效提升了GUI智能体的长时序任务执行能力和泛化性能，代码与数据集将开源。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by vision-language models hold promise for automating real-world mobile tasks. However, progress is limited by the lack of high-coverage, long-horizon interaction trajectories collected from element-rich and rapidly evolving apps. Existing pipelines often rely on costly human demonstrations or on-policy framework, which tends to over-sample common flows while missing rare transitions and complex multi-step procedures. To address this problem, we propose SEE, a two-stage data synthesis framework consisting of (i) an efficient exploration stage that builds an explicit UI transition graph over screens and elements, and (ii) a graph-based synthesis stage that composes diverse multi-step trajectories via planning and controlled sampling. This design yields reproducible and explainable data generation, while explicitly preventing spurious cycles and enabling long-horizon composition. Across multiple real-world apps, SEE produces trajectories with an average length of 14.8 steps while avoiding spurious loops, and agents fine-tuned on SEE achieve improved task success and generalization to unseen screens. We will publicly release our synthesis code and dataset.

</details>


### [239] [SGN: A Similarity-based Generative Network for Data Generation under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18072)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Xincheng Chen,Yuncheng Wu,Zhaojing Luo,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参数更新的可复用生成框架SGN，利用标签诱导的相似性结构在潜在空间中进行目标域数据增强，解决源域到目标域的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源域训练的生成模型在目标域上样本对齐差，传统域适应需要额外优化和特定参数，缺乏可复用性。

Method: 构建编码器-解码器架构，学习基于标签诱导成对相似性的潜在空间结构；生成时编码目标域小样本标注集，在潜在空间组合并继承目标域特征。

Result: 在图像和表格数据集上验证了SGN在源到目标分布偏移下进行目标引导数据增强的有效性。

Conclusion: SGN是一种无需参数更新的可复用框架，通过相似性结构实现跨域数据增强，并对相似性结构的可实现性和维度需求进行了理论分析。

Abstract: Generative models trained on a source domain often produce samples that are poorly aligned with shifted target domains, limiting their effectiveness for target-domain data augmentation. Although target-specific adaptation can reduce this mismatch, it typically requires additional optimization and domain-specific parameters. We propose a Similarity-based Generative Network (SGN), a reusable framework that is trained once on labeled source data and applied to new target domains without parameter updates. SGN learns a latent space structured by label-induced pairwise similarities while preserving reconstructive information through an encoder-decoder architecture. At generation time, a small labeled representative set from the target domain is encoded and combined in the learned latent space, allowing the generated samples to inherit target-specific characteristics while maintaining class consistency. We further analyze the realizability and dimensionality requirements of the proposed similarity structure. Experiments on image and tabular datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SGN for target-guided data augmentation under source-to-target distribution shifts.

</details>


### [240] [Sobek: Streaming Equivariant Tensor Product Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18074)
*Vladimir Chorošajev,Cédric Bény*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sobek，一种流式执行方案，通过重新结合径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合，消除等变图神经网络中边缘规模张量积中间变量的内存瓶颈，在75种配置下均实现1.2倍至49.7倍加速，峰值内存降低高达99%，并能执行超出OpenEquivariance两个数量级的工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变图神经网络实现中，边缘特定的权重、消息和伴随矩阵会导致张量积工作空间和内存流量随图规模和算子宽度快速增长，限制了可行工作负载规模，无法充分利用GPU算力。

Method: 通过重新关联径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合操作，将边缘局部的乘积直接消费到有界的接收端状态中，形成流式执行方案，该方案保留了全连接多重性混合，并支持前向、反向和双反向传播。

Result: 在两种算子族和所有三种微分阶数下，Sobek在75种容量匹配的比较中全部更快，加速比为1.2倍至49.7倍，峰值分配内存降低高达99%，并能执行超出OpenEquivariance容量两个数量级的工作负载且保持近峰值吞吐量。

Conclusion: 边缘规模张量积工作空间并非等变卷积本身的固有需求，而是传统执行调度的产物。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks repeatedly apply edge-conditioned tensor-product convolutions over graph edges. Conventional implementations materialize edge-specific weights, messages, and adjoints, causing tensor-product workspace and memory traffic to grow rapidly with graph size and operator width. This limits feasible workloads and can prevent larger problems from fully utilizing the GPU.
  We show that these edge-sized intermediates are artifacts of the execution schedule, not requirements of the equivariant operator. By reassociating radial projection, spherical-harmonic coupling, and graph aggregation, edge-local products can be consumed directly into bounded receiver-side state. The resulting streaming formulation preserves fully connected multiplicity mixing and extends through forward, backward, and double backward.
  We implement this formulation in Sobek, a generated-CUDA backend, and evaluate it across edge-scaling regimes and varied feature structures. Across two operator families and all three differentiation orders, Sobek is faster in all 75 capacity-matched comparisons, with speedups ranging from $1.2\times$ to $49.7\times$, and reduces peak allocated memory by up to 99\%. It also executes workloads up to two orders of magnitude beyond OpenEquivariance's capacity while retaining near-peak throughput. These results show that edge-scaled tensor-product workspace is a property of the conventional schedule, not of equivariant convolution itself.

</details>


### [241] [Generalised Bellman recurrence and three dualities in sequential decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18077)
*Fernando E. Rosas,David Hyland,Daniel Polani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Bellman方程的形式来源于三个条件：动态通过充分统计量分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合与两者兼容。当这些条件在共同状态上满足时，Bellman方程由它们的一致性产生；当某个条件不满足时，可通过增广状态或变形回报/动态来恢复可解性。这三个条件还产生了三种对偶性，将强化学习、控制理论和决策理论中的方法统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Bellman方程形式背后的深层原因，解释其递归性质从何而来，并统一强化学习、控制理论和决策理论中各自发展的方法。

Method: 提出三个充分必要条件（动态分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合兼容性），分析它们如何共同导出Bellman方程，并讨论条件不满足时的补救策略（状态增广、回报或动态变形）。进一步揭示三种对偶性（概率-回报、回报-聚合、聚合-概率）来源于同一构造。

Result: Bellman方程的递归性质源于三个条件的互恰性；三个条件分别对应三种对偶性；当条件不满足时，可通过增广状态或变形回报/动态恢复可解性；该框架统一了RL、控制和决策理论中的多种方法。

Conclusion: Bellman方程的形式不是偶然的，而是由动态、回报和不确定性聚合三个条件的一致性推导而来，三种对偶性源于同一构造，为强化学习、控制和决策理论提供了统一视角。

Abstract: What gives the Bellman equation its form? We show that the recursive properties of optimal value functions follow from three conditions: that the dynamics decomposes through sufficient statistics, that the return decomposes recursively, and that the aggregation of uncertainty is compatible with both. When all three conditions hold on a common state, the Bellman equation arises from their mutual consistency; when one fails, tractability can often be recovered by augmenting the state or by deforming return or dynamics. The same conditions are shown to give rise to three dualities: one between probability and return, one between return and aggregation, and one between aggregation and probability. Our framework reveals these dualities as arising from a single construction, unifying methods developed separately across reinforcement learning, control, and decision theory.

</details>


### [242] [SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Eric Schniedermeyer,Artem Burchanow,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectInfer是一个针对边缘设备上LLM推理的神经元级优化框架，通过选择性神经元加载和计算，显著减少内存占用和计算量，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临高计算和内存需求的挑战，现有粗粒度剪枝或量化方法会损害精度或需要重新训练/微调。

Method: 通过离线LLM分析器识别任务特定和通用神经元，实施两种优化：选择性加载（仅加载最重要的神经元子集减少内存）和选择性计算（运行时仅动态计算最相关的神经元）。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估显示，SelectInfer在显著减少内存占用和计算量的同时，保持了任务性能。

Conclusion: SelectInfer是推动LLM在边缘设备上部署的实用步骤，实现了内存与计算效率与任务性能之间的有效平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, but their high computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. Existing approaches to model compression and optimization often rely on coarse-grained pruning or quantization, which can compromise accuracy or require re-training and fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce SelectInfer, a neuron-level optimization framework that enables efficient LLM inference on edge devices through selective neuron loading and computation. By profiling and identifying both task-specific and general-purpose neurons using an offline LLM profiler, SelectInfer implements two key optimizations: selective loading, which reduces memory footprint by selectively loading a subset of neurons that were identified to be most important during the offline stage, and selective computation, which dynamically computes only the most relevant neurons at runtime. Evaluation across multiple datasets shows that SelectInfer achieves significant reductions in memory footprint and computation while preserving task performance, making it a practical step towards enabling LLM deployment on edge devices

</details>


### [243] [Enhancing Rubric-based RL via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18082)
*Mingxuan Xia,Yuhang Yang,Chao Ye,Shuai Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Siqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于评分标准的强化学习中未探索准则（UC）和抑制准则（SC）两大失败模式，本文提出CriPO方法，通过同策略自蒸馏同时解决两者，在医学和科学基准上以约2倍少的优化步骤取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的RL存在两大问题：(1) 未探索准则(UC)因无rollout满足而无法获得优化信号；(2) 抑制准则(SC)虽被部分rollout满足，但标量奖励聚合导致非正优势，学习信号被丢失。此外，已有探索方法引入训练-推理不匹配问题。本文旨在同时解决UC和SC而不引入该不匹配。

Method: 提出CriPO，基于同策略自蒸馏：(1) 针对UC，构建准则注入自教师，用局部前向KL散度将缺失行为注入策略；(2) 针对SC，使用反事实自教师定位负优势rollout中与准则相关的token，将其token级优势翻转为正值，保留有用模式。

Result: 实验表明SC普遍存在：57%以上样本在训练中经历此失败模式，平均每个样本有1.8个SC。在医学和科学基准上，CriPO持续优于基于评分标准的RL基线，且优化步数减少约2倍。

Conclusion: CriPO通过同策略自蒸馏有效解决了UC和SC两大失败模式，避免了训练-推理不匹配，显著提升了基于评分标准强化学习的效率和效果。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL has recently shown promise in improving LLMs on open-ended tasks. A widely recognized limitation of rubric-based RL is limited exploration: criteria that no rollout manages to satisfy (Unexplored Criteria, UC) receive no optimization signal. Recent methods address this by incorporating rubric information as external guidance during rollout, yet they introduce a train-inference mismatch: the policy is optimized on rollouts produced under external guidance while this guidance is absent at inference time, causing error accumulation through autoregressive decoding. Moreover, these exploration-focused approaches overlook a fundamentally different failure mode that we term Suppressed Criteria (SC) -- criteria that are satisfied by some rollouts yet whose learning signals are lost during optimization because scalar reward aggregation assigns them non-positive aggregate advantages. Our analysis reveals that SC are remarkably prevalent: over 57% of samples exhibit this failure mode throughout training, with an average of 1.8 SC per sample. To simultaneously address both UC and SC without introducing training-inference mismatch, we propose Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization (CriPO), which enhances rubric-based RL via on-policy self-distillation. For UC, CriPO constructs a criterion-injection self-teacher and computes a localized forward-KL loss to inject missing behaviors into the policy. For SC, CriPO employs a counterfactual self-teacher to locate criterion-relevant tokens in negative-advantage rollouts and flips their token-level advantages to positive values, preserving useful patterns that would otherwise be suppressed. Experiments on medicine and science benchmarks demonstrate that CriPO consistently outperforms rubric-based RL, achieving stronger final performance with approximately $2\times$ fewer optimization steps.

</details>


### [244] [The Label Complexity of Class-Conditional Coverage under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18088)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移下分类条件覆盖失败的问题，揭示了无标签方法无法同时实现有效且高效的条件覆盖，并量化了实现条件有效性所需的标签数量，通过骨架动作识别和自然图像损坏基准验证了发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准识别系统的评估中训练集和测试集分布不同，导致分裂保形预测的边缘覆盖虽接近名义水平，但每类覆盖显著恶化（如骨架基准中10/60类低于80%覆盖）。作者旨在表征恢复每类有效性的代价并量化所需标签数量。

Method: 理论分析：证明当偏移联合作用于协变量和标签时，目标类条件分数律无法从源标签和无标签目标样本中识别，即无标签方法无法同时有效且高效；推导出有效性所需标签数与效率容忍度平方反比、类别数对数成正比的上下界；在预测驱动推理框架下分析伪标签的收益上限。实验：在跨主体骨架动作识别基准和自然图像损坏基准上进行验证。

Result: 无标签方法无法同时实现有效且高效的条件覆盖；仅需每类少量标签即可恢复条件有效性，但兼顾效率时标签需求随容忍度平方反比增长；伪标签在覆盖崩溃处最多提供小常数因子的效率提升；源标签校准可恢复大部分条件差距，直至边缘覆盖本身崩溃。

Conclusion: 恢复分类条件覆盖在分布偏移下需要目标标签，仅靠无标签数据不可行；源域校准能部分缓解但存在极限；理论界与实验验证一致，表明该发现跨模态成立。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of many recognition systems contains distribution shift by construction, since benchmarks place disjoint conditions in the training and test splits. Under such a shift, split conformal prediction keeps marginal coverage near the nominal level while per-class coverage fails silently: on a real cross-subject skeleton benchmark, marginal coverage stays near ninety percent, the worst action class is covered about seventy percent of the time, and ten of the sixty classes fall below eighty percent coverage.
  We characterize the cost of restoring per-class validity. First, an impossibility: once the shift acts jointly on the covariates and the labels, the target class-conditional score law is unidentified from source labels and an unlabeled target sample, so no label-free method attains per-class coverage that is at once valid and efficient. Second, we make the cost precise: per-class validity alone needs only a handful of target labels per class, while the label count necessary and sufficient for validity together with per-class efficiency grows as the inverse square of the efficiency tolerance and the logarithm of the number of classes, with matching upper and lower bounds. Third, within the evaluated prediction-powered inference family, even the most favorable use of the classifier's own pseudo-labels on an unbounded unlabeled target pool improves efficiency by at most a small constant factor where coverage collapses.
  Skeleton action recognition is our real-data case study. A per-class calibration using source labels alone recovers a substantial share of the per-class gap while the shift preserves marginal coverage, and stops helping exactly when marginal coverage itself breaks. Three real shifts of increasing severity trace this boundary, and the same collapse and recovery appears on a natural-image corruption benchmark, beyond any single modality.

</details>


### [245] [Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections for Parameter-Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18130)
*Valentijn Oldenburg,Floris de Kam,Bente Zuijdam,Lieve Eberson,Nicky van Zutphen,Stef de Wildt,Ivo Verhoeven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流形约束超连接（mHC）作为残差连接的泛化形式，将其作为一种新的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法。实验发现，mHC可微调冻结Transformer，但在微调中将残差混合矩阵固定为单位矩阵通常能提升性能。作为独立PEFT方法，mHC未持续优于LoRA；但在匹配可训练参数预算下，mHC+LoRA组合能改善语言建模损失，并在1B和7B规模上展现任务相关的基准提升。整体结果表明，残差路由是一个有前景的新PEFT维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法主要调整权重或激活，但忽略了Transformer中残差连接这一关键组件。本文旨在探索如何将残差连接泛化为一种新的PEFT方法。

Method: 提出流形约束超连接（mHC），作为残差连接的泛化形式，用于包装冻结的OLMo-2骨干网络，通过学习残差路由模块进行微调。

Result: 1) mHC可微调冻结Transformer，但固定残差混合矩阵为单位矩阵时性能更优；2) 作为独立PEFT方法，mHC未持续优于LoRA；3) 在匹配参数预算下，mHC+LoRA组合改善语言建模损失，并在1B和7B规模上取得任务相关基准增益。

Conclusion: 残差路由是一个区别于现有方法的、有前景的新PEFT方向。

Abstract: Most parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) methods adapt weights or activations, thus leaving one of the key Transformer components unchanged: residual connections. This paper investigates Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), a generalisation of residual connections, as a novel PEFT approach, wrapping frozen OLMo-2 backbones with learned residual routing modules. We find that mHC can finetune frozen Transformers, but that its role differs fundamentally from the original pre-training setting: in finetuning, fixing the residual mixing matrix to identity often improves performance. As a standalone PEFT method, mHC does not consistently outperform LoRA. However, at matched trainable parameter budgets, mHC+LoRA combinations improve language-modelling loss and show task-dependent benchmark gains at both 1B and 7B scale. Overall, our results identify residual routing as a distinct and promising novel PEFT axis.

</details>


### [246] [Totally Positive Matrices and the Highest-Order Coefficients of the Characteristic Polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18148)
*Tiago Closs,Leandro Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通过特征多项式最高阶系数区分全然正矩阵的能力，发现三个最高阶系数 (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) 在维度5、10和30下已能有效分离全然正与非全然正矩阵，且不同结构化全然正族呈现出不同的椭球签名特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全然正矩阵能否通过特征多项式的最高阶系数进行区分，并识别最具信息量的系数组合。

Method: 采用神经网络分类器结合特征归因方法，基于正双对角矩阵乘积、Vandermonde矩阵和Cauchy矩阵等结构化全然正族构建数据集，在三维系数空间中使用马氏椭球进行几何描述。

Result: 系数 (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) 包含强判别信息，能有效分离全然正与非全然正矩阵；不同结构化全然正族呈现不同椭球签名，且签名间分离度随维度增加而增大。

Conclusion: 提出关于结构化全然正族在特征多项式最高阶系数空间中几何分离的猜想。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which totally positive matrices can be distinguished through the highest-order coefficients of their characteristic polynomials. To identify the most informative coefficients, we also employed neural-network classifiers together with feature-attribution methods. Using datasets built from several structured totally positive families, including products of positive bidiagonal matrices, Vandermonde matrices, and Cauchy matrices, we find that the coefficients (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) already contain strong discriminatory information for separating totally positive from non-totally positive matrices in dimensions 5, 10, and 30. The resulting separation is markedly nonlinear and admits a natural geometric description in the corresponding three-dimensional coefficient space by means of Mahalanobis ellipsoids. These ellipsoids enclose the totally positive samples while excluding most non-totally positive ones. Moreover, different structured totally positive families exhibit distinct ellipsoidal signatures, and the separation between these signatures increases with the dimension. These observations lead us to formulate a conjecture on the geometric separation of structured totally positive families in the space determined by the three highest-order characteristic coefficients.

</details>


### [247] [Differentiable Logic Gate Networks for Low-Latency EEG Classification on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18149)
*Shyamal Y. Dharia,Stephen D. Smith,Camilo E. Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了可微分逻辑门网络（Diff-Logic）在边缘设备上进行实时脑电图分类的性能，与同等参数量的MLP和BNN相比，Diff-Logic在痴呆检测任务上F1分数高出6.8%，推理速度提升2.9倍，且模型规模增长时推理时间几乎恒定，适用于资源受限的脑机接口场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络依赖浮点运算，在边缘设备上实现实时EEG分类存在性能瓶颈。作者希望探索一种硬件原生的替代方案，将模型编译为纯布尔电路，通过位运算在CPU上高效执行，以满足便携式BCI设备的延迟和内存约束。

Method: 使用Diff-Logic将神经网络编译为纯布尔逻辑电路，通过位运算执行推理。在4个EEG数据集上、2个分类任务（二分类痴呆检测和三分类情绪识别）中，设置4个复杂度层级（5万-50万参数），与等容量的MLP和BNN进行对比实验。

Result: 痴呆检测任务Diff-Logic达到80.2% Macro F1，超过MLP基线6.8%；情绪识别任务MLP有适度优势但延迟高出2.3倍、模型大小高出14倍。Diff-Logic在模型规模扩大10倍时推理时间几乎不变，最大复杂度层级实现2.9倍速度提升。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑的神经架构是资源受限脑机接口的实用范式，能在满足便携式边缘部署延迟和内存约束的同时，达到具有竞争力甚至更优的分类性能。

Abstract: Real-time EEG classification on edge devices is bottlenecked by the floating-point arithmetic of conventional neural networks. We investigated Differentiable Logic Gate Networks (Diff-Logic) as a hardware-native alternative that compiles models into pure Boolean circuits executable via bitwise CPU operations. Through rigorous iso-parameter experiments across four EEG datasets spanning two classification tasks, binary dementia detection and 3-class emotion recognition, we compared Diff-Logic against matched-capacity Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) and Binarized Neural Network (BNN) baselines at four complexity tiers (50k-500k parameters). On dementia screening, Diff-Logic achieved 80.2% Macro F1, outperforming the MLP baseline by 6.8%. On emotion recognition, the MLP retained a moderate performance advantage but incurred a 2.3$\times$ higher latency and 14$\times$ larger model size when deployed on a power-constrained (7W) Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano CPU (Single-core). Critically, Diff-Logic inference time remained nearly constant across a 10$\times$ increase in model scale, achieving a peak speedup of 2.9$\times$ over MLPs at the largest complexity tier. Our results establish logic-based neural architectures as a practical paradigm for resource-constrained brain-computer interfaces, achieving competitive or superior performance while natively satisfying the latency and memory constraints of portable edge deployment. Code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/Shyamal-Dharia/eeg-difflogic

</details>


### [248] [The Calibration Channel Determines the Bayes-Error Proxy: An Exact Law for Temperature-Induced Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18162)
*Shreyas Pradeepkumar Khandale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）作为一种后验概率校准方法，会系统性地扭曲软标签贝叶斯误差估计器β(z)=E[min(z,1-z)]。作者推导出温度缩放后的代理值与分类器边际分布之间的精确恒等式，证明对于一个固定决策和固定0-1误差的分类器，可以通过调节温度产生任意(0,1/2)区间的代理值。在高斯logits假设下进一步给出闭合形式解，实验表明代理值在恒定测试误差下可变化56倍至980倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ishida等人提出的软标签贝叶斯误差估计器β(z)=E[min(z,1-z)]在估计二分类任务不可约误差时，对概率值的准确性高度敏感。Ushio等人已证明即使完美校准的软标签也可能产生严重偏差，并提出等渗校准作为修复方案。本文旨在深入理解最广泛使用的后验校准方法——温度缩放——如何系统性地扭曲这一代理估计量，揭示其数学本质。

Method: 1) 理论推导：证明温度缩放后的代理值与分类器边际分布之间的精确恒等式，建立严格单调性和连续双射关系；2) 在高斯logits模型假设下推导代理值-温度曲线的双参数闭合形式；3) 在CIFAR-10、Fashion-MNIST、SVHN等数据集上的8个二分类任务进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 温度缩放后的代理值与温度严格单调，且从温度轴到(0,1/2)区间存在连续双射；2) 固定分类器可通过调整温度报告任意代理值；3) 恒定测试误差下代理值变化范围达56倍至980倍；4) 闭合形式解与实验曲线误差在0.018以内；5) 最小化期望校准误差的最优温度与任何稳定的代理值不一致。

Conclusion: 温度缩放会系统性且可预测地扭曲贝叶斯误差代理估计量，其对代理值的调节能力远超实际分类性能的变化。代理值必须与其生成概率的机制一同解读才有意义。这一发现强化了校准驱动修复方法存在的必要性，并为实践者提供了重要的警示。

Abstract: The soft-label Bayes-error estimator beta(z) = E[min(z, 1-z)] of Ishida et al. estimates the irreducible error of a binary task directly from probability-valued labels. Recent work by Ushio et al. showed that this estimator is fragile when the probabilities are not the true posterior: even perfectly calibrated soft labels can yield a substantially inaccurate estimate, and they propose isotonic calibration as a consistent remedy. We complement that line of work by characterizing exactly how the most widely used post-hoc calibration map -- temperature scaling -- distorts the proxy. We prove an exact, model-free identity reducing the temperature-scaled proxy to the classifier's margin distribution, from which we obtain (i) strict monotonicity in the temperature and (ii) a continuous bijection from the temperature axis onto the open interval (0, 1/2), so that a fixed classifier -- with fixed decisions and fixed 0-1 error -- can be made to report any proxy value whatsoever. Under a Gaussian model of the logits we further derive a two-parameter closed form for the entire proxy-versus-temperature curve. Across CIFAR-10, Fashion-MNIST, and SVHN (eight binary tasks), the proxy varies by 56x to 980x at constant test error, the closed form reproduces the empirical curve to within 0.018, and the calibration temperature that minimizes the expected calibration error does not coincide with any stable proxy value. Our results give a precise, predictive account of the distortion whose existence motivates calibration-based remedies, and they reinforce the practical recommendation that a proxy value is meaningful only together with the mechanism that produced its probabilities.

</details>


### [249] [OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163)
*Chinmay Rane,Kanishka Tyagi,Michael Manry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究输出重置（OR）这一平滑单侧饱和规则，作为PPO和GRPO中剪辑代理目标的替代方案，用于大语言模型后训练。实验表明，在GAE下PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均奖励分数高0.305，但在组相对优势下GRPO-OR未获得更高平均分数，仅展现出更小的跨种子离散度、近零的终端OR残差和下降的过冲比例。两种组相对方法均表现出比GAE方法更大的位移，OR未能一致减小该位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO和GRPO中使用的剪辑代理目标在有利方向饱和时会在标量目标导数中引入突变。作者探究输出重置（OR）作为一种平滑的单侧饱和规则，能否为大语言模型后训练提供有用的替代方案。

Method: 将PPO和GRPO中的剪辑策略项替换为OR平方边际损失，在rollout相对token对数比率空间中使用；优势符号决定更新方向，token在越过有利边际后贡献零直接OR残差。使用Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct在Anthropic hh-rlhf数据集上，采用一个共享奖励模型和每个方法三个种子进行比较。

Result: 在GAE下，PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均训练奖励模型得分高0.305，但跨种子离散度更大。在组相对优势下，GRPO-OR未获得更高平均分，但展现出更小的跨种子离散度、近零终端OR残差和下降的过冲比例。两种组相对方法均比GAE方法表现出更大的log-ratio位移，OR未能一致减小该位移。

Conclusion: OR在两种对比中均改变了优化行为，但观测到的奖励效果不同。在G=2时，GRPO-OR的诊断结果未转化为奖励分数增益。更大组的设置是否改变这一结果尚待研究。报告分数为训练时奖励模型测量值，非保留的人类偏好性能。

Abstract: PPO and the GRPO baseline studied here use clipped surrogate objectives whose favorable-direction saturation introduces an abrupt change in the scalar objective's derivative. We ask whether Output Reset (OR), a smooth one-sided saturation rule, offers a useful alternative for large language model post-training. PPO-OR and GRPO-OR replace the clipped policy term with an OR squared-margin loss in rollout-relative token log-ratio space; the advantage sign determines the update direction, and a token contributes zero direct OR residual after crossing the favorable margin. We compare PPO-clip with PPO-OR under generalized advantage estimation (GAE), and GRPO with GRPO-OR under group-relative advantages, using \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} on Anthropic \texttt{hh-rlhf} with one shared reward model and three seeds per method. Under GAE, PPO-OR has a mean final training-time reward-model score $0.305$ higher than PPO-clip, with a larger observed across-seed spread. Under group-relative advantages, GRPO-OR does not have a higher mean score, but shows a smaller observed spread, a near-zero terminal OR residual, and a declining overshoot fraction, while the matched GRPO clipped-objective trace remains variable. Both group-relative methods exhibit substantially larger rollout-to-current log-ratio displacement than the GAE methods, and OR does not consistently reduce it. Thus, OR changes optimization behavior in both matched comparisons, but the observed reward effect differs between them. At $G=2$, the GRPO-OR diagnostics do not translate into a reward-score gain. Whether larger groups change this outcome remains open. The reported scores are training-time reward-model measurements, not held-out human-preference performance.

</details>


### [250] [A Continual Validation, Updating, and Decision-Making Framework for Self-Adaptive Digital Twins via Robust Model Predictive Control: A Case Study in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18164)
*Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Vispi Karkaria,Seul Lee,Daniel Apley,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应数字孪生框架，集成Fisher分数漂移检测、LoRA高效微调和Mann-Whitney U统计验证，在随机线性系统和定向能量沉积增材制造案例中，成功检测并恢复概念漂移下的预测精度与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生依赖的代理模型在运行条件变化时会遭遇概念漂移，导致保真度下降。现有自适应框架缺乏基于原理的漂移检测机制、从有限流数据高效更新的能力，以及更新后性能改进的统计认证方法。

Method: ① 基于Fisher分数的多变量漂移检测器监控代理模型置信度；② 检测到漂移后激活LoRA，只更新不到1%的模型参数进行持续学习；③ 采用Mann-Whitney U检验在线统计验证预测性能是否真正提升，确认后方部署更新模型。

Result: 在随机线性系统和定向能量沉积增材制造两个案例中，框架能短延迟检测分布偏移，并在突变和渐变漂移下恢复预测精度及不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了统计严谨且计算高效的方法，用于维护基于神经网络的数字孪生在整个运行生命周期中的可信度。

Abstract: Digital Twins rely on surrogate models to mirror physical systems in real time, yet these models can degrade as operating conditions evolve, a phenomenon known as concept drift. Maintaining surrogate fidelity under drift, particularly when models must also capture aleatoric uncertainty, remains an open challenge. Existing adaptive frameworks lack principled mechanisms for detecting when updates are needed, for efficiently adapting models from limited streaming data, and for certifying that updates genuinely improve predictive performance. Here we present an adaptive Digital Twin framework that integrates a Fisher score--based multivariate drift detector, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient continual learning, and a Mann--Whitney $U$ test for online statistical validation. The framework monitors surrogate-model confidence via Fisher score vectors, triggers targeted fine-tuning of fewer than 1% of model parameters upon drift detection, and statistically certifies predictive improvement before deploying the updated surrogate. Applied to a stochastic linear system and a directed energy deposition additive manufacturing process as case studies, the framework successfully detects distributional shifts with short delays and restores both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification under abrupt and incremental drift. These results establish a statistically rigorous and computationally tractable pathway for sustaining the trustworthiness of neural-network--based Digital Twins throughout their operational life cycle.

</details>


### [251] [FlashRT: Agent Harness for Guiding Agents to Deploy Real-Time Multimodal Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18171)
*Krish Agarwal,Zhuoming Chen,Yanyuan Qin,Zhenyu Gu,Atri Rudra,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashRT 是一个智能体框架，通过链式编程范式（Chain-of-Program）将开发者编写的参考实现自动转化为优化的多 GPU 部署，在 NVIDIA B200 上实现最高 70 倍延迟降低和 2.8 倍吞吐提升，在 AMD MI355X 上实现 3.6 倍吞吐提升，并在 Qwen3-Omni 文本转音频推理中相较专家级 vLLM-Omni 实现降低 65% 响应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态应用（如语音智能体、交互式视频生成）由异构模型组成的流水线构成，其高效部署需要针对具体应用做出放置、流式传输和模型内并行的优化决策。现有服务系统和自动并行编译器仅支持有限的变换和固定的工作负载假设，导致新应用的高性能实现仍需人工手工定制。

Method: FlashRT 采用链式编程范式（Chain-of-Program），通过多轮变换过程：1）将参考实现转化为中间表示（IR）以捕获数据依赖和持久状态作用域；2）通过顺序解释器验证 IR 正确性；3）进行静态分析识别候选变换；4）在测量门控的优化循环中迭代实现、验证和基准测试每个候选变换，最终生成适应不同硬件预算的高效部署。

Result: 在视频世界模型和多模态大语言模型等多种应用中，FlashRT 将参考实现转化为高效部署：在 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上实现最高 70 倍延迟降低和 2.8 倍吞吐提升；在 AMD MI355X GPU 上将峰值吞吐提升进一步扩大至 3.6 倍；在 Qwen3-Omni 文本转音频推理中，相较专家级 vLLM-Omni 实现，响应延迟降低 65%。

Conclusion: FlashRT 证明了智能体驱动的优化在专家优化不够成熟的平台上更具可扩展性，能够自动将开发者编写的简单参考实现转化为高度优化的多 GPU 部署，灵活权衡延迟和吞吐等目标指标。

Abstract: Real-time multimodal applications, including voice agents and interactive video generation, compose heterogeneous models into pipelines whose efficient deployment requires application-specific decisions about placement, streaming, and intra-model parallelism. Existing serving systems and auto-parallelism compilers commit to limited transformations and fixed workload assumptions, so achieving high performance on a new application requires hand-crafting an efficient implementation. We present FlashRT, an agent harness that guides coding agents to lift simple developer-written reference implementations into optimized multi-GPU deployments that flexibly weigh target metrics like latency and throughput. Using a new chain-of-program paradigm, FlashRT directs a generic coding agent through a multi-pass transformation process where an agent transforms the reference into an intermediate representation (IR) to capture data dependencies and persistent-state scopes, validates this IR via a sequential interpreter, and performs static analyses to identify candidate transformations. Then, the agent iteratively implements, verifies, and benchmarks each candidate under a measurement-gated optimization loop to produce effective deployments that span different hardware budgets. Across various applications, including video world models and multimodal LLMs, FlashRT converts reference implementations into highly efficient deployments, delivering up to ~70x latency reduction and 2.8x throughput improvement on NVIDIA B200 GPUs. On AMD MI355X GPUs, FlashRT matches the peak latency reduction while increasing peak throughput improvement to 3.6x, demonstrating that agent-driven optimization can be more scalable on platforms with less mature expert optimization. In fact, for Qwen3-Omni text-to-audio inference, FlashRT reduces response latency by 65% compared to the expert vLLM-Omni implementation on AMD MI355X.

</details>


### [252] [Three-Body Scattering for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18198)
*Peng Sun,Zhenglin Cheng,Deyuan Liu,Jun Xie,Xinyi Shang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三体散射建模（TBSM），通过分布能诱导样本级运动并提供直接回归监督，实现一步生成器训练。在ImageNet-256上，像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型依赖对抗性判别器、预设噪声到数据路径或自回归分解。本文旨在探索能否通过适当的分布能（distributional energy）直接为一步生成器提供样本级运动引导和回归监督，避免现有方法的复杂架构或全配对计算开销。

Method: TBSM将能量距离转化为每个抛射体（projectile）的常量大小相互作用：每个抛射体被吸引向一个真实源，同时被一个独立生成的源排斥。在给定抛射体及其条件后，其期望等于1/2 D_E^2(P_θ,Q)的2-Wasserstein梯度流速度。利用冻结图像特征中的散射，在ImageNet-256上训练一步生成器。

Result: 像素空间PixelDiT-XL在ImageNet-256上FID=2.23；潜空间DiT-XL在ImageNet-256上FID=1.63，NFE=1。

Conclusion: TBSM建立了一种无需全配对计算的高维一步生成方法，提供了扩散相关监督、Drift类动力学和GAN类目标之间的设计图谱，验证了跟踪散射作为高维一步生成路径的有效性。

Abstract: Modern generative models typically rely on an adversarial critic, a prescribed noise-to-data path, or an autoregressive factorization. Instead, we show that a proper distributional energy can induce sample-level motion and provide direct regression supervision for a one-step generator. Three-Body Scattering Modeling (TBSM) for generation turns the energy distance into a constant-size per-projectile interaction: each projectile is attracted toward one real source and repelled from one independently generated source. Conditioned on the projectile and its condition, its expectation equals the $2$-Wasserstein gradient-flow velocity of $\frac12D_E^2(P_θ,Q)$. A batch of $B$ frozen-target events yields $O(B)$ sample-level losses, each using one reference for its condition instead of the minibatch-wide all-pairs field used by methods such as Drifting Models. Tracking this conditional expectation online can reduce field noise. Using scattering in frozen image features, TBSM trains one-step generators on ImageNet-256, achieving FID${}=2.23$ with pixel-space PixelDiT-XL and FID${}=1.63$ with latent-space DiT-XL at NFE${}=1$. We provide a design map relating diffusion-related supervision, Drift-like dynamics, and GAN-like objectives. These results establish tracked scattering as a route to high-dimensional one-step generation. Code: https://github.com/sp12138/TBSM.

</details>


### [253] [Causal Discovery on Irregular Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18226)
*Martim Penim,Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro Bizarro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的PCMCI+方法，通过聚合预定义时间窗口内的因果影响来处理不规则时间序列数据，在合成数据上显著优于标准PCMCI+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如PCMCI+）依赖于规则离散的滞后结构，仅适用于规则采样数据，而现实世界中的传感器流、医疗数据和金融交易等场景常涉及不规则采样的事件流，亟需能处理不规则时间序列的因果发现方法。

Method: 将标准PCMCI+扩展，不再通过固定滞后依赖建模因果关系，而是通过聚合预定义时间窗口内的因果影响来处理不规则时间序列。

Result: 在具有已知因果结构的合成不规则事件流上，在不同信噪比条件下，该方法能一致地恢复潜在因果图，且显著优于标准PCMCI+在不规则采样数据上的表现。

Conclusion: 提出的扩展方法有效解决了不规则时间序列的因果发现问题，为处理不规则采样数据提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods have shown strong performance in temporal systems, but they typically rely on regular and discrete lag structures, limiting their applicability to regularly sampled data. However, many real-world tasks require dealing with irregularly sampled streams of events, such as sensor streams, healthcare data, and financial transactions. In this work, we propose an extension of PCMCI+, a state-of-the-art method for causal discovery on regular multivariate time series, to allow for handling irregular time series. Instead of modelling causal relations through fixed-lag dependencies, our method aggregates causal influence over predefined temporal windows. We evaluate our method on synthetic irregular event streams with known causal structures under different signal-to-noise ratios, showing that it consistently recovers the underlying causal graph and substantially outperforms the standard PCMCI+ on irregularly sampled data.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [SALT: Salience-Aware Lexical Trie for Long-Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17486)
*Oteo Mamo,Hyunjin Yi,Joydhriti Choudhury,Shangqian Gao,Weikuan Yu*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: SALT是一种与模型无关的抽取式框架，通过构建基于句子频率的trie结构来组织文档主题，实现预算在多个主题间的合理分配，避免主题坍塌，从而在压缩长上下文提示词时保持主题覆盖率，降低预填充计算和KV-cache内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输入级提示词压缩方法使用标量相关性分数对每个句子单独评分，将文档视为无结构的词句池，在紧缩预算下导致主题坍塌——文档中的主导主题吞噬预算，丢弃了频率较低但与任务相关的主题。

Method: 提出SALT框架：1) 将每个句子的关键词组织成基于句子频率（SF）排序的trie结构；2) trie结构平滑内存分配，防止主导主题垄断预算；3) 多锚点检索可激活任意深度的查询关键词标记的trie节点；4) trie在对话轮次间持久化，支持多轮使用而无需重新编码文档。

Result: SALT减少了长上下文提示词的预填充计算和内存成本，同时保持文档主题覆盖率，并且可与针对解码时延和内存的KV-cache方法组合使用。

Conclusion: 通过基于主题结构的预算分配策略，SALT在压缩长上下文时有效解决了主题坍塌问题，是一种与模型无关、可组合使用的轻量级抽取式压缩方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) process increasingly longer prompts, computation and KV-cache memory costs have emerged as major bottlenecks in inference systems. Existing input-level prompt compression methods address this, but rank each sentence by a scalar relevance score, treating the document as an unstructured pool of words and sentences. Under tight budgets, this causes theme collapse, where the dominant theme(s) of a document consumes the budget, discarding less-frequent yet task-relevant themes. Preserving thematic coverage instead requires allocating the budget across recurring themes rather than scoring sentences in isolation. To this end, we propose SALT, a model-agnostic extractive framework that organizes per-sentence keywords into a trie ordered by sentence frequency (SF), a lightweight, reusable proxy for document thematic structure. This trie-based organization smooths memory allocation and prevents dominant themes from monopolizing the budget. Multi-anchor retrieval activates trie nodes labeled by query keywords at any depth, and the trie persists across dialogue turns, supporting multi-turn use without re-encoding the document. By preserving document themes, SALT reduces the prefill computation and memory cost of long-context prompts while remaining composable with KV-cache methods that target decoding-time latency and memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [AirMoE: Statistic-Augmented Over-the-Air MoE for Collaborative Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16562)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Jingreng Lei,Guangxu Zhu,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种统计增强的空中混合专家模型（AirMoE）范式，通过特征检索库减少上行传输开销，并利用多址信道物理聚合专家输出，以解决无线云边网络中MoE部署的路由拥塞和聚合噪声两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线云边网络中部署MoE时，存在两个耦合瓶颈：1）路由激活客户端需要传输原始特征，导致带宽受限的上行链路过载；2）通过无线链路聚合激活专家输出受信道噪声和可扩展性差的影响。

Method: 提出AirMoE范式：路由侧，客户端通过本地特征检索库（FRL）用云广播的紧凑查询检索原型诱导统计量，数字化上报给云端，云端通过JS散度对齐统计量与LM特征选择最相关客户端；聚合侧，选中专家同时通过多址信道传输输出，利用波形叠加物理计算加权和，通过信道感知功率控制实现加权系数。

Result: 在语义分割任务上，AirMoE优于MoE基线和单模型竞争者。消融实验进一步验证了各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: AirMoE通过算法和物理层面的解耦，有效解决了无线MoE部署中的路由和聚合瓶颈，具有理论收敛性和迭代复杂度保证。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) are increasingly deployed over wireless cloud-edge networks, as a single edge device lacks sufficient resources to host large-scale models locally. In this distributed architecture, a cloud-hosted pretrained Large Model (LM) acts as a shared backbone for latent feature extraction, while heterogeneous experts deployed across distributed, wirelessly-connected clients collaboratively form the task head. However, deploying MoE over wireless links exposes two coupled bottlenecks. On the one hand, routing which clients to activate generally overloads bandwidth-limited uplinks due to required raw feature transmission. On the other hand, aggregating the activated experts' outputs over wireless links is hindered by channel noise and poor scalability. To break these bottlenecks, we propose a statistic-augmented over-the-air MoE (AirMoE) paradigm. Specifically, on the routing side, each client queries its local Feature Retrieval Library (FRL) with a cloud-broadcast compact query, retrieves a prototype-induced statistic, and reports it digitally to the cloud, drastically reducing uplink traffic; the cloud then selects the most relevant clients by aligning these statistics with the LM-extracted features via Jensen--Shannon (JS) divergence. On the aggregating side, selected experts simultaneously transmit their outputs over the multiple-access channel, which physically computes the reweighted sum via waveform superposition, with reweighting coefficients realized through channel-aware power control. The two mechanisms are thus decoupled both algorithmically and physically. We further provide theoretical analyses on convergence and iteration complexity. Taking semantic segmentation task as an example, extensive experiments demonstrate that AirMoE outperforms MoE baselines and single-model competitors. Ablations further confirm the effectiveness of each incorporated component.

</details>


### [256] [Wait-Free Locks Should Not Fear Later Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16571)
*Tong Che*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SeniorLock锁和SeniorObj通用构造，实现了"回顾性无等待"保证：调用仅需对其取票时已激活的请求负责，无需处理后续到达的请求，时间复杂度为O((β+1)(T+1))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"帮助"机制虽能实现无等待性，但可能导致调用被迫不断帮助后来的请求而永远无法返回。作者希望设计一种机制，使调用只承担其取票时已存在的请求的开销。

Method: 提出SeniorLock，一种确定性的可帮助thunk锁，利用票据时间优先级机制；以及SeniorObj通用构造，将确定性顺序对象转换为回顾性无等待对象。

Result: SeniorLock中，具有票据时间优先级β的调用在O((β+1)(T+1))步内完成；SeniorObj无需复制对象表示，且无高级调用活跃时操作成本接近原生成本。

Conclusion: 实现了"回顾性无等待"这一新保证，为并发锁和通用构造提供了更好的性能隔离保障。

Abstract: Helping seems to make a lock wait-free: wrap the critical section in an idempotent thunk that any process can finish once the holder stalls. Yet helping protects the system, not the call. An overwritable candidate lets later requests bump one another in sequence, so a call can be forced to help newcomer after newcomer and never return, while point contention never exceeds two.
  We ask whether a call can instead be charged only for the requests active when it takes its ticket, never for what arrives afterward. We show that the answer is yes. SeniorLock is a deterministic helpable thunk lock in which a call with ticket-time seniority $β$ finishes in $O((β+1)(T+1))$ local shared-memory steps, where $T$ bounds one thunk's cost, independently of later invocations. We call this guarantee \emph{retrospective wait-freedom}.
  The same lock doubles as a universal construction we call SeniorObj: it turns any deterministic sequential object whose operations are bounded, concurrently idempotent thunks into a retrospective wait-free one, with no copying of its representation and, when no senior is active, at essentially the native cost of the operation.

</details>


### [257] [Hardware-Transparent I/O Governance in Disaggregated Heterogeneous Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16578)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Sue K. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了I/O资源管理器（IORM），一种多阶段分布式调度器，用于解决共享无状态分解存储集群中的异构硬件性能一致性和全局I/O限流问题，已在Oracle Exadata Exascale生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度器无法解决两个治理问题：1) 跨异构硬件代际保持一致的性能；2) 当访问模式倾斜到部分存储节点时强制执行全局I/O限制。

Method: IORM结合三种机制：1) 硬件感知成本建模器，利用数据表固定成本归一化I/O记账；2) 基于量子的速率限制器，带有限额结转信用以容纳数据库微突发；3) 分布式自适应反馈控制器，重新分配未使用的配额以解决拓扑访问倾斜。

Result: 在8节点测试集群上运行100个并发租户卷时，IORM在极端顺序倾斜下收敛到配置限制的5%以内，无租户间干扰，存储节点故障后15秒内恢复全部吞吐量。

Conclusion: IORM有效解决了分解存储集群中的性能隔离和全局限流问题，并提供了生产部署的操作经验。

Abstract: Shared-nothing disaggregated storage clusters that serve both latency-sensitive databases and opaque block-volume workloads face two governance problems unsolved by existing schedulers: maintaining consistent performance across heterogeneous hardware generations, and enforcing global I/O limits when access patterns skew to a subset of storage nodes. We present the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a multi-stage distributed scheduler deployed in production within Oracle Exadata Exascale. IORM combines three mechanisms: a hardware-aware cost modeler that normalizes I/O accounting using datasheet-derived fixed costs to make limits invariant across hardware generations; a quantum-based rate limiter with bounded carry-forward credits that accommodates database micro-bursts while enforcing long-term SLOs; and a distributed adaptive feedback controller that redistributes unused entitlements across the cluster to resolve topological access skew. Beyond design, we share operational lessons from production deployment. On an 8-node test cluster running up to 100 concurrent tenant volumes, IORM converges within 5\% of provisioned limits under extreme sequential skew, scales without inter-tenant interference, and recovers full throughput within 15 seconds of a storage-node failure.

</details>


### [258] [Cold-Start Model Delivery in Kubernetes Inference Serving: An Empirical Study of OCI-Based Distribution and Its Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16596)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了Kubernetes上模型服务Pod的冷启动延迟瓶颈——模型权重加载，发现当前主要依赖从对象存储下载，缺乏容器镜像的缓存、摘要寻址和验证机制。作者在KServe平台实现了两种新的OCI交付路径（oci+native://和oci+fetch://），并进行了首个受控对比实验。结果表明，节点缓存的OCI交付使热副本添加与模型大小无关（70B模型11.7秒 vs. 对象存储重新下载40.7分钟，差异208倍），冷拉取成本仅为普通下载的2倍（瓶颈在containerd双写）。此外，针对无准入验证的模型URI，提出了服务时完整性设计方案（摘要锁定+OpenSSF签名验证），流式哈希验证开销低于0.1%，而事后验证高达53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务Pod在Kubernetes上的启动延迟主要由模型权重加载步骤主导。随着大语言模型权重达数百GB，冷启动交付时间成为制约自动扩缩容和scale-to-zero经济性的关键因素。然而，当前主流机制仍是临时从对象存储下载模型，缺乏Kubernetes为容器镜像提供的拉取缓存、摘要寻址和验证支持，亟需系统化分析和优化。

Method: 首先从两个轴（哪个组件拉取工件，以及准入验证器能否将部署引用绑定到到达的字节）分析Kubernetes服务平台的交付路径。然后在KServe平台上实现两种新交付路径：oci+native://（将模型镜像挂载为Kubernetes镜像卷，KEP-4639，已合并上游）和oci+fetch://（在存储初始化器中拉取OCI工件，审查中）。对1B、7B、70B类模型（2-140 GB）的fp16权重进行首个受控对比实验，比较modelcar sidecar、原生镜像卷和对象存储下载三种路径的性能。

Result: 节点缓存的OCI交付使热副本添加与模型大小无关：70B类工件仅需11.7秒，而对象存储重新下载需40.7分钟，差异达208倍。首次冷拉取成本最高为普通下载的2倍，瓶颈在containerd的blob写入后解包双遍操作。流式哈希验证在下载期间开销低于0.1%，而事后验证增加高达53%的交付时间。

Conclusion: OCI交付路径在Kubernetes模型服务中具有显著性能优势，尤其节点缓存使热扩缩容不再受模型大小限制。针对s3://、gs://、hf://等缺乏准入验证的模型URI，提出摘要锁定和OpenSSF模型签名验证的服务时完整性设计方案，其中流式验证几乎零开销，优于事后验证。

Abstract: The startup latency of a model-serving pod on Kubernetes is dominated by one step: delivering the model weights. As models reach the hundred-gigabyte weights of large language models, cold-start delivery time governs the economics of autoscaling and scale-to-zero, yet the dominant mechanisms remain ad-hoc downloads from object storage, with none of the pull caching, digest addressing, or verification Kubernetes provides for container images. We analyze the delivery paths available to a Kubernetes serving platform along two axes: which component pulls the artifact, and whether any admission-time verifier can bind the deployed reference to the arriving bytes. We validate the analysis upstream in KServe, a widely deployed CNCF model-serving platform, by implementing two new delivery paths: oci+native://, which mounts model images as Kubernetes image volumes (KEP-4639), merged upstream, and oci+fetch://, which pulls OCI artifacts inside the storage initializer, under review. We report, to our knowledge, the first controlled comparison of model delivery paths in a Kubernetes serving platform (modelcar sidecars, native image volumes, object-storage download) on artifacts sized to fp16 weights of 1B-, 7B-, and 70B-class models (2-140 GB). Node-cached OCI delivery makes warm replica addition size-independent: 11.7 s for a 70B-class artifact versus 40.7 minutes of re-download over object storage, a 208x difference, while the first cold pull costs up to 2x a plain download, localized to containerd's blob-write-then-unpack double pass. For models on s3://, gs://, or hf:// URIs, where no admission-time verifier observes the bytes, we present a serving-time integrity design proposed to the KServe community: digest pinning and OpenSSF model-signing enforcement in the storage initializer. Streaming hash verification during download adds under 0.1% to delivery time; a post-download pass adds up to 53%.

</details>


### [259] [Roomie: Interference-Aware Colocation for Efficient Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16784)
*Youssouph Faye,Francescomaria Faticanti,Shubham Jain,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Roomie是一种模型服务编排架构，通过基于占用的分析模型和贪心启发式算法预测并避免GPU上DNN推理的内核级干扰，在云和边缘场景下将SLO违规减少高达3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DNN推理需求增长，GPU容量过度订阅，迫使多个模型共存于同一设备。现有系统要么忽略内核级干扰，要么使用无法捕获时间动态的聚合资源画像，导致SLO违反频繁发生。

Method: 离线内核配置提取与在线干扰预测解耦；基于占用的分析模型（避免画像引起的时间失真）；成对贪心启发式将多模型干扰计算从指数时间降至多项式时间；在线放置算法为每个传入模型分配最小化预测延迟的GPU。

Result: 相比现有方案，SLO违规（推理延迟）降低高达3倍，同时保持可比甚至更优的有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: Roomie成功通过内核级干扰预测与高效放置策略，在云和边缘GPU部署中实现了更好的模型共存性能。

Abstract: As demand for DNN inference grows, GPU capacity is increasingly oversubscribed, forcing operators to colocate multiple models on the same device in both cloud and edge deployments. Whether colocation succeeds or violates SLOs depends on the temporal overlap of kernels from concurrently executing models -- an effect that existing serving systems either ignore or approximate using aggregate resource profiles that fail to capture temporal dynamics. This paper presents Roomie, a model serving orchestration architecture that predicts and avoids kernel-level interference between colocated DNNs. Roomie decouples offline kernel profiling from online interference prediction. It uses profiling only to extract per-kernel resource configurations, and predicts interference with an occupancy-based analytical model immune to profiler-induced timing distortion. A pairwise greedy heuristic then approximates multi-model interference in polynomial rather than exponential time, and an online placement algorithm then uses these estimates to assign each incoming model to the GPU that minimizes predicted slowdown. Our experimental evaluation compares Roomie against state-of-the-art solutions across both cloud-grade server clusters and embedded edge devices, demonstrating that Roomie reduces SLO violations (i.e., inference latency) by up to 3x, while maintaining comparable, and in many cases superior, goodput relative to existing approaches.

</details>


### [260] [Technical Report: AI-Assisted Gated DeltaNet Optimization on NVIDIA Blackwell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16831)
*Hyunjun Shin,Jiseung Jang,Jaewoo Maeng,Hyunjun Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过MSInfer团队在MLSys 2026 FlashInfer竞赛中的优化实践，论证了AI辅助GPU编程不仅是内核生成循环，而是涵盖算法设计、负载特化、测量工具、构建评估、评审对齐和人工解释的全栈系统工程问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助GPU编程常被简化为"生成-基准测试-重复"的内核生成循环，但竞赛级优化需要超越内核体改进，涉及更广泛的系统层面问题，现有研究对此认识不足。

Method: 以MSInfer团队在FlashInfer竞赛中优化Gated DeltaNet的实战案例为基础，系统分析优化过程中的各个环节，揭示端到端系统问题的关键作用。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200/Blackwell上实现官方1.58倍加速，decode平均延迟9.315μs，prefill平均延迟239.48μs。

Conclusion: 有效的局部内核改进在需要结构重组和评审对齐的负载中会遭遇瓶颈，AI辅助内核优化应被视为涵盖算法设计、负载特化、测量工具、构建评估、评审对齐和人工解释的全栈系统工程。

Abstract: AI-assisted GPU programming is often framed as a kernel-generation loop: ask a model to produce faster CUDA code, benchmark the result, and repeat. This case study argues that contest-grade optimization involves more than improving the kernel body. We examine the Agent-Assisted submission by our team, MSInfer, to the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer Contest. The submission optimized Gated DeltaNet decode and prefill on NVIDIA B200/Blackwell and achieved an official $1.58\times$ speedup, with approximate average latencies of $9.315\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for decode and $239.48\,μ\mathrm{s}$ for prefill. Our experience shows that even effective local kernel improvements can plateau when a workload requires structural reformulation and evaluator-aligned measurement. We therefore characterize AI-assisted kernel optimization as an end-to-end systems problem that encompasses algorithm design, workload specialization, measurement tooling, build and evaluation surfaces, evaluator alignment, and human interpretation.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Storage: State as a Runtime Control Problem in Parallel and Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16836)
*Shuhao Zhang,Haoran Peng,Chun Ho Ma,Yancan Mao,Yufeng Du,Shifeng Liu,Ruijie Qiu,Xiaofei Liao,Hai Jin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文围绕共享状态管理，从状态访问与调度、状态感知执行、状态演化与复用三个维度系统梳理了流处理、服务、检索和持续学习系统中的相关研究，并提出了一种以契约为导向的设计蓝图和干扰感知评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在访问控制、硬件感知执行、内存管理和长时更新等方面相互孤立，缺乏统一的跨领域视角来理解共享状态如何影响系统性能与故障行为。

Method: 通过三个耦合维度（状态访问与调度、状态感知执行、状态演化与复用）组织文献，并以五种共同要素（状态对象、控制面、耦合路径、评估边界、未解决契约）作为分析支架进行比较综合。

Result: 识别出状态管理中反复出现的反模式，并提出了一个契约为导向的蓝图和干扰感知的评估议程。

Conclusion: 状态管理本质上是一个运行时控制问题，需要在统一的契约框架下进行系统化的设计、评估与优化。

Abstract: Shared state increasingly shapes both performance and failure behavior in streaming, serving, retrieval, and continual-learning systems. Existing studies, however, often isolate access control, hardware-aware execution, memory management, and long-horizon updates. The review organizes this literature around three coupled dimensions: state access and scheduling, state-aware execution, and state evolution and reuse. Across these dimensions, the literature is synthesized through a common scaffold: state object, control surface, coupling path, evaluation boundary, and unresolved contract. This comparison identifies recurring anti-patterns and informs a contract-oriented blueprint and disturbance-aware evaluation agenda. Taken together, the evidence characterizes state management as a runtime control problem.

</details>


### [262] [EdgeCoInfer: Hierarchical Collaborative Inference for On-Device Multimodal Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17143)
*Lin Tan,David K. Y. Yau,Songtao Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EdgeCoInfer通过粒度自适应部署、模块共享与细粒度分区，结合混合进化分层强化学习（HE-HRL）解决MINLP问题，在高并发边缘环境下实现100%任务完成率、76%系统成本降低和71.88%内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代移动应用需要并发执行多模态大模型（MLLMs）提供普适智能，但边缘环境面临多任务并发与严格硬约束耦合的挑战，现有方法难以同时满足这些约束。

Method: 提出EdgeCoInfer框架，采用模型间功能模块共享与模型内细粒度分区实现粒度自适应部署；将问题建模为混合整数非线性规划（MINLP），提出混合进化分层强化学习（HE-HRL）范式，其中遗传算法（GA）负责离散模型放置，SAC智能体负责连续资源分配；引入可行性引导的构造执行机制，包括构造性切割步解码器、预激活剪枝和两阶段课程策略。

Result: 在高并发场景下实现100%任务完成率，相比基线方法降低76%系统成本，节省71.88%内存。

Conclusion: EdgeCoInfer通过协同优化模型共享与细粒度分区，有效解决了边缘环境中多MLLM并发部署的硬约束问题，实现了高效且稳定的任务执行。

Abstract: Modern mobile applications predominantly execute concurrent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to provide ubiquitous intelligence. However, satisfying this demand within edge environments faces significant challenges due to multi-task concurrency and strictly coupled hard constraints. To address these issues, we propose EdgeCoInfer, a framework enabling granularity-adaptive deployment by co-optimizing inter-model functional module sharing and \textbf{intra-model fine-grained partitioning}. We solve the underlying Mixed-Integer Non-Linear Programming (MINLP) problem via a Hybrid Evolutionary Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HE-HRL) paradigm, which synchronizes a Genetic Algorithm (GA) for discrete model placement with a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent for continuous resource allocation. To navigate the sparse feasible region, we introduce a feasibility-guided constructive execution mechanism, integrating a constructive cut-step decoder with pre-act pruning and a two-phase curriculum strategy for stable adaptation. Experimental results demonstrate that EdgeCoInfer ensures a 100\% task completion rate in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving a 76\% reduction in system cost and 71.88\% memory savings compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [263] [LMEdge: QoS-Aware LLM Inference Orchestration on Edge Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17175)
*Reza Farahani,Zoha Azimi,Mario Colosi,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LMEdge是一个面向异构边缘设备的QoS感知LLM编排服务，通过BILP优化和轻量级ML预测模型，在满足精度、网络和资源约束下最小化响应时间，相比基线方法在延迟、资源利用率和服务比率上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘基础设施上的LLM服务面临异构且资源受限的设备挑战，需要联合确定模型配置（系列、大小、量化级别）和执行放置，同时满足用户和系统级的QoS要求，现有方法缺乏有效的编排机制。

Method: 将问题建模为二元整数线性规划（BILP）优化问题，以在精度、网络和资源约束下最小化响应时间；使用五种轻量级ML模型预测查询的延迟、精度、资源使用和响应大小；设计轻量级启发式算法近似BILP解；收集包含59000+行的基准数据集用于模型训练。

Result: 在基于Kubernetes的边缘测试床上（57个实例，多种查询类别）进行评估，LMEdge相比两个基线方法在延迟、精度保持、资源利用率和服务比率方面均有提升。

Conclusion: LMEdge通过QoS感知的联合模型配置和执行放置决策，能够有效应对异构边缘设备上的LLM服务编排挑战，实现了更好的性能和资源效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services increasingly operate on edge infrastructure, enabling low-latency and privacy-preserving AI services. However, efficiently serving LLM requests across heterogeneous and resource-constrained edge devices require orchestration mechanisms that jointly determine model configuration (family, size, and quantization level) and execution placement while satisfying user- and system-level quality of service (QoS) requirements. This paper introduces LMEdge, a QoS-aware orchestration service that dynamically makes these decisions across heterogeneous edge devices. We formulate the problem as a binary integer linear programming (BILP) optimization that minimizes response time under accuracy, network, and resource constraints. To enable scalable online scheduling, we employ five lightweight machine learning (ML) models to predict query-specific latency, accuracy, resource usage, and response size for each model-size-quantization-device combination, and design a lightweight heuristic that approximates the BILP solution. We collect a comprehensive benchmarking dataset of over 59000 rows to train models and support reproducibility. Evaluation on a Kubernetes-based edge testbed with 57 instances and diverse query categories shows that LMEdge reduces latency, preserves accuracy, improves resource utilization, and increases serving ratio compared to two baselines.

</details>


### [264] [Talaria: Session-Aware Serverless Serving of Hundred-Billion-Parameter LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17181)
*Utopia Meng,Unicornt Zhao,Derek Li,Goalen Gao,Frank Du*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Talaria 是一个会话感知的无服务器多模型 LLM 服务系统，通过联合放置和准入决策来保持会话连续性，在单 TP=8 服务器上将 30 个 SWE-Bench 模型会话的 p50 会话完成时间从 1000 秒降至 189 秒（5.3 倍加速），p95 从 2296 秒降至 867 秒（2.6 倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器多模型 LLM 系统独立调度每个请求，但工具调用代理会打破这种抽象：会话在短工具间隙中重复调用 LLM、携带长可复用 KV 前缀，且以会话完成时间（SCT）为评判标准。纯负载路由可能将延续请求与模型和 KV 状态分离，基于轮次的模型复用可能延迟正确放置的延续请求。这些问题对千亿参数模型尤其严重：模型权重限制驻留，长上下文 KV 重建或迁移成本极高。

Method: Talaria 的核心创新是将会话连续性作为联合放置和准入决策。其路由器根据模型驻留、KV 局部性和实例压力对放置进行排序，同时采用软预留机制将可能返回的会话纳入最后服务实例的准入预算。会话预填充（SP）在活跃模型时隙关闭前准入符合条件的延续请求。实例本地子系统保持 HBM 地址稳定、保存可主机恢复的 KV，并在模型切换时分阶段加载权重。

Result: 在单 TP=8 服务器上，对 30 个 SWE-Bench 模型会话（960 次调用）在三个超过 100B 参数的模型上进行重放。相较于禁用了会话预填充、主机 KV 恢复和 D2D 分阶段加载的轮次调度器，Talaria 将 p50 SCT 从 1000 秒降至 189 秒（5.3 倍加速），p95 SCT 从 2296 秒降至 867 秒（2.6 倍加速）。

Conclusion: 会话感知的联合放置和准入决策能显著改善工具调用代理场景下无服务器多模型 LLM 系统的会话完成时间，特别是对于千亿参数大模型。

Abstract: Serverless multi-model LLM systems multiplex popularity-skewed model catalogs over shared GPU pools, yet typically schedule each request independently. Tool-using agents break this abstraction: a session repeatedly calls an LLM across short tool gaps, carries a long reusable KV prefix, and is judged by session completion time (SCT). Load-only routing can separate a continuation from both its model and KV state, while round-based model multiplexing can delay even a correctly placed continuation until the target model's next slot. Both failures are especially costly for hundred-billion-parameter models: their weights constrain residency, while long-context KV is expensive to reconstruct or move.
  We present Talaria, a session-aware serverless multi-model serving system that makes session continuity a joint placement-and-admission decision. Its router ranks placements by model residency, KV locality, and instance pressure, while soft reservations account for likely returns in the last serving instance's admission budget. Session-prefill (SP) admits budget-eligible continuations before the active model slot closes. An instance-local substrate keeps HBM addresses stable, preserves host-restorable KV, and stages weights across model switches.
  On a single TP=8 server, we replay 30 SWE-Bench model-sessions (960 calls) over three models, each with more than 100B total parameters. Against an otherwise identical round scheduler with SP, host-KV restoration, and D2D staging disabled, Talaria cuts p50 SCT from 1000 s to 189 s and p95 from 2296 s to 867 s, speedups of 5.3x and 2.6x.

</details>


### [265] [Taurus: Accelerating Out-of-Core Graph Neural Network Inference on Billion-Scale Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17374)
*Pranjal Naman,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Taurus是一个单机GNN推理系统，通过源中心广播、顺序SSD扫描、流水线GPU-CPU-SSD架构等技术，在内存无法容纳的十亿级图上实现高效推理，相比现有方法加速7-140倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿级图的GNN推理面临特征和嵌入的大内存占用以及高磁盘I/O开销问题。现有分布式系统通信时间长、基础设施成本高，而基于磁盘的GNN系统主要针对训练设计，在全图推理时存在大量无效读取。

Method: 将逐层推理重新表述为源中心广播，通过顺序SSD扫描实现；采用流水线GPU-CPU-SSD层次架构；使用拓扑感知重排序、待处理消息驱逐、GPU驻留高精度顶点存储；利用非缓冲顺序读取和GPU支持的写入减少页缓存污染和主机内存压力。

Result: 在最多包含2.69亿顶点、40亿边、514GiB特征的外存图上，Taurus比最强的逐层基线DGI快7-25倍，比逐顶点基线快40-140倍。

Conclusion: Taurus证明了单机系统通过精心设计的存储层次和数据流优化，可以在外存图上实现高效的GNN推理，避免了分布式系统的高通信成本和传统磁盘系统的无效读取问题。

Abstract: Graph Neural Network (GNN) inference on billion-scale graphs is challenging due to the large memory footprint of features and embeddings and high disk I/O costs in out-of-core settings. Existing distributed GNN systems incur high communication times and infrastructure costs, while disk-based GNN systems are primarily tailored to training and experience massive wasted reads during inference on the entire graph. We present Taurus, a single-machine system for GNN inference on graphs that do not fit in RAM, supporting both \textit{exact} full-graph inference and fanout-sampled inference. To avoid random and repeated feature gathers, Taurus reformulates layer-wise inference as source-centric broadcasts over sequential SSD scans, backed by a pipelined GPU-CPU-SSD hierarchy, topology-aware reordering, pending-message eviction, and a GPU-resident store for high-degree vertices. It further uses non-buffered sequential reads and GPU-backed writes to reduce page-cache pollution, host-memory pressure, and write overheads. On out-of-core graphs with up to $269M$ vertices, $4B$ edges, and $514$ GiB of features, Taurus outperforms the strongest layer-wise baseline, DGI, by $7$-$25\times$, and vertex-wise baselines by $40$-$140\times$.

</details>


### [266] [A Training-Memory Regression in MLA Sequence Parallelism: Why Megatron-Core Forbids Absorption, and LAGA -- a Communication-Efficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17644)
*Changzheng Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MLA在Megatron-Core中有显式和吸收式两种实现，吸收式虽减少通信但被限制仅用于推理。原因是其在训练中会膨胀激活内存20-34%（DeepSeek-V3规模下可达9.2-19.2 GB）。本文提出LAGA方法，保留吸收式的通信优势，同时避免内存陷阱，在8x Ascend 910B上通信降低1.98x，内存与显式持平，吞吐提升1.04-1.24x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示MLA吸收式实现被限制在推理阶段使用的根本原因（训练中激活内存膨胀），并解决低通信MLA训练路径缺失的问题。

Method: LAGA（Latent All-Gather Attention）：保留吸收式的latent-gather通信方式，但拒绝吸收式重计算，改为从收集的latent中局部重建每头K/V。

Result: 吸收式在训练中激活内存膨胀20-34%，DeepSeek-V3规模下达9.2 GB（eager kernel）至19.2 GB（fused kernel）。LAGA在8x Ascend 910B上通信降低1.98x，内存与显式差异<0.5%，SP=1时比特一致，SP=2-8时误差<1e-3，注意力块吞吐提升1.04-1.06x（单节点）和1.07-1.24x（跨节点）。

Conclusion: 吸收式训练内存膨胀是其被限制使用的根本原因；LAGA提供了首个低通信MLA训练方案，在保持通信优势的同时消除内存代价，在所有序列长度下跨节点场景均领先。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) ships two implementations in Megatron-Core: an explicit form used for training and an absorbed form -- which slashes collective communication by gathering only the compressed latent -- that is fully implemented but hard-asserted out of training (the forward opens with "assert not (self.training and self.cache_mla_latents)"), allowed only in inference decode. The library documents no reason. We show the restriction is well-founded and quantify why: ported to training, the absorbed form is a memory trap -- its intermediates live in n_h x d_kv dimensions per token, larger than the per-head K/V they replace -- inflating activation memory by 20-34%, up to 9.2 GB at DeepSeek-V3 scale (n_h=128, seq=16384, SP=8, eager kernel; the gap widens to 19.2 GB under a fused kernel), enough to change device-fit. This measurement, validated on two axes (linear in seq and n_h) and cross-verified on NVIDIA A100, explains the otherwise-undocumented restriction and leaves practitioners with no low-communication MLA training path. We then provide one. LAGA (Latent All-Gather Attention) keeps the absorbed form's latent-gather communication but rejects the absorb reformulation, instead reconstructing per-head K/V locally from the gathered latent. On 8x Ascend 910B at real DeepSeek-V3 dimensions, LAGA cuts collective communication 1.98x, matches explicit memory within 0.5%, is bit-identical to explicit at SP=1 and equivalent to within 1e-3 at SP=2-8, and under a fused attention kernel improves attention-block throughput 1.04-1.06x single-node and 1.07-1.24x cross-node -- leading at all sequence lengths in the cross-node regime MLA is deployed for.

</details>


### [267] [Byzantine Fault-Tolerant Post-Quantum Distributed Quorum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17700)
*Quentin Kniep,Jakub Sliwinski,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式法定签名(DQS)原语，通过改变通信协议而非密码学原语，实现了常量子签名，解决了后量子时代拜占庭容错系统中法定签名的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子安全时代中，现有的法定签名(门限签名、聚合签名、多重签名)均无法实现常量子签名，其签名大小随签名者数量增长且聚合速度慢，成为将拜占庭容错系统迁移至后量子安全的最大障碍。

Method: 提出分布式法定签名(DQS)原语，仅基于普通数字签名和Bracha风格批准广播构建。将证书从网络消息转变为局部事件，引入两种事件类型：弱证书(捕获安全性)和强证书(捕获活跃性)。

Result: DQS中每条消息均为常量大小（无论节点数量多少，适配单个数据报），总通信量为二次方，安全假设不变。在大规模分布式系统中，后量子DQS的开销与经典的预量子BLS方案相当。

Conclusion: 通过改变协议通信方式而非解决底层密码学难题，DQS在原语层面绕过了后量子法定签名的开放性问题，为后量子拜占庭容错系统提供了实用的常量子签名替代方案。

Abstract: Threshold, aggregate, and multi-signatures -- which we collectively call quorum signatures -- certify that a quorum of nodes endorsed a statement, with a certificate as small as a single signature. No constant-size post-quantum quorum signature is known: all candidates grow with the number of signers and are slow to aggregate, making quorum signatures the hardest obstacle to migrating byzantine fault-tolerant systems to post-quantum security.
  In this paper, we sidestep this open cryptographic problem by changing how the protocol communicates. We introduce a primitive we call Distributed Quorum Signature (DQS), built solely from ordinary digital signatures and a Bracha-style approval broadcast. DQS turns certificates from network messages into local events. Two event types divide the roles certificates play: weak certificates capture safety, strong certificates capture liveness. In DQS every message is constant size, fitting a single datagram regardless of the number of nodes. The total communication is quadratic, and no security assumptions change. In a large distributed system, the overhead of post-quantum DQS is competitive with the canonical pre-quantum BLS scheme.

</details>


### [268] [AutoEncoder-Compressed Parallel Split Learning for Pre-trained Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17913)
*Bas Meuwissen,Vasileios Tsouvalas,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对分布式微调中通信开销大的问题，提出AE-PSL框架，在分割层引入轻量级自编码器压缩中间激活和梯度，并通过两阶段对齐机制确保与预训练基础模型兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行拆分学习（PSL）中客户端与服务器间需频繁交换中间激活和梯度，通信开销大；现有通信压缩方法多为任务无关启发式方法，而可学习的压缩器直接插入预训练模型会导致特征分布错配，降低分布式微调性能。

Method: 提出AE-PSL框架，在拆分层部署轻量级自编码器（AE）压缩中间激活和梯度；设计两阶段对齐机制，首先将AE适配到预训练模型的特征流形，再适配到客户端特定的特征分布，确保AE压缩与预训练基础模型的兼容性。

Result: 在保持分布式微调性能的前提下，显著降低了通信开销。

Conclusion: AE-PSL有效解决了可学习压缩器与预训练模型间的特征分布错配问题，为资源受限边缘设备上的基础模型分布式微调提供了一种通信高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed Fine-Tuning (DFT) of large-scale Foundation Models (FMs) on resource-constrained edge devices is limited by local compute constraints and communication overhead. Parallel Split Learning (PSL) reduces client-side computation by keeping few model layers on each client and offloading the remaining computation to the server; however, clients must exchange intermediate activations and gradients with the server at every training step. Existing SL communication-compression methods mainly rely on task-agnostic heuristics, such as sparsification and quantization. While learnable SL compressors can better adapt to intermediate representations, they require co-training with the target model. Therefore, directly inserting them into off-the-shelf FMs introduces feature-distribution misalignment and degrades DFT performance. To address this, we propose AE-PSL, a communication-efficient PSL framework that compresses intermediate activations and gradients using a lightweight AutoEncoder (AE) placed at the split layer. To ensure compatibility of AE compression with pre-trained FMs, AE-PSL introduces a novel two-stage alignment mechanism, which adapts the AE to the pre-trained model's feature manifold and client-specific feature distributions before DFT.

</details>


### [269] [ExpertPlex: A High-Goodput Disaggregated Serving System for MoE LLMs with Adaptive Persistent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18002)
*Bingyang Wu,Chao Jin,Zili Zhang,Xinming Wei,Yinmin Zhong,Ruidong Zhu,Chengxu Yang,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ExpertPlex提出了一种新的MoE服务架构，将大规模MoE专家在prefill和decode阶段之间共享，同时解耦轻量级注意力模块，通过自适应持久化内核、注意力发起的MoE通信和tile-to-cluster模型优化，在保持高效隔离执行的同时消除冗余权重、减少通信开销并避免网络干扰，相比实例级prefill-decode解耦和共存方案分别实现了最高2.01×和1.66×的goodput提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型服务方案中，实例级prefill-decode解耦会导致每个阶段独立部署完整模型副本，随着MoE权重增长，资源分配粒度变粗，配置比率难以匹配实际需求，造成资源浪费或过载；而prefill-decode共存方案虽避免冗余，但Green Context等方案按GPU分区并固定阶段资源，无法跟踪操作间资源变化和逐层路由专家负载变化，导致队头阻塞或资源闲置，同时分区使局部资源减少、并行度和通信开销增加，且共享网络上prefill与decode流量相互干扰。

Method: ExpertPlex的核心思路是共享大规模MoE专家、解耦轻量级注意力模块。具体包含三个关键技术：(1) 自适应持久化内核（adaptive persistent kernels），以tile粒度调度动态专家计算，实现高效隔离执行；(2) 注意力发起的MoE通信（attention-initiated MoE communication），避免网络干扰并实现跨阶段通信-计算重叠；(3) tile-to-cluster模型，用于优化上述机制以获得最大goodput。

Result: 在服务MiniMax-M2.7和GLM-5.1-FP8两个大规模MoE模型的实验中，ExpertPlex相比实例级prefill-decode解耦方案，goodput最高提升2.01倍；相比prefill-decode共存方案，goodput最高提升1.66倍。同时，专家共享消除了超过95%的重复模型权重。

Conclusion: ExpertPlex通过共享专家和解耦注意力的架构设计，结合自适应的调度与通信优化，有效解决了大规模MoE模型服务中prefill和decode阶段的资源分配与干扰问题，显著提升了系统goodput。

Abstract: LLMs scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parameters for superior intelligence, but massive weights and dynamic computation impede efficient serving. Existing instance-level prefill-decode disaggregation isolates the phases on separate full-model replicas. As MoE weights grow, each instance may span tens to hundreds of GPUs, making resource allocation increasingly coarse. Configured prefill-to-decode ratios thus often mismatch demand, overprovisioning one phase while overloading the other. Prefill-decode colocation avoids this duplication, but existing Green Context solutions partition each GPU by phase and fix phase resources during a kernel. They cannot track resource changes across operations or layerwise variation in routed expert load, causing head-of-line blocking or idle reserved resources.
